Original Abstract: Accurate polyp segmentation in colonoscopy is essential for early colorectal cancer detection, yet real-world clinical environments pose persistent challenges such as motion blur, specular reflections, and illumination instability. Most existing methods are optimized on clean benchmark images and suffer noticeable performance degradation when deployed in authentic surgical scenarios. We propose DepthPolyp, a lightweight and robust segmentation framework based on pseudo-depth-guided multi-task learning and efficient feature modulation. The architecture combines hierarchical Ghost factorization for compact feature generation, Interleaved Shuffle Fusion for low-cost cross-scale interaction, and Dynamic Group Gating for adaptive group-wise feature weighting. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DepthPolyp achieves strong cross-dataset generalization when trained on degraded data and evaluated on both clean and noisy target domains, consistently outperforming lightweight baselines and remaining competitive with substantially larger models. In real surgical video evaluation on PolypGen, DepthPolyp achieves better segmentation performance than models up to $20\times$ larger while preserving real-time inference speed. With only 3.57M parameters and 0.86 GMACs, the proposed method runs at over 180 FPS on mobile devices, making it well suited for real-time deployment in resource-constrained clinical environments. Code and pretrained weights are available at: https://github.com/ReaganWu/DepthPolyp/

中文摘要翻译: 结肠镜检查中的准确息肉分割对于早期结直肠癌检测至关重要，然而真实临床环境存在运动模糊、镜面反射和光照不稳定等持续挑战。现有方法大多在干净的基准图像上优化，在真实手术场景中部署时会出现明显的性能下降。我们提出DepthPolyp，一种基于伪深度引导多任务学习和高效特征调制的轻量级鲁棒分割框架。该架构结合了分层Ghost分解以实现紧凑特征生成、交错洗牌融合以实现低成本的跨尺度交互，以及动态分组门控以实现自适应分组特征加权。大量实验表明，DepthPolyp在退化数据上训练并在干净和噪声目标域评估时，表现出强大的跨数据集泛化能力，持续优于轻量级基线，并与规模大得多的模型保持竞争力。在PolypGen真实手术视频评估中，DepthPolyp以优于20倍大的模型的分割性能，同时保持实时推理速度。该方法的参数量仅为3.57M，计算量为0.86 GMACs，在移动设备上运行速度超过180 FPS，非常适合资源受限临床环境中的实时部署。代码和预训练权重可在https://github.com/ReaganWu/DepthPolyp/获取。

</details>


### [32] [Multi-hop Relational Contrastive Learning: Extending Spatial Contrastive Pre-training Beyond Pairwise Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16456)
*Sheikh Tanvir Ahmed,Md. Tanvir Raihan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多跳关系对比学习（MRCL）框架，通过在场景图上追踪k跳路径来捕捉超越直接物体对的空间依赖关系，实验表明该方法在场景图检索、空间关系识别和基于图的问答等任务上优于仅使用成对关系的方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有对比预训练方法大多只建模物体间的成对关系，忽略了更丰富的组合式和多跳交互信息，限制了模型对场景结构的深层理解能力。

Method: MRCL框架将空间对比学习扩展到图结构的场景表示上，通过检测到的物体构建场景图并追踪k跳路径来捕获隐式空间依赖；同时定义了一个多级对比目标，涵盖节点、边和多跳路径三个层次，使嵌入在保持物体语义稳定性的同时响应空间布局变化。

Result: 在GQA子集上，MRCL在基于内容的图检索任务中NDCG@5达到0.748，并在空间关系识别和基于图的问答等下游任务中持续带来性能提升。

Conclusion: 多跳关系监督比仅使用成对关系的方法提供了更丰富的结构指导，能够产生更鲁棒、更具组合性和几何感知能力的视觉表征。

Original Abstract: Understanding how objects relate to each other in space is fundamental to scene understanding, yet most contrastive pre-training approaches only model pairwise relationships, leaving richer compositional and multi-hop interactions largely unexplored. We introduce Multi-Hop Relational Contrastive Learning (MRCL), a framework that extends spatial contrastive learning to graph-structured scene representations. By tracing k-hop paths through scene graphs built from detected objects, MRCL captures implicit spatial dependencies that go well beyond what direct object pairs can express. We define a multi-level contrastive objective spanning nodes, edges, and multi-hop paths, encouraging embeddings that remain stable across object semantics while staying responsive to spatial layout. On a GQA subset, MRCL produces spatially-aware representations that improve content-based graph retrieval (NDCG@5 = 0.748) and consistently benefit downstream tasks, including spatial relationship recognition and graph-based question answering. Together, these results suggest that multi-hop relational supervision offers substantially richer structural guidance than pairwise-only methods, leading to visual representations that are more robust, compositional, and geometry-aware.

中文摘要翻译: 理解物体在空间中的相互关系是场景理解的基础，然而大多数对比预训练方法仅建模成对关系，忽略了更丰富的组合式和多跳交互。我们提出了多跳关系对比学习（MRCL），一个将空间对比学习扩展到图结构场景表示的框架。通过在检测到的物体构建的场景图上追踪k跳路径，MRCL能够捕捉远超直接物体对所能表达的隐式空间依赖。我们定义了一个涵盖节点、边和多跳路径的多级对比目标，使嵌入在保持物体语义稳定性的同时，对空间布局变化保持响应。在GQA子集上，MRCL产生了具有空间感知能力的表征，改进了基于内容的图检索（NDCG@5 = 0.748），并持续有益于空间关系识别和基于图的问答等下游任务。这些结果表明，多跳关系监督比仅用成对关系的方法提供了更丰富的结构引导，从而产生更鲁棒、更具组合性和几何感知能力的视觉表征。

</details>


### [33] [Conservative AI for Safety-Sensitive Medical Image Restoration: Residual-Bounded CT-CTA Enhancement for Intracranial Aneurysm-Relevant Signal Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16458)
*Weijun Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种残差有界的2.5D医学图像修复框架，通过在CT/CTA图像中限制编辑幅度和空间范围，在改善图像质量的同时避免对临床重要区域（如动脉瘤相关血管）的不可控修改，实验表明该方法在边界敏感血管成像中可行。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的图像修复模型在安全敏感型医疗场景中可能对临床重要区域（如颅内小血管和动脉瘤相关线索）进行不可控的修改，尤其是在高对比度解剖边界附近，因此需要一种保守AI方法，在提升图像质量的同时严格限制修改的幅度和空间范围。

Method: 提出残差有界的2.5D修复框架：在合成退化的CT/CTA输入上训练，模型通过一个编辑控制图（edit-control map）对原始中心切片添加学习到的残差，该控制图限制修改的幅度和空间范围。

Result: 在50个分布外CT-CTA病例上，有界模型平均目标增益0.0635，平均PSNR 37.51 dB，医源性编辑率4.0%；1000次蒙特卡洛运行中85.4%为净正向，无稳定负向案例；在外部位低剂量CT上方向有益且编辑足迹远小于基线；有意义编辑集中在脑部和颅骨区域，无关解剖区域变化可忽略。

Conclusion: 残差有界修复在边界敏感的血管成像中是可行的，但该研究仅提供初步计算证据，未建立临床诊断性能，需专家评审和前瞻性验证后方可用于临床。

Original Abstract: Image restoration models are increasingly applied to degraded medical scans, but in safety-sensitive settings they must improve image quality without uncontrolled modification of clinically important regions. This is especially relevant for intracranial CT and CT angiography (CTA), where small vessels and aneurysm-relevant cues lie near high-contrast anatomical boundaries. We frame medical image restoration as a conservative AI problem and present a residual-bounded 2.5D restoration framework trained on synthetically degraded CT/CTA inputs. The model adds a learned residual to the original center slice through an edit-control map that limits the magnitude and spatial extent of modification. We evaluate the framework using an aneurysm-relevant image-recovery matrix, paired comparison against a Gaussian baseline, Monte Carlo stability testing, anatomical localization of meaningful edits, and external evaluation on low-dose CT. On 50 out-of-distribution CT-CTA cases, the bounded model achieved a mean target gain of 0.0635, a mean PSNR of 37.51 dB, and an iatrogenic-edit rate of 4.0%. Across 1,000 Monte Carlo runs, it remained net positive in 85.4% of runs with no stably negative cases. On external low-dose CT, the model was directionally beneficial and produced a substantially smaller modification footprint than the baseline. Meaningful edits concentrated in brain and skull regions while unrelated anatomy showed negligible change. These findings provide preliminary computational evidence that residual-bounded restoration is feasible in boundary-sensitive vascular imaging, but they do not establish clinical diagnostic performance and require expert review and prospective validation before clinical use.

中文摘要翻译: 图像修复模型越来越多地应用于退化的医学扫描图像，但在安全敏感场景中，它们必须在改善图像质量的同时，避免对临床重要区域进行不可控的修改。这一点对于颅内CT和CT血管造影（CTA）尤为重要，因为小血管和动脉瘤相关线索位于高对比度解剖边界附近。我们将医学图像修复作为一个保守AI问题来构建，并提出了一个残差有界的2.5D修复框架，该框架在合成退化的CT/CTA输入上进行训练。模型通过一个编辑控制图向原始中心切片添加学习到的残差，该控制图限制了修改的幅度和空间范围。我们使用动脉瘤相关图像恢复矩阵、与高斯基线的配对比较、蒙特卡洛稳定性测试、有意义编辑的解剖定位以及外部位低剂量CT评估来对该框架进行评价。在50个分布外CT-CTA病例上，有界模型实现了平均目标增益0.0635、平均PSNR 37.51 dB和医源性编辑率4.0%。在1000次蒙特卡洛运行中，85.4%的运行保持净正向，且无稳定负向案例。在外部位低剂量CT上，该模型具有方向性益处，且产生的编辑足迹远小于基线。有意义的编辑集中在脑部和颅骨区域，而不相关的解剖区域变化可忽略。这些发现提供了初步的计算证据，表明残差有界修复在边界敏感的血管成像中是可行的，但该研究未建立临床诊断性能，在临床应用前需要专家评审和前瞻性验证。

</details>


### [34] [REC-RL: Referring expression counting via Gaussian and range-based reward optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16460)
*Hui Liu,Yunlai Teng,Kunlong Bai,Pengfei Qi,Haotian Yan,Liang Li,Junlan Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出REC-RL，一个基于强化学习的指代表达式计数框架，通过think-range-answer范式优化视觉推理过程，结合两组轻量级奖励（精度奖励+格式奖励），无需额外标注即可提升推理质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有REC方法大多依赖基于规则的强化学习，奖励仅关注最终精度，忽略了中间推理过程的质量，导致模型缺乏可解释的视觉推理能力。

Method: 提出REC-RL框架，采用think-range-answer三阶段范式，使用Group Relative Policy Optimization算法，设计两种轻量级奖励：1）精度奖励（结合基于区间的区间监督与基于高斯分布的精度引导）；2）格式奖励（强制执行结构化输出）。通过将中间聚焦预测建模为内部决策过程，避免额外标注。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上取得一致性改进，并展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: REC-RL通过显式优化推理过程而非仅优化最终结果，有效提升了指代表达式计数性能，且与人类感知更好地对齐，具有良好的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Referring expression counting (REC) is an intention-driven task that requires context-aware visual reasoning. While recent vision-language models incorporate language for visual understanding, most existing REC methods rely on rulebased reinforcement learning with rewards focused primarily on final accuracy, overlooking the quality of intermediate reasoning. We propose REC-RL, a reinforcement learning framework that introduces a think-range-answer paradigm to explicitly optimize the visual reasoning process. RECRL employs Group Relative Policy Optimization and two lightweight rewards: an accuracy reward that combines range-based interval supervision with Gaussian-based precision guidance, and a format reward that enforces structured outputs. By modeling intermediate focus prediction as internal decision-making, REC-RL avoids additional annotations and better aligns with human perception. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines and robust generalization across benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 指代表达式计数（REC）是一项意图驱动的任务，需要上下文感知的视觉推理。虽然最近的视觉-语言模型将语言融入视觉理解，但大多数现有的REC方法依赖基于规则的强化学习，其奖励主要关注最终精度，忽视了中间推理过程的质量。我们提出REC-RL，一种强化学习框架，引入了"思考-范围-回答"（think-range-answer）范式来显式优化视觉推理过程。REC-RL采用组相对策略优化（Group Relative Policy Optimization）和两种轻量级奖励：一种精度奖励，结合了基于范围的区间监督与基于高斯的精度引导；以及一种格式奖励，用于强制执行结构化输出。通过将中间聚焦预测建模为内部决策，REC-RL避免了额外标注，并更好地与人类感知对齐。大量实验表明，该方法在强基线基础上实现了一致性改进，并在多个基准上展现出鲁棒的泛化能力。

</details>


### [35] [SeamCam: Quantifying Seamless Camouflage via Multi-Cue Visual Detectability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16515)
*Amin Karimi Monsefi,Abolfazl Meyarian,Mridul Khurana,Shuheng Wang,Pouyan Navard,Cheng Zhang,Anuj Karpatne,Wei-Lun Chao,Rajiv Ramnath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SeamCam，一种基于视觉定位的伪装量化指标，通过条件检测和最大可恢复定位信号评估动物的不可检测性，并在人类感知实验中优于现有方法；同时利用该指标通过DPO微调扩散模型生成伪装图像，并构建了无遮挡伪影的CamFG-1.5k数据集。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究缺乏标准化的定量指标来评估动物的伪装效果，即动物与背景无缝融合的程度。

Method: SeamCam将伪装评估建模为视觉定位问题：给定图像和目标物种，生成类别条件检测候选框，提取分割掩码，找出与真实掩码交并比最高的子集联合；SeamCam分数=1−最大可恢复定位信号。同时利用SeamCam作为偏好信号，通过DPO微调扩散修复模型生成伪装图像。

Result: 在94名参与者的2390组人类两选一强迫选择实验中，SeamCam与人类伪装难度判断的一致率达78.82%，比现有最优方法高出约25%。

Conclusion: SeamCam是一种有效的伪装量化指标，可作为伪装生成模型的训练信号，推动伪装评估与生成的标准化研究。

Original Abstract: Animals are described as effectively camouflaged when they blend seamlessly with their surrounding, yet no standardized quantitative measure of this seamlessness exists. We address this gap by framing camouflage evaluation as a visual localization problem: a well-camouflaged animal is one that remains difficult to detect even when its category is known. We introduce SeamCam (Seamless Camouflage), a metric that quantifies how detectable an animal is from the available visual evidence. Given an image and a target species, SeamCam generates category-conditioned detection proposals, extracts segmentation masks, and identifies the subset whose collective union yields the highest IoU with the ground-truth mask. The SeamCam score is one minus this maximum recoverable localization signal, where a higher score indicates stronger camouflage (i.e., lower detectability). In a human two-alternative forced-choice study with 94 participants and 2,390 comparisons, SeamCam achieves 78.82% agreement with human camouflage difficulty judgments, outperforming state-of-the-art by about 25%. We then demonstrate SeamCam's utility as a preference signal for Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to fine-tune a diffusion-based inpainting model for camouflage generation. This offers an affordable training approach with an objective explicitly suited for camouflage generation, unlike typical diffusion models. To support rigorous benchmarking, we further introduce CamFG-1.5k, a curated dataset of 1,521 high-resolution images in which animals are fully visible prior to camouflage generation, enabling unbiased evaluation by controlling for occlusion artifacts present in existing datasets. https://7amin.github.io/SeamCam/

中文摘要翻译: 当动物与其周围环境无缝融合时，它们被描述为具有有效的伪装，但目前尚缺乏标准化的定量指标来衡量这种无缝性。我们通过将伪装评估框架化为一个视觉定位问题来填补这一空白：一个伪装良好的动物是指即使其类别已知也难以被检测到的动物。我们提出SeamCam（Seamless Camouflage），一种从可用视觉证据中量化动物可检测性的指标。给定一张图像和一个目标物种，SeamCam生成类别条件检测候选框，提取分割掩码，并找出其联合与真实掩码交并比最高的子集。SeamCam分数等于1减去该最大可恢复定位信号，分数越高表示伪装越强（即可检测性越低）。在一项包含94名参与者和2390组比较的人类两选一强迫选择实验中，SeamCam与人类伪装难度判断的一致率达到78.82%，比现有最优方法高出约25%。我们进一步展示了SeamCam作为直接偏好优化（DPO）的偏好信号，用于微调基于扩散的修复模型以生成伪装。这提供了一种经济实惠的训练方法，其目标函数明确适合伪装生成，不同于典型的扩散模型。为了支持严谨的基准测试，我们进一步引入了CamFG-1.5k数据集，该数据集包含1521张高分辨率图像，其中动物在伪装生成前完全可见，通过控制现有数据集中存在的遮挡伪影来实现无偏评估。

</details>


### [36] [SWoMo: Neuro-Symbolic World Model for Cataract Surgery Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16530)
*Ssharvien Kumar Sivakumar,Akwele Johnson,Anirudh Dhingra,Yannik Frisch,Ghazal Ghazaei,Anirban Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SWoMo是一个用于白内障手术模拟的神经符号世界模型，通过将符号模拟器（基于规则的运动和交互建模）与扩散模型（视觉真实感生成）解耦，实现了高视觉真实感、物理真实的交互以及超越训练分布的泛化能力。采用逆配对策略从真实手术视频重建模拟数据以训练视频扩散模型，实验证明其在交互几何泛化、下游阶段检测和无监督风格迁移等方面优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有手术模拟方法在临床应用中未能同时满足视觉真实感、物理真实的交互以及模拟超出训练分布场景的能力这三个关键标准。

Method: 提出神经符号世界模型SWoMo：符号组件（基于规则的模拟器+场景图表示）负责运动动力学和工具-组织交互建模；扩散模型负责生成包含纹理和组织变形的真实视觉外观。通过逆配对策略从真实手术视频重建模拟数据，获得成对的模拟-真实视频对，用于训练视频扩散模型实现从模拟到真实的翻译。解耦运动生成与视觉真实感，分别处理两个子问题。

Result: 定性和定量结果均优于先前工作。满足关键标准：泛化到未见过的交互几何结构、下游阶段检测任务性能提升、无监督视频风格迁移能力。

Conclusion: SWoMo通过神经符号方法有效解耦了运动生成与视觉渲染，在满足临床适用性的关键标准上取得了显著进展，代码和数据已开源。

Original Abstract: Realistic surgical simulation plays a crucial role in training novice surgeons and in the development of autonomous agents. World models can scale such simulation environments to realistic and diverse procedures by predicting future patient states conditioned on current observations and surgical actions. However, current state-of-the-art approaches often fail to satisfy key criteria required for clinical applicability, including visual realism, physically grounded interactions, and the ability to simulate scenarios beyond the training distribution. Hence, we introduce SWoMo, a neuro-symbolic world model for cataract surgery simulation that decouples motion generation from visual realism. The symbolic component, consisting of a rule-based simulator and scene graph representations, models motion dynamics and tool-tissue interactions, while a diffusion model produces realistic visual appearance, including textures and tissue deformations. We propose an inverse pairing strategy that reconstructs real surgical videos in the simulator to obtain paired simulated and real videos, which are then used to train our video diffusion model for the reverse objective of sim-to-real translation. Our experiments show both qualitative and quantitative improvements over prior work. We demonstrate that our simulator further satisfies the key criteria, including generalisation to unseen interaction geometries, improvements in downstream phase detection, and unsupervised video style transfer. The code, data, and model weights are available at: https://ssharvienkumar.github.io/SWoMo/

中文摘要翻译: 逼真的手术模拟在培训新手外科医生和开发自主智能体方面起着至关重要的作用。世界模型可以通过基于当前观测和手术动作预测未来患者状态，将此类模拟环境扩展到逼真且多样化的手术流程中。然而，当前最先进的方法往往无法满足临床适用性所需的关键标准，包括视觉真实感、物理真实的交互以及模拟超出训练分布场景的能力。为此，我们提出了SWoMo，一种用于白内障手术模拟的神经符号世界模型，它将运动生成与视觉真实感解耦。符号组件由基于规则的模拟器和场景图表示构成，负责建模运动动力学和工具-组织交互；扩散模型则生成真实的视觉外观，包括纹理和组织变形。我们提出了一种逆配对策略，在模拟器中重建真实手术视频，从而获得配对的模拟和真实视频，这些数据随后被用于训练我们的视频扩散模型，以实现从模拟到真实翻译的逆向目标。实验结果表明，我们在定性和定量两方面均优于先前工作。我们证明，该模拟器进一步满足了关键标准，包括对未见过的交互几何结构的泛化能力、下游阶段检测任务的性能提升以及无监督视频风格迁移。代码、数据和模型权重可在以下地址获取：https://ssharvienkumar.github.io/SWoMo/

</details>


### [37] [TriALS: Triphasic-Aided Liver Lesion Segmentation Benchmark in Non-Contrast CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16572)
*Marawan Elbatel,Mohamed Ghonim,Jiaji Mao,Zhuosheng Lin,Katharina Eckstein,Andrés Martínez Mora,Jonathan Deissler,Maximilian Rokuss,Constantin Ulrich,Zdravko Marinov,Wenhui Deng,Baoxun Li,Huijun Hu,Jun Shen,Mohanad Ghonim,Khadiga Omar Nassar,Mariam Elbakry,Menna Dyab,Amr Muhammad Abdo Salem,Nouran Elghitany,Noha Elghitany,Yi Qin,Xuanqi Huang,Haonan Wang,Shao-Woo Yen,Ahmed Elghamry Saba,Salma Ahmad,Xinyan Fang,Jiahao Zhang,Xiaodi Wang,Xinghua Ma,Gongning Luo,Jessica C. Delmoral,João Manuel R. S. Tavares,Ankan Deria,Adinath Dukre,Yutong Xie,Imran Razzak,Dongwook Kim,Matthew Choi,Hanxiao Zhang,Minghui Zhang,Xin You,Abdul Qayyum,Steven A. Niederer,Moona Mazher,Rachika E. Hamadache,Ricardo Montoya-del-Angel,Robert Martí,Xavier Lladó,Toufiq Musah,Livingstone Eli Ayivor,Enrique Almar-Munoz,Agnes Mayr,Kaouther Mouheb,Esther E. Bron,Stefan Klein,Ahmed Abouelhoda,Amira Adel,Susan Adil Ali,Rainer Stiefelhagen,Klaus H. Maier-Hein,Fabian Isensee,Aya Yassin,Xiaomeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了TriALS挑战赛，用于在无对比剂CT（NCCT）上自动分割肝脏病变，基于150例四期CT多中心数据集（600个体积）。最佳方法的静脉期Dice为0.754（接近人类水平），但在NCCT上降至0.57；外部验证中领先方法在NCCT上的Dice优于现成模型达28%。研究发现训练数据规模和预训练策略是算法性能的最强预测因子，而跨年度比较揭示了仅靠扩大预训练无法克服NCCT上的感知障碍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在非洲和亚洲等资源匮乏地区，对比剂经常不可用，因此NCCT上的肝脏病变自动分割具有重要的临床价值，但缺乏带标注的NCCT基准数据集严重限制了该领域的发展。

Method: 构建了包含150例四期CT采集（600个体积）的多中心数据集，来自埃及和中国医疗机构；在三个机构的70例病例上评估算法，包括独立外部队列；通过挑战赛形式比较多种分割方法。

Result: 最佳方法静脉期Dice为0.754（接近人类水平），NCCT上降至0.57；外部验证中NCCT Dice领先现成模型达28%；训练数据规模和预训练策略是算法性能的最强预测因子；跨年度比较显示仅靠扩大预训练无法克服NCCT感知障碍。

Conclusion: NCCT上的肝脏病变分割仍面临根本性挑战，仅靠扩大预训练规模无法克服其感知瓶颈，需要新的方法突破。

Original Abstract: Automated segmentation of liver lesions on non-contrast computed tomography (NCCT) is clinically important but fundamentally challenging, particularly in low-resource settings across Africa and Asia where contrast agents are frequently unavailable. Progress has been limited by the absence of annotated NCCT benchmarks. Here we describe the TriALS challenge for automated liver lesion segmentation under contrast-limited conditions, supported by a multi-centre dataset of 150 cases with four-phase CT acquisitions (600 volumes) from Egyptian and Chinese institutions. Algorithms were evaluated on 70 cases from three institutions, including an independent external cohort. The top-performing method achieved a mean venous-phase Dice of 0.754, consistent with human-level performance, yet dropped to 0.57 on NCCT. On external validation, the leading method outperformed off-the-shelf models by up to 28% in Dice on NCCT. Algorithm performance was most strongly predicted by training data scale and pre-training strategy. A cross-year comparison exposed a persistent perceptual barrier on NCCT that scaling pre-training alone cannot overcome. Data, annotations, and code are available at https://github.com/xmed-lab/TriALS.

中文摘要翻译: 在非增强计算机断层扫描（NCCT）上自动分割肝脏病变具有重要的临床意义，但也面临着根本性的挑战，尤其是在非洲和亚洲等对比剂经常不可用的资源匮乏地区。由于缺乏带标注的NCCT基准数据集，该领域的进展一直受限。本文描述了针对对比剂受限条件下的自动肝脏病变分割的TriALS挑战赛，该挑战赛依托一个包含150例四期CT采集（600个体积）的多中心数据集，数据来自埃及和中国的医疗机构。算法在来自三个机构的70例病例上进行了评估，其中包括一个独立的外部队列。性能最佳的方法实现了平均静脉期Dice为0.754，与人类水平相当，但在NCCT上降至0.57。在外部验证中，领先方法在NCCT上的Dice比现成模型高出多达28%。算法性能的最强预测因子是训练数据规模和预训练策略。跨年度比较揭示了NCCT上存在一个仅靠扩大预训练规模无法克服的持续感知障碍。数据、标注和代码均可在https://github.com/xmed-lab/TriALS获取。

</details>


### [38] [Attend Locally, Remember Linearly: Linear Attention as Cross-Frame Memory for Autoregressive Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16579)
*Kunyang Li,Mubarak Shah,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ARL2，一种混合注意力模块，将自回归视频扩散模型中的二次复杂度跨帧注意力替换为固定大小的循环状态，实现线性时间和常量内存扩展，同时提升时序一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自回归视频扩散依赖softmax自注意力导致序列长度二次复杂度和KV缓存线性内存增长，现有方法（稀疏注意力、KV缓存压缩）虽降低单步成本但仍无法解决线性内存增长和流式上下文管理问题。

Method: 提出ARL2混合注意力模块，分解自注意力为两个分支：(1) 帧内softmax分支，捕捉空间细节和局部依赖；(2) 帧间门控循环线性分支，维护固定大小状态用于流式上下文。循环状态仅在去噪通过后更新以避免噪声干扰，所有token共享更新前的相同状态以避免帧内信息不对称。通过两阶段训练将预训练AR视频扩散模型转换为混合线性注意力架构。

Result: 用75%的层替换为混合线性注意力，实现2.26倍实际加速和54%内存减少，同时保持可比质量并提升时序一致性。

Conclusion: ARL2是首个将预训练AR视频扩散模型转换为混合线性注意力架构的工作，有效解决了自回归视频生成的可扩展性瓶颈。

Original Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video diffusion is a powerful paradigm for streaming and interactive video generation. However, its reliance on softmax self-attention leads to quadratic compute complexity in sequence length and memory usage due to key-value caching, which limits its scalability to long video horizons. Existing remedies (e.g., sparse attention and KV-cache compression) reduce per-step cost but still rely on a linearly growing cache or irreversibly discard past context, and thus fail to address linear memory growth and streaming context management. To address this scalability bottleneck, we propose ARL2 (Attend Locally, Remember Linearly), a hybrid attention module that replaces quadratic cross-frame attention with a fixed-size recurrent state. We decompose self-attention into two branches: an intra-frame softmax branch for spatial detail and local dependencies, and an inter-frame gated recurrent linear branch that maintains a fixed-size state for streaming context. Our key insight is that softmax attention captures fine-grained local interactions, while a recurrent state provides controllable long-range memory. This design achieves linear-time scaling with constant memory while improving temporal consistency over the full-softmax model. To prevent noisy intermediate states from corrupting memory, we update the recurrent state only after the denoised pass. To avoid within-frame information asymmetry, all tokens share the same pre-update state rather than sequential updates. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to convert a pretrained AR video diffusion model into a hybrid linear attention architecture, through an efficient two-stage training scheme for AR video. With 75% of layers replaced by hybrid linear attention, the model achieves up to 2.26 wall-clock speedup and 54% memory reduction, while maintaining comparable quality with improving temporal consistency.

中文摘要翻译: 自回归（AR）视频扩散是流式与交互式视频生成的一种强大范式。然而，其对softmax自注意力的依赖导致序列长度上的二次计算复杂度和由于键值缓存带来的内存二次增长，这限制了其向长视频序列的扩展。现有的补救措施（如稀疏注意力和KV缓存压缩）降低了单步成本，但仍依赖于线性增长的缓存或不可逆地丢弃过去上下文，因此无法解决线性内存增长和流式上下文管理问题。为了解决这一可扩展性瓶颈，我们提出了ARL2（局部注意、线性记忆），一种混合注意力模块，将二次复杂度的跨帧注意力替换为固定大小的循环状态。我们将自注意力分解为两个分支：一个帧内softmax分支用于空间细节和局部依赖，一个帧间门控循环线性分支用于维护固定大小的流式上下文状态。我们的关键洞察是，softmax注意力捕捉细粒度的局部交互，而循环状态提供可控的长程记忆。该设计实现了线性时间扩展和常量内存，同时相比全softmax模型提升了时序一致性。为防止噪声中间状态污染记忆，我们仅在去噪通过后更新循环状态。为避免帧内信息不对称，所有token共享更新前的相同状态而非顺序更新。据我们所知，这是首个将预训练AR视频扩散模型转换为混合线性注意力架构的工作，通过一种高效的两阶段训练方案实现AR视频生成。当75%的层被替换为混合线性注意力时，模型实现了高达2.26倍的实际加速和54%的内存减少，同时保持可比质量并提升时序一致性。

</details>


### [39] [ArtMesh: Part-Aware Articulated Mesh Fields with Motion-Consistent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16582)
*Sylvia Yuan,Dan Wang,Ravi Ramamoorthi,Xinrui Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArtMesh是一种基于网格的可微渲染方法，用于从多视角图像中重建具有逐部分刚体运动的铰接物体，以显式连接的三角网格形式输出。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的3D高斯泼溅铰接重建方法基于无结构点云，缺乏表面拓扑，难以推理部件边界或沿物体连接性强制执行运动一致性。

Method: 提出部件感知受限Delaunay网格重划技术生成不跨越语义边界的连通子网格；通过双向顶点级运动一致性和像素级运动一致性优化动态网格场的铰接参数。

Result: 在Artriculate-100基准（100个铰接物体，16个类别）上，ArtMesh在关节参数估计和部件级几何重建上优于现有3DGS方法，在多可动部件物体上提升最大。

Conclusion: 基于网格的可微渲染结合部件感知拓扑约束，能有效提升铰接物体重建的质量和运动一致性。

Original Abstract: We present ArtMesh, a mesh-native method for reconstructing articulated objects explicitly as connected triangle meshes with per-part rigid motion from multi-view images in start and end states. Existing 3D Gaussian Splatting pipelines for articulated reconstruction inherit the unstructured point-based geometry of their splatting base, which provides no surface topology for reasoning about part boundaries or enforcing motion consistency along the object's connectivity. ArtMesh instead builds on a mesh-based differentiable rendering backbone, enabling part-aware dynamics to act directly on the structured topology. To make the topology compatible with articulation, we introduce part-aware restricted Delaunay remeshing, producing connected submeshes whose triangles do not cross semantic part boundaries. The dynamic mesh field then optimizes articulation using bidirectional Vertex-wise Motion Consistency on transported mesh vertices and Pixel-wise Motion Consistency on rendered RGB-D observations. We introduce Articulate-100, a new benchmark of 100 articulated objects spanning 16 PartNet-Mobility categories. On this benchmark, ArtMesh outperforms prior 3DGS-based pipelines in joint parameter estimation and part-level geometric reconstruction, with the largest gains on objects with many movable parts.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了ArtMesh，一种原生网格方法，用于从起始和终止状态的多视角图像中，将铰接物体显式重建为具有逐部分刚体运动的连通三角网格。现有的用于铰接重建的3D高斯泼溅管线继承了其泼溅基元无结构的点基几何特性，这缺乏用于推理部件边界或沿物体连接性强制执行运动一致性的表面拓扑。ArtMesh转而基于网格的可微渲染骨干，使部件感知动力学能直接作用于结构化拓扑。为使拓扑兼容铰接，我们引入了部件感知受限Delaunay网格重划，生成其三角形不跨越语义部件边界的连通子网格。随后，动态网格场利用对传输网格顶点的双向顶点级运动一致性以及对渲染的RGB-D观测的像素级运动一致性来优化铰接。我们提出了Artriculate-100，一个包含跨越16个PartNet-Mobility类别的100个铰接物体的新基准。在该基准上，ArtMesh在关节参数估计和部件级几何重建方面优于先前的基于3DGS的管线，在多可动部件物体上取得了最大的性能提升。

</details>


### [40] [Controlla: Learning Controllability via Graph-Constrained Latent Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16603)
*Jamuna S. Murthy,Amin Karimi Monsefi,Rajiv Ramnath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Controlla提出了一种基于结构化隐空间几何的模块化分解控制框架，通过图约束最优传输实现多模态生成中的属性轨迹可控性与身份保持，并在新构建的AffectHuman-43K基准上验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有可控多模态生成方法（如提示、引导或辅助模块）未显式结构化语义属性的演化过程，导致身份漂移和跨模态行为不一致。

Method: 提出Controlla框架：从多模态输入中学习身份因子和属性因子，利用图约束最优传输将其与图先验对齐，促使属性沿图一致轨迹演化同时保留参考身份。

Result: 在AffectHuman-43K基准上，Controlla在可控性、身份保持和跨模态对齐方面取得一致改进；还进行了图敏感性、可扩展性和鲁棒性分析。

Conclusion: 将可控性视为结构化隐几何性质的分解控制框架能有效提升多模态生成的一致性和忠实度。

Original Abstract: Controllable multimodal generation is commonly formulated as an inference-time conditioning problem using prompts, guidance, or auxiliary modules. While effective, such approaches do not explicitly structure how semantic attributes evolve, which can lead to identity drift and inconsistent cross-modal behavior. We propose Controlla, a modular factorized-control framework that treats controllability as a property of structured latent geometry. Controlla learns identity and attribute factors from multimodal inputs and aligns them with graph priors using graph-constrained optimal transport, encouraging attributes to follow graph-consistent trajectories while preserving reference identity. To evaluate this setting, we construct AffectHuman-43K, a leakage-aware multimodal benchmark for reference-grounded affective control, and introduce geometry-aware metrics for trajectory consistency and latent disentanglement. Experiments show consistent improvements in controllability, identity preservation, and cross-modal alignment, with additional analyses on graph sensitivity, extensibility, and robustness.

中文摘要翻译: 可控多模态生成通常被建模为推理时的条件化问题，通过提示、引导或辅助模块实现。尽管有效，这类方法并未显式结构化语义属性的演化过程，可能导致身份漂移和跨模态行为不一致。我们提出Controlla，一种模块化解耦控制框架，将可控性视为结构化隐空间几何的性质。Controlla从多模态输入中学习身份因子和属性因子，并利用图约束最优传输将其与图先验对齐，促使属性沿图一致轨迹演化，同时保留参考身份。为评估该设置，我们构建了AffectHuman-43K，一个面向参考情感控制的无泄漏多模态基准，并引入了几何感知指标用于轨迹一致性和隐空间解耦度量。实验表明，Controlla在可控性、身份保持和跨模态对齐上均取得一致改进，并提供了图敏感性、可扩展性和鲁棒性的进一步分析。

</details>


### [41] [SCARED-C: Corrected Camera Poses for Endoscopic Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16628)
*John J. Han,Adam Schmidt,Max Allan,Jie Ying Wu,Omid Mohareri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCARED-C，对SCARED内窥镜深度估计数据集进行修正，将可靠RGB-D对从35个扩展到17,135个。该方法利用COLMAP重新估计所有帧的相机位姿，并通过尺度恢复对齐到度量空间。通过立体视差评估和单目深度估计实验验证了修正位姿的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: SCARED数据集中非关键帧的深度图依赖机器人运动学，导致位姿误差较大，仅有35个关键帧具有可靠标注，限制了数据集的可用性。因此，需要一种方法扩展可靠标注帧的数量。

Method: 采用COLMAP（一种运动恢复结构系统）重新估计所有帧的相机位姿，然后通过尺度恢复步骤，利用真实关键帧深度图将重建结果对齐到度量空间，从而获得更多可靠的RGB-D对。

Result: 可靠RGB-D对从35个扩展到17,135个；通过立体视差评估和单目深度估计实验验证了修正位姿的有效性。

Conclusion: SCARED-C有效地修正并扩展了SCARED数据集的可靠标注，为内窥镜深度估计研究提供了更丰富的基准数据。修正后的数据集和代码已公开发布。

Original Abstract: The SCARED dataset is a widely used benchmark for endoscopic depth estimation, offering ground-truth 3D reconstructions captured with a structured light sensor. However, the depth maps for non-keyframe images rely on robot kinematics that introduce substantial pose errors, limiting the reliably labeled portion of the dataset to 35 keyframes. We present SCARED-C, a corrected version of the SCARED dataset that expands the number of reliable RGB-D pairs from 35 to 17,135. Our pipeline applies COLMAP, a Structure-from-Motion system, to re-estimate camera poses for all frames, followed by a scale recovery step that aligns the resulting reconstructions to metric space using the ground-truth keyframe depth maps. We validate the corrected poses through (1) stereo disparity evaluation and (2) monocular depth estimation experiments. The corrected dataset and code are publicly released to the community.

中文摘要翻译: SCARED数据集是内窥镜深度估计领域广泛使用的基准数据集，其通过结构光传感器捕捉的真实3D重建提供了深度真值。然而，非关键帧图像的深度图依赖于机器人运动学，这引入了显著的位姿误差，使得数据集中仅有35个关键帧具有可靠标注。本文提出SCARED-C，即SCARED数据集的修正版本，将可靠RGB-D对从35个扩展至17,135个。我们的流程采用运动恢复结构系统COLMAP重新估计所有帧的相机位姿，随后通过尺度恢复步骤，利用真实关键帧深度图将重建结果对齐到度量空间。我们通过（1）立体视差评估和（2）单目深度估计实验验证了修正位姿的有效性。修正后的数据集和代码已公开发布给社区。

</details>


### [42] [Right Predictions, Misleading Explanations: On the Vulnerability of Vision-Language Model Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16651)
*Narges Babadi,Hadis Karimipour*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉-语言模型（特别是CLIP）中的解释热力图可以在不改变模型预测的情况下被系统性地操控，揭示了预测行为与解释忠实度之间的脱节。作者提出X-Shift攻击方法，通过扰动图像块级视觉表示将解释热力图重定向到语义无关区域，在多个数据集和模型架构上验证了这一漏洞。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言模型解释机制虽然被用于支持透明度和信任，但其鲁棒性尚未被充分理解。特别是在需要人类监督的高风险应用中，解释热力图是否真实反映模型推理过程存在疑问，因此需要系统研究解释机制在对抗条件下的忠实性。

Method: 提出X-Shift，一种灰盒攻击方法，通过扰动图像块级（patch-level）视觉表示，在保持模型原始预测不变的前提下，将解释热力图重定向到语义不相关的区域。该方法不修改模型参数，适用于多种CLIP架构和解释方法。

Result: 在ImageNet-1k、MS-COCO和Flickr30K数据集上，X-Shift在不可察觉的扰动下持续降低了解释对齐度，同时保持预测稳定性。标准面向预测的对抗攻击即使在更大扰动预算下也无法复现相同的解释偏移行为。

Conclusion: 当前视觉-语言模型的解释机制存在根本性局限，其作为模型可信度可靠指标在高影响应用中的使用值得担忧。

Original Abstract: Explanation mechanisms are increasingly used to support transparency and trust in vision-language models (VLMs), particularly in settings where model decisions require human oversight. However, the robustness of these explanations remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we investigate whether explanation heatmaps in VLMs, particularly CLIP-based models, faithfully reflect model reasoning under adversarial conditions. We show that explanation maps can be systematically manipulated while preserving the model's original prediction, revealing a disconnect between predictive behavior and explanation faithfulness. To study this vulnerability, we introduce X-Shift, a novel grey-box attack that perturbs patch-level visual representations to redirect explanation heatmaps toward semantically irrelevant regions without altering the predicted output. Unlike conventional adversarial attacks that aim to induce misclassification, X-Shift specifically targets the integrity of the explanation process itself. The attack operates without modifying model parameters and generalizes across multiple CLIP architectures and explanation methods. We evaluate the proposed approach on ImageNet-1k, MS-COCO, and Flickr30K, demonstrating consistent degradation in explanation alignment under imperceptible perturbations while maintaining prediction stability. Furthermore, standard prediction-oriented adversarial attacks fail to reproduce the same explanation-shifting behavior even under substantially larger perturbation budgets. Our findings highlight a fundamental limitation of current explanation mechanisms in VLMs and raise concerns about their use as reliable indicators of model trustworthiness in high-impact applications.

中文摘要翻译: 解释机制越来越被用于支持视觉-语言模型（VLM）的透明度和可信度，特别是在需要人类监督的模型决策场景中。然而，这些解释的鲁棒性尚未得到充分理解。本文研究了VLM（尤其是基于CLIP的模型）中的解释热力图在对抗条件下是否忠实地反映模型推理过程。我们证明，解释图可以在保持模型原始预测不变的情况下被系统性操控，揭示了预测行为与解释忠实度之间的脱节。为研究这一漏洞，我们提出了X-Shift——一种新颖的灰盒攻击方法，通过扰动图像块级视觉表示，在不改变预测输出的前提下将解释热力图重定向到语义无关区域。与旨在诱导错误分类的传统对抗攻击不同，X-Shift专门针对解释过程本身的完整性。该攻击无需修改模型参数，且适用于多种CLIP架构和解释方法。我们在ImageNet-1k、MS-COCO和Flickr30K上评估了所提方法，证明了在不可察觉的扰动下解释对齐度持续退化，同时预测稳定性得以保持。此外，标准的面向预测的对抗攻击即使在显著更大的扰动预算下也无法复现相同的解释偏移行为。我们的发现揭示了当前视觉-语言模型解释机制的根本性局限，并对其在高影响应用中作为模型可信度可靠指标的使用提出了质疑。

</details>


### [43] [Multi-Object Tracking Consistently Improves Wildlife Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16672)
*Mufhumudzi Muthivhi,Jiahao Huo,Fredrik Gustafsson,Terence L. van Zyl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文利用多目标跟踪（MOT）模型融合相机陷阱视频中连续帧的分类概率，以修正单帧分类错误，提升野生动物识别模型的鲁棒性和准确性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的野生动物分类模型在高质量数据集上表现优异，但在真实环境中对时间连续序列的推理结果不稳定，同一目标的预测标签在帧间频繁跳变。因此需要利用时序信息来增强分类的鲁棒性。

Method: 采用多种标准多目标跟踪（MOT）模型链接连续帧中的检测结果，构建目标轨迹；利用轨迹融合各帧的softmax分类概率，得到一致的共识类别标签，替代因噪声导致的误分类。

Result: 所提策略在所有数据集和所有指标上均优于单独的分类器。最佳MOT模型在三个MOT数据集上分别将加权F1分数提升了5.1%、3.1%和2.0%。

Conclusion: 利用时序信息通过MOT轨迹融合分类概率，能有效提升野生动物分类模型在真实相机陷阱数据上的预测稳定性和准确性。

Original Abstract: Camera traps have become a common tool for wildlife monitoring efforts in ecological research and biodiversity conservation. Wildlife classification models have benefited from the increase in wildlife visual data. These models reach high levels of accuracy on curated, high-quality datasets. However, their performance remains sensitive to real-world environmental constraints. They often produce inconsistent predictions when performing inference on temporally coherent sequences. The predicted label for a single individual shifts rapidly between frames. This study exploits the temporal nature of camera-trap data to augment inferred predictions from a wildlife classification model. Specifically, we adopt several standard Multi-Object Tracking (MOT) models to link detections across consecutive frames. The curated trajectories are used to fuse the softmax class probabilities. The fused probability score produces a single consensus class label estimate that overrides misclassifications caused by noise. The analysis of the experimental results shows that our proposed strategy improves over a standalone classifier over all datasets and for each metric. Specifically, the best-performing MOT models gain a weighted F1-Score of 5.1%, 3.1% and 2.0% over the classifier across three MOT datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 相机陷阱已成为生态研究和生物多样性保护中野生动物监测的常用工具。野生动物分类模型受益于野生动物视觉数据的增长，在精心策划的高质量数据集上达到了较高准确率。然而，它们的性能仍对真实环境中的约束条件敏感。当对时间连续的序列进行推理时，这些模型常常产生不一致的预测结果，单个个体的预测标签在帧间快速跳变。本研究利用相机陷阱数据的时间特性来增强野生动物分类模型的推断预测。具体而言，我们采用多种标准多目标跟踪（MOT）模型来链接连续帧中的检测结果，利用生成的轨迹融合softmax类别概率。融合后的概率分数产生一个一致的共识类别标签估计，从而覆盖由噪声引起的误分类。实验结果表明，我们提出的策略在所有数据集和所有指标上均优于单独的分类器。具体而言，最佳性能的MOT模型在三个MOT数据集上相比分类器分别提升了5.1%、3.1%和2.0%的加权F1分数。

</details>


### [44] [MAVEN A Multi-Agent Framework for Multicultural Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16716)
*Shuowei Li,Yuming Zhao,Parth Bhalerao,Oana Ignat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAVEN是一个多智能体提示词细化框架，通过将提示词分解为人物、动作和地点三个维度并由专门智能体并行或串行处理，显著提升文本到视频生成中的文化保真度。基于243个跨三种文化（中美罗）的基准评估表明，并行专业化策略在保持视频质量的同时大幅改善了文化相关性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本到视频生成技术在视觉保真度上进展迅速，但缺乏对单个提示中多元文化忠实表征能力的系统性探索，特别是单文化和跨文化场景下的文化保真度问题。

Method: 提出MAVEN多智能体提示词细化框架，将原始提示词分解为人物(person)、动作(action)和地点(location)三个维度，由三个专门化智能体分别处理，支持并行和串行两种运行模式，以提升生成视频的文化保真度。

Result: 基于243个文化提示和972个对应视频的基准评估，结合CLIP指标、VLM评判和视频质量度量，发现多智能体细化特别是并行专业化策略在保持视觉质量和时间一致性的同时，显著提升了文化相关性。

Conclusion: 多智能体提示词细化框架能够有效改善文本到视频生成中的文化保真度，其中并行专业化模式效果最优，为跨文化视频生成提供了可行的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) generation has rapidly progressed in visual fidelity, yet its ability to faithfully represent multiple cultures within a single prompt remains underexplored. We introduce MAVEN, a multi-agent prompt refinement framework designed to improve cultural fidelity in both mono-cultural and cross-cultural T2V generation. MAVEN decomposes prompts into person, action, and location dimensions, handled by specialized agents operating in parallel or sequentially. To support systematic evaluation, we contribute a new benchmark of 243 culturally grounded prompts and 972 corresponding videos, spanning three cultures (Chinese, American, Romanian), three action categories, and both mono-cultural and cross-cultural scenarios. Evaluations combining CLIP-based metrics, VLM-as-judge assessments, and videoquality measures show that multi-agent refinement, particularly parallel specialization, significantly improves cultural relevance while preserving visual quality and temporal consistency. The dataset and code are available athttps://github.com/AIM-SCU/CRAFT

中文摘要翻译: 文本到视频生成在视觉保真度方面取得了快速发展，但其在单个提示中忠实表征多元文化的能力仍未得到充分探索。我们提出了MAVEN，一个多智能体提示词细化框架，旨在提升单文化和跨文化文本到视频生成中的文化保真度。MAVEN将提示词分解为人物、动作和地点三个维度，由专门化的智能体以并行或串行方式处理。为支持系统性评估，我们贡献了一个新的基准数据集，包含243个文化相关的提示词及其对应的972个视频，涵盖三种文化（中国、美国、罗马尼亚）、三个动作类别以及单文化和跨文化场景。结合基于CLIP的度量、以VLM作为评判的评估以及视频质量度量，实验结果表明多智能体细化，特别是并行专业化策略，在保持视觉质量和时间一致性的同时，显著改善了文化相关性。数据集和代码可在https://github.com/AIM-SCU/CRAFT获取。

</details>


### [45] [Compositional Adversarial Training for Robust Visual Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16720)
*Anirudh Satheesh,Michael-Andrei Panaitescu-Liess,Andrew Xu,Georgios Milis,Heng Huang,Zikui Cai,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出组合对抗训练（CAT）框架，通过可微分的对抗性攻击序列训练鲁棒水印，替代传统随机后处理增强，在图像和视频水印任务上显著提升了水印容量和鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统鲁棒水印训练采用随机后处理增强，但随机采样难以覆盖真实攻击管线的组合空间，导致训练不稳定且样本效率低下。

Method: 提出组合对抗训练（CAT），将水印鲁棒性建模为结构化组合变换空间上的最小-最大问题。CAT学习一个序列可微对抗器，通过直通Gumbel-Softmax攻击选择与熵正则化，实现端到端可微的反向传播，跨攻击族聚合梯度信息，实现更快、更平滑的收敛。

Result: 在单步攻击设置下提升水印容量高达63.5%，组合攻击设置下提升13.0%；自回归设置下在困难几何变换上TPR@FPR=1%平均提升12%。在VideoSeal、PixelSeal及WMAR等水印方法上均一致超越随机增强基线。

Conclusion: 鲁棒视觉水印应从训练对抗随机独立损坏转向训练自适应组合对抗器，才能更有效地提升鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Robust watermarking is typically trained with random post-processing augmentation, but random sampling under-covers the combinatorial space of realistic attack pipelines and rarely encounters the rare compositions that actually break detection. This leads to unstable training and poor sample efficiency. We instead formulate watermark robustness as a min-max problem over a structured space of compositional transformations. We propose Compositional Adversarial Training (CAT), a plug-in framework that learns a sequential differentiable adversary that observes the current watermarked image and selects an attack family at each step to maximally disrupt message recovery. CAT combines a straight-through Gumbel-Softmax attack selection with entropy regularization, allowing the backward pass to be end-to-end differentiable and aggregate gradient information across attack families, yielding faster, smoother convergence without collapsing to a single attack mode. We evaluate CAT on post-generation watermarks VideoSeal 0.0, VideoSeal 1.0, and PixelSeal and in-generation WMAR under both single-step and two-step attack suites, on in-distribution and multiple out-of-distribution image and video benchmarks. CAT consistently outperforms random-augmentation baselines trained with the same augmentation budget, with the largest gains on hard composed attacks and OOD evaluations; improving overall watermark capacity by up to $63.5\%$ in the single-step attack setting and $13.0\%$ in the compositional setting. In the autoregressive setting, CAT improves the TPR@FPR$=1\%$ by $12\%$ on average on difficult geometric transformations. These results show that robust visual watermarking benefits from training against adaptive compositional adversaries rather than independent random corruptions.

中文摘要翻译: 鲁棒水印通常使用随机后处理增强进行训练，但随机采样无法充分覆盖现实攻击管线的组合空间，也很难遇到真正能破坏检测的罕见组合。这导致训练不稳定且样本效率低下。本文转而将水印鲁棒性建模为结构化组合变换空间上的最小-最大问题。我们提出组合对抗训练（CAT），一个即插即用的框架，它学习一个序列可微的对抗器，该对抗器观察当前已加水印的图像，并在每一步选择一个攻击族以最大程度地破坏消息恢复。CAT将直通Gumbel-Softmax攻击选择与熵正则化相结合，使得反向传播能够端到端可微，并在攻击族之间聚合梯度信息，从而实现更快、更平滑的收敛，同时避免坍缩到单一攻击模式。我们在生成后水印方法VideoSeal 0.0、VideoSeal 1.0和PixelSeal以及生成中水印方法WMAR上，在单步和两步攻击套件下，对分布内和多个分布外的图像及视频基准进行了评估。CAT在使用相同增强预算的情况下，一致优于随机增强基线，在困难组合攻击和分布外评估上提升最大；在单步攻击设置下，水印容量提升高达63.5%，在组合攻击设置下提升13.0%。在自回归设置中，CAT在困难几何变换上平均将TPR@FPR=1%提升了12%。这些结果表明，鲁棒视觉水印从对抗自适应组合对抗器的训练中获益，而非依赖于独立的随机损坏。

</details>


### [46] [CAB: Accelerating Flow and Diffusion Sampling via Rectification and Corrected Adams-Bashforth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16736)
*Anuska Roy,Pravin Nair*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CAB（校正Adams-Bashforth），一种无需训练、可同时加速流模型和扩散模型的采样器，通过将采样动力学变换到公共校正坐标系并应用带校正项的多步Adams-Bashforth预测器，在低NFE（6-20）区间显著改善质量-效率权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有流模型和扩散模型虽能生成高保真、高分辨率图像，但采样时需大量函数评估（NFE）。现有加速方法要么需要额外的蒸馏训练，要么依赖无训练的高阶求解器，两者在低NFE预算下都会降低样本质量。因此，亟需一种无需训练、不牺牲质量且能兼容两类模型的通用加速采样方法。

Method: CAB方法首先将采样动力学变换到一个公共的校正坐标系（rectified coordinate system）中，然后应用多步Adams-Bashforth预测器，并基于过去的速度评估值添加一个简单的校正项（该校正项不增加额外的NFE）。该方法简单，在不同模型类中保持相同的算法形式，具有至少三阶局部截断误差和二阶全局误差。

Result: 在预训练的流模型和扩散模型（包括类条件和大规模文生图基准）上的实验表明，CAB在6-20 NFEs的低步数范围内改善了质量-NFE权衡；在更高步数下，与强无训练采样器相比仍具有竞争力。

Conclusion: CAB提供了一种简单、统一、无需训练的高阶加速采样方案，在低NFE场景下显著优于现有方法，且在不同模型类上表现一致，为扩散模型和流模型的快速采样提供了一种实用有效的训练免费方案。

Original Abstract: Flow and diffusion models achieve high-fidelity, high-resolution image synthesis, but often require many function evaluations (NFEs) at sampling time. Existing acceleration methods either require additional training through distillation or rely on training-free high-order solvers, and both can degrade sample quality at low NFE budgets. We propose CAB (Corrected Adams-Bashforth), a training-free sampler that accelerates both flow and diffusion models. CAB first transforms the sampling dynamics to a common rectified coordinate system, and then applies a multistep Adams-Bashforth predictor augmented with a simple correction term based on past velocity evaluations and therefore incurs no additional NFEs. The resulting method is simple, has the same algorithmic form across model classes, and has at least third-order local truncation error and second-order global error. Experiments on pretrained flow and diffusion models, including class-conditional and large-scale text-to-image benchmarks, show that CAB improves quality-NFE trade-offs in the low-step regime of 6-20 NFEs. It also remains competitive with strong training-free samplers at higher step counts across most tested models. The official implementation is available at https://github.com/Anuska-Roy/CAB.

中文摘要翻译: 流模型和扩散模型能够实现高保真、高分辨率的图像合成，但通常在采样时需要大量的函数评估（NFEs）。现有的加速方法要么需要通过蒸馏进行额外训练，要么依赖于无训练的高阶求解器，而这两者在低NFE预算下都会降低样本质量。我们提出CAB（校正Adams-Bashforth），一种无需训练的采样器，可同时加速流模型和扩散模型。CAB首先将采样动力学变换到一个公共的校正坐标系中，然后应用多步Adams-Bashforth预测器，并基于过去的速度评估值添加一个简单的校正项，因此不增加额外的NFE。所得方法简单，在不同模型类中具有相同的算法形式，且具有至少三阶局部截断误差和二阶全局误差。在预训练流模型和扩散模型（包括类条件和大规模文生图基准）上的实验表明，CAB在6-20 NFEs的低步数范围内改善了质量-NFE权衡。在大多数测试模型的高步数下，它也能与强大的无训练采样器保持竞争力。官方实现见 https://github.com/Anuska-Roy/CAB。

</details>


### [47] [TRACE: Evidence Grounding-Guided Multi-Video Event Understanding and Claim Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16740)
*Pengyu Yan,Akhil Gorugantu,Mahesh Bhosale,Abdul Wasi,Vishvesh Trivedi,David Doermann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRACE提出了一种“先定位后推理”的多视频事件理解框架，通过OCR和物体检测构建可文本搜索的时间线，再由纯文本LLM进行查询感知的证据定位，最后引导LVLM生成事实性更强的声明和跨视频引用，在MAGMaR 2026和WikiVideo上显著提升了事实完整性和归因忠实度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有大型视觉语言模型在多视频事件理解中，由于上下文预算有限且难以精确定位关键片段，常常遗漏广播图形、字幕、记分牌等密集信息线索，导致事实完整性和归因准确性不足。

Method: TRACE采用“先定位后推理”策略：首先利用OCR和物体检测为每个视频构建结构化的可文本搜索时间线；然后由纯文本LLM进行查询感知的证据定位，选择相关时刻；最后将检索到的帧及其定位摘要用于引导LVLM生成声明并进行跨视频引用整合。

Result: 在MAGMaR 2026验证集上，TRACE将宏平均MiRAGE F1从0.705提升至0.811，引用召回率从0.440提升至0.628，并在官方排行榜上取得了当前最优结果。

Conclusion: 结构化定位显著提升了多视频事件理解的事实完整性和归因忠实度，证明了“先定位后推理”策略在长视频异构语料分析中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Multi-video event understanding demands models that can locate and attribute query-relevant evidence scattered across long, heterogeneous video corpora. Existing large vision-language models (LVLMs) often underperform in this regime because they quickly exhaust their context budget and struggle to precisely localize evidentially important segments, frequently missing dense informational cues such as broadcast graphics, subtitles, and scoreboards. We introduce TRACE, an evidence grounding-guided framework that follows a ground-before-reasoning strategy for multi-video event reasoning. Our approach first builds a structured, text-searchable timeline for each video using OCR and object detection. A text-only LLM then conducts query-aware evidence localization, selecting relevant moments prior to any downstream visual reasoning. The retrieved frames and their grounding summaries are subsequently used to steer LVLM-based claim generation and cross-video citation consolidation. Experiments on MAGMaR 2026 and WikiVideo demonstrate that structured grounding markedly boosts factual completeness and attribution fidelity. On the MAGMaR validation split, TRACE raises macro-average MiRAGE F1 from 0.705 to 0.811 compared to an unguided Qwen3-VL-30B baseline, with especially strong improvements in citation recall from 0.440 to 0.628. The method also attains state-of-the-art results on the official MAGMaR 2026 leaderboard.

中文摘要翻译: 多视频事件理解要求模型能够定位并归因于分散在长且异构的视频语料库中的查询相关证据。现有大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在此场景下表现不佳，因为它们容易耗尽上下文预算，难以精确定位证据关键片段，并且经常遗漏广播图形、字幕和记分牌等密集信息线索。我们提出了TRACE，一种基于证据定位引导的框架，采用“先定位后推理”策略进行多视频事件推理。该方法首先利用OCR和物体检测为每个视频构建结构化的可文本搜索时间线。然后，纯文本LLM进行查询感知的证据定位，在进入下游视觉推理之前选择相关时刻。检索到的帧及其定位摘要随后用于引导LVLM生成声明并进行跨视频引用整合。在MAGMaR 2026和WikiVideo上的实验表明，结构化定位显著提升了事实完整性和归因忠实度。在MAGMaR验证集上，TRACE将宏平均MiRAGE F1从0.705提升至0.811（相比未引导的Qwen3-VL-30B基线），引用召回率从0.440大幅提升至0.628。该方法还在MAGMaR 2026官方排行榜上取得了当前最优结果。

</details>


### [48] [EVA01: Unified Native 3D Understanding and Generation via Mixture-of-Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16745)
*Zongyuan Yang,Mingjing Yi,Wanli Ma,Chenzhuo Fan,Bocheng Li,Baolin Liu,Yuke Lou,Yingde Song,Yongping Xiong,Zhengdong Guo,Shimu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EVA01框架，基于混合专家Transformer架构，将3D网格作为原生模态融入多模态大语言模型，实现了原生文本到3D生成与长上下文几何编辑，无需中间2D表示。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法要么将3D视为外部输出而非多模态序列的原生组成部分，要么依赖扩散模型将语义理解与几何推理解耦，缺乏对几何流形与MLLM特征空间对齐的系统分析。

Method: 基于Mixture-of-Transformers架构，将模型解耦为预训练的语义理解专家和结构镜像的生成专家，通过共享全局自注意力与硬模态路由耦合，实现语义潜空间与几何流形的直接对齐。

Result: 在原生文本到3D生成任务上达到最先进保真度，实现了无状态重建管线无法实现的长上下文多轮几何编辑与身份保持能力。

Conclusion: EVA01为将2D基础模型与3D任务整合提供了架构洞见，为3D原生多模态系统的设计指明了方向。

Original Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of integrating 3D meshes as a native modality within Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Diffusion-based large reconstruction models decouple semantic understanding from geometric reasoning, operating as stateless reconstructors conditioned on dense 2D pixel priors. Recent MLLM-based methods treat the 3D modality as an external output rather than a native component of the multimodal sequence, making incremental adaptations without a systematic analysis of how geometric manifolds align with MLLM feature spaces. We introduce EVA01, a unified framework that extends the modality boundary of MLLMs to natively incorporate 3D mesh understanding, generation, and context-aware editing. Built upon a Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture, EVA01 decouples the model into a pre-trained Understanding Expert ($E_{\mathrm{und}}$) and a structurally mirrored Generation Expert ($E_{\mathrm{gen}}$), coupled through shared global self-attention with hard modality routing. This design aligns the semantic latent space of the MLLM backbone with the geometric manifold, enabling direct transfer of multimodal priors without intermediate 2D representations. Results show that EVA01 achieves state-of-the-art native text-to-3D generation fidelity and unlocks robust long-context multi-turn geometric editing with identity preservation, a capability fundamentally inaccessible to stateless reconstruction pipelines. Our findings further offer architectural insights for integrating 2D foundation models with 3D tasks, informing the design of 3D-native multimodal systems. Project Page: https://www.seeles.ai/research/pages/EVA01

中文摘要翻译: 本文解决了将3D网格作为原生模态集成到多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中的挑战。基于扩散的大规模重建模型将语义理解与几何推理解耦，作为以密集2D像素先验为条件的无状态重建器运行。近期基于MLLM的方法将3D模态视为外部输出而非多模态序列的原生组成部分，仅做增量式适配，缺乏对几何流形与MLLM特征空间对齐的系统分析。我们提出EVA01，一个统一框架，将MLLM的模态边界扩展以原生地融入3D网格的理解、生成和上下文感知编辑。EVA01基于混合专家Transformer（MoT）架构构建，将模型解耦为预训练的理解专家（$E_{\mathrm{und}}$）和结构镜像的生成专家（$E_{\mathrm{gen}}$），通过共享全局自注意力与硬模态路由耦合。该设计将MLLM主干的语义潜空间与几何流形对齐，使得无需中间2D表示即可直接迁移多模态先验。结果表明，EVA01实现了最先进的原生文本到3D生成保真度，并解锁了具有身份保持能力的鲁棒长上下文多轮几何编辑，这是无状态重建管线根本上无法实现的能力。我们的发现进一步为将2D基础模型与3D任务整合提供了架构洞见，并为3D原生多模态系统的设计提供指导。项目页面：https://www.seeles.ai/research/pages/EVA01

</details>


### [49] [GLT-PEFT: Gated Lie-Tucker Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for Alzheimer's Disease Diagnosis with Hippocampal Segmentation Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16769)
*Guanghua He,Hancan Zhu,Gaohang Yu,An Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GLT-PEFT框架，结合Tucker分解和Lie群变换，实现3D卷积核的张量感知低秩适配与几何保结构乘法更新，并通过门控机制统一加性/乘性微调，用于阿尔茨海默病诊断中的跨任务迁移。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有PEFT方法主要针对矩阵结构参数设计，不适合医学影像模型中的高维卷积核；且多采用加性更新，缺乏对预训练参数几何结构的保持，而乘性（几何感知）更新难以在统一框架中集成。

Method: 提出GLT-PEFT框架：1) 使用Tucker分解对3D卷积核进行张量感知的低秩适配；2) 引入Lie群变换实现保结构的乘法更新；3) 设计门控机制融合加性与乘性更新形式，形成统一稳定的微调策略。将海马体分割预训练模型迁移至AD分类下游任务。

Result: 实验表明GLT-PEFT在显著减少可训练参数的同时，实现了有效的跨任务迁移，验证了其在医学影像模型中高效、鲁棒适配的有效性。

Conclusion: GLT-PEFT通过张量分解与几何感知更新的有机结合，为医学影像模型中的参数高效微调提供了一种统一且稳定的新范式。

Original Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has emerged as a promising paradigm for adapting pretrained models under limited data conditions. However, most existing PEFT methods are designed for matrix-structured parameters and are not well suited for high-dimensional convolutional kernels in medical imaging models. Moreover, they typically rely on additive updates and lack mechanisms to preserve the geometric structure of pretrained parameters, while multiplicative (geometry-aware) updates are difficult to integrate within a unified framework. To address this issue, this paper proposes GLT-PEFT, a gated Lie-Tucker parameter-efficient fine-tuning framework for Alzheimer's disease (AD) diagnosis. The proposed approach transfers a hippocampal segmentation pretrained model to a downstream classification task. Tucker decomposition enables tensor-aware low-rank adaptation of 3D convolutional kernels, while Lie group-based transformations provide structure-preserving multiplicative updates. A gating mechanism further reconciles additive and multiplicative update forms, resulting in a unified and more stable fine-tuning strategy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GLT-PEFT achieves effective cross-task transfer while significantly reducing trainable parameters, highlighting its effectiveness for efficient and robust adaptation in medical imaging models.

中文摘要翻译: 参数高效微调（PEFT）已成为在有限数据条件下适配预训练模型的一种有前景的范式。然而，大多数现有PEFT方法针对矩阵结构参数设计，不适用于医学影像模型中的高维卷积核。此外，它们通常依赖于加性更新，缺乏保持预训练参数几何结构的机制，而乘性（几何感知）更新难以在统一框架中集成。针对这一问题，本文提出了GLT-PEFT——一种用于阿尔茨海默病（AD）诊断的门控Lie-Tucker参数高效微调框架。该方法将海马体分割预训练模型迁移至下游分类任务。Tucker分解实现了3D卷积核的张量感知低秩适配，而基于Lie群的变换则提供了保结构的乘法更新。门控机制进一步调和了加性与乘性更新形式，形成统一且更稳定的微调策略。大量实验表明，GLT-PEFT在显著减少可训练参数的同时实现了有效的跨任务迁移，凸显了其在医学影像模型中实现高效鲁棒适配的有效性。

</details>


### [50] [CANSURF: An ASV-View Can Dataset and Benchmark for Detection and Tracking of Surface-Level Debris](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16774)
*Zaid Aljundi,Zahra F. Rahmatullah,Mostafa Elemam,Abdullah Moosa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个水面铝罐检测数据集CANSURF（含~57k增强图像），并系统评测了多种检测与跟踪管道。实验表明，YOLOv11+CANSURF相比通用数据集性能提升12倍；YOLOv11+ByteTrack跟踪稳定性最佳，YOLOv11+SAHI在远场小目标召回上更优。该数据集填补了水面视角铝罐检测的空白。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 水面小尺寸、高反光目标（如铝罐）在强光、波纹、部分淹没等条件下难以被自主清理船（ASV）从远距离检测，现有通用数据集无法覆盖这一复杂场景，且缺乏公开的水面视角铝罐数据集。

Method: 1) 构建CANSURF数据集：从视频中提取约7.3k原始图像并标注边界框，通过十种数据增强方式扩充至~57k训练/验证图像，涵盖多种光照和水面状态；2) 设计并基准测试多组检测器及检测-跟踪流水线（含YOLOv11+ByteTrack、YOLOv11+SAHI等），专门适配水面作业场景。

Result: 1) 在CANSURF上训练YOLOv11，性能比使用通用数据集提升12倍；2) YOLOv11+ByteTrack获得最稳定的跟踪轨迹（身份切换最少）和更强的多目标精度；3) YOLOv11+SAHI提高了远场铝罐的召回率，但以降低全上下文输入下的精度为代价；4) 针对单罐拾取任务，YOLOv11+SAHI能检测到最多铝罐。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了首个面向水面视角铝罐检测的开源数据集CANSURF，并系统评估了多种检测与跟踪方案，验证了数据集的有效性和各方案在任务中的适用性，为水面自主清理提供了可复现的基准。

Original Abstract: Surface-level marine debris remains a practical bottleneck for autonomous clean-up, where small, reflective targets (e.g., aluminum cans) must be detected at distance under glare, ripples, and partial submersion. This paper presents, an ASV vision system and a new surface-can dataset. The dataset comprises ~7.3k raw images extracted from videos and annotated with bounding boxes, expanded via ten augmentation types to ~57k training/validation images spanning diverse lighting and water states. A family of detector and detector-tracker pipelines tailored to surface operations were benchmarked. Training YOLOv11 on CANSURF boosts performance 12x over generic datasets, highlighting the dataset's value. Experiments show that YOLOv11+ByteTrack yields the most stable tracks (fewer identity switches) and stronger multi-object accuracy under, while YOLOv11+SAHI increases recall on far-field cans at the cost of lower precision in full-context inputs. Given the mission profile, single-can pickup with approach and grab, YOLOv11 + SAHI proves better for detecting the maximum number of cans. No prior open dataset targets aluminum cans on water from a surface-level viewpoint; this dataset fills this gap and supports reproducible evaluation.

中文摘要翻译: 水面海洋垃圾仍是自主清理的实际瓶颈，小型反光目标（如铝罐）需在强光、波纹和部分淹没条件下从远处被检测到。本文提出了一套ASV视觉系统和一个新的水面铝罐数据集。该数据集包含从视频中提取的约7.3k张原始图像并标注了边界框，通过十种增强类型扩充至约57k张训练/验证图像，覆盖多种光照和水面状态。研究基准测试了一系列专为水面作业设计的检测器与检测-跟踪流水线。在CANSURF上训练YOLOv11相比通用数据集性能提升12倍，凸显了该数据集的价值。实验表明，YOLOv11+ByteTrack产生最稳定的轨迹（更少的身份切换）和更强的多目标精度，而YOLOv11+SAHI在远场铝罐上提高了召回率，但代价是全上下文输入下精度降低。针对单罐拾取（接近并抓取）的任务场景，YOLOv11+SAHI在检测最多铝罐方面表现更优。目前尚无公开数据集以水面视角瞄准水面铝罐检测；本数据集填补了这一空白，并支持可复现的评估。

</details>


### [51] [VolTA-3D: Self-Supervised Learning for Brain MRI using 3D Volumetric Token Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16775)
*Amy Makawana,Abhijeet Parida,Marius George Linguraru,Julia Ive,Syed Muhammad Anwar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Volta-3D，一个自监督3D Vision Transformer框架，通过联合对齐全局类别token和局部patch token（学生-教师范式）并施加细粒度结构重建，学习可迁移的体素表示，在脑MRI的下游任务（海马体分割、性别分类、阿尔茨海默病分类）中优于随机初始化基线，展现更强的跨域泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D脑MRI模型通常针对分割或分类等单一任务专门化，难以跨数据集、成像协议和下游任务泛化，限制了未标记体积数据的临床实用价值。

Method: 采用学生-教师范式的自监督3D Vision Transformer框架，联合对齐全局类别（class）token和局部补丁（patch）token，并强制执行细粒度结构重建损失，实现全局语义一致性与局部结构学习的联合预训练。

Result: 在多个分布外下游任务（海马体分割、性别分类、阿尔茨海默病vs健康对照分类）中，Volta-3D学习的表示显著优于随机初始化基线，表现出更优的可迁移性和对域偏移的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 在预训练阶段联合施加全局语义一致性和局部结构学习，能够从未标记脑MRI数据中学习更广泛的概念，支持多任务下游性能，向泛化性强且临床可行的3D模型迈进一步。

Original Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has advanced medical image analysis be enabling learning form large unlabelled data. However, in brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI), most 3D models remain specialized for either segmentation of classification, limiting their ability to generalize across datasets, imaging protocols,, and downstream tasks. This lack of transferability constrains the clinical utility of 3D MRI models, despite the availability of unlabeled volumetric data. We present Volta-3D, a self-supervised 3D Vision Transformer framework designed to learn transferable volumetric representations. Volta-3D jointly aligns global class-style tokens and local patch tokens within a student-teacher paradigm and enforces fine-grained structural reconstruction. This combined global-local alignment addresses the limited semantic diversity and subtle anatomical characteristics of brain MRI, which challenges existing SSL approaches. We evaluate Volta-3D on multiple out-of-distribution downstream tasks, including hippocampal segmentation and classification of sex and Alzheimer's disease versus healthy controls. Across all tasks, representations learned by Volta-3D outperform randomly initialized baselines, demonstrating improved transferability and robustness under domain shift. Hence jointly enforcing global semantic consistency and local structural learning during pretraining enables broader concept learning from unlabeled brain MRI data. Overall VolTA-3D supports effective multi-task downstream performance with task-specific pertaining, a step towards generalizable and clinically viable 3D models.

中文摘要翻译: 自监督学习（SSL）通过从大量未标记数据中学习，推动了医学图像分析的发展。然而，在脑磁共振成像（MRI）中，大多数3D模型仍然专门用于分割或分类任务，限制了它们跨数据集、成像协议和下游任务的泛化能力。尽管未标记的体积数据可获取，但这种可迁移性的缺乏制约了3D MRI模型的临床实用性。我们提出Volta-3D，一个自监督3D视觉Transformer框架，旨在学习可迁移的体积表示。Volta-3D在学生-教师范式中联合对齐全局类别（class）token和局部补丁（patch）token，并强制执行细粒度结构重建。这种全局-局部联合对齐解决了脑MRI语义多样性有限和解剖特征细微的问题，这些问题对现有SSL方法构成了挑战。我们在多个分布外下游任务上评估Volta-3D，包括海马体分割、性别分类以及阿尔茨海默病与健康对照的分类。在所有任务中，Volta-3D学习的表示均优于随机初始化的基线，展现出在域偏移下更强的可迁移性和鲁棒性。因此，在预训练期间联合施加全局语义一致性和局部结构学习，能够从未标记的脑MRI数据中学习更广泛的概念。总体而言，Volta-3D通过任务特定的预训练支持有效的多任务下游性能，向着泛化性强且临床可行的3D模型迈出了一步。

</details>


### [52] [Accelerating Rectified Flow Models via Trajectory-Aware Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16789)
*Xiao Liu,Kai Liu,Naiyang Guan,Hongliang Lu,Zhixin Wang,Zhikai Chen,Renjing Pei,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TACache是一种无需额外训练的加速框架，通过对整流流轨迹上的速度场进行正交分解（平行分量+正交残差），实现跳过时间步并用历史信息补偿，在文本到图像和视频生成任务上取得显著加速。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有缓存方法通过跳过时间步来加速扩散/整流流模型的采样，但粗粒度的近似在长跳过间隔下会积累误差，导致生成质量下降。需要一种更精细的近似策略来平衡加速比与生成质量。

Method: 提出TACache框架，遵循"先跳过再补偿"范式。首先对整流流轨迹上的离散速度加速度进行正交分解，分为平行分量和正交残差，从而分离每步近似误差的大小和方向来源。离线阶段，基于累积变化阈值在幅度和方向指标上生成跳过调度，并限制跳过间隔长度；在线阶段，在每次跳过的步骤中，结合离线统计与样本的历史正交方向来重构被跳过的速度，无需额外模型评估。

Result: 在BAGEL、FLUX.1-dev和Wan2.1-1.3B上，TACache在文本到图像生成任务上实现最高4.14倍加速，在文本到视频生成任务上实现2.11倍加速，在所有基于参考的保真度指标上均优于先前的缓存方法。

Conclusion: TACache通过轨迹感知的正交分解和跳过-补偿机制，有效缓解了缓存方法在长跳过间隔中的累积误差，实现了更优的加速比与生成质量权衡。

Original Abstract: Diffusion and rectified flow (RF) models generate high-fidelity images and videos, but their iterative velocity-field evaluations are computationally expensive. Existing caching methods accelerate sampling by skipping timesteps, yet their coarse approximations introduce accumulated errors over long skip intervals and degrade quality under aggressive acceleration. We propose TACache (Trajectory-Aware Cache), a training-free acceleration framework following a skip-then-compensate paradigm. TACache performs an orthogonal decomposition of discrete velocity acceleration along the RF trajectory into a parallel component and an orthogonal residual, isolating the magnitude and directional sources of per-step approximation error. The framework operates in two stages: offline, cumulative variation thresholds on the magnitude and direction indicators yield the skip schedule and bound how far each skip interval may extend; online, at each skipped step the offline statistics are combined with the sample's historical orthogonal direction to reconstruct the skipped velocity without additional model evaluations. Experiments on BAGEL, FLUX.1-dev, and Wan2.1-1.3B show that TACache achieves up to 4.14 speedup on text-to-image generation and 2.11 speedup on text-to-video generation, with consistent improvements over prior cache-based methods on all reference-based fidelity metrics. Code will be released soon.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散模型和整流流模型能够生成高保真的图像和视频，但其迭代式的速度场评估计算成本高昂。现有的缓存方法通过跳过时间步来加速采样，但它们的粗粒度近似在长跳过间隔中会引入累积误差，并在激进加速下导致质量下降。我们提出TACache（轨迹感知缓存），一种无需训练的加速框架，遵循"先跳过再补偿"范式。TACache将沿整流流轨迹的离散速度加速度正交分解为平行分量和正交残差，从而分离每步近似误差的幅度和方向来源。该框架分两个阶段运行：离线阶段，基于幅度和方向指标的累积变化阈值生成跳过调度，并限制每个跳过间隔的扩展范围；在线阶段，在每个跳过的步骤中，结合离线统计与样本的历史正交方向来重构被跳过的速度，无需额外的模型评估。在BAGEL、FLUX.1-dev和Wan2.1-1.3B上的实验表明，TACache在文本到图像生成任务上实现最高4.14倍加速，在文本到视频生成任务上实现2.11倍加速，在所有基于参考的保真度指标上均一致优于先前的缓存方法。代码即将开源。

</details>


### [53] [3DPhysVideo: Consistency-Guided Flow SDE for Video Generation via 3D Scene Reconstruction and Physical Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16795)
*Hwidong Kim,Yunho Kim,Tae-Kyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 3DPhysVideo 是一个无需训练的管线，利用现成视频模型将单张图像生成符合物理规律的3D视频。它通过两步法：先用视频模型做新视角合成重建3D场景几何，再基于物理模拟后的点云引导视频模型生成高质量视频，在单GPU上即可运行。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频生成模型常产生违背物理动力学的视觉伪影，而PhysGen3D等物理生成方法在流体建模、多物体交互和逼真度方面仍存在挑战。作者希望弥合从单张图像到物理合理视频之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出训练无关的两阶段管线3DPhysVideo：1) 利用现成I2V视频模型作为新视角合成器，用渲染的点云引导生成360度3D场景几何；2) 对重建几何施加物理求解器进行模拟，用模拟后的点云引导同一I2V模型合成最终视频。提出一致性引导流SDE（Consistency-Guided Flow SDE），将预测速度分解为去噪项和一致性偏置，从而有效约束模型对条件输入的保真度。

Result: 在多物体和流体交互场景的实验中，方法成功实现了从单张图像到物理合理视频的生成，在单张消费级GPU上高效运行。在GPT评分、VideoPhy基准和人工评估上均优于现有SOTA基线。

Conclusion: 3DPhysVideo通过巧妙复用现成视频模型，实现了无需额外训练即可从单图生成物理真实感的视频，有效处理了流体动力学和多物体交互，兼顾了高效性和逼真度。

Original Abstract: Video generative models have made remarkable progress, yet they often yield visual artifacts that violate grounding in physical dynamics. Recent works such as PhysGen3D tackle single image-to-3D physics through mesh reconstruction and Physically-Based Rendering, but challenges remain in modeling fluid dynamics, multi-object interactions and photorealism. This work introduces 3DPhysVideo, a novel training-free pipeline that generates physically realistic videos from a single image. We repurpose an off-the-shelf video model for two stages. First, we use it as a novel view synthesizer to reconstruct complete 360-degree 3D scene geometry by guiding the image-to-video (I2V) flow model with rendered point clouds. Second, after applying physics solvers to this geometry, the physically simulated point cloud is used to guide the same I2V flow model to synthesize final, high-quality videos. Consistency-Guided Flow SDE, which decomposes the predicted velocity of the I2V flow model into denoising and consistency bias, enforces consistency to the conditional inputs, allowing us to effectively repurpose the model for both 3D reconstruction and simulation-guided video generation. In the diverse experiments including multi-objects, and fluid interaction scenes, our method successfully bridges the gap from single-images to physically plausible videos, while remaining efficient to run on a single consumer GPU. It outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on GPT-based scores, VideoPhy benchmark and human evaluation.

中文摘要翻译: 视频生成模型已取得显著进展，但生成的视频常常出现违反物理动力学的视觉伪影。PhysGen3D等近期工作通过网格重建和基于物理的渲染（Physically-Based Rendering）解决了单图像到3D物理的问题，但在流体动力学建模、多物体交互和逼真度方面仍面临挑战。本文提出3DPhysVideo，一种新颖的无需训练（training-free）的管线，能从单张图像生成物理上逼真的视频。我们重新利用现成的视频模型完成两个阶段：首先，将其作为新视角合成器，通过使用渲染的点云引导图像到视频（I2V）流模型，重建完整的360度3D场景几何；其次，在对该几何体施加物理求解器后，用经过物理模拟的点云引导同一个I2V流模型合成最终的高质量视频。本文提出的一致性引导流随机微分方程（Consistency-Guided Flow SDE），将I2V流模型预测的速度分解为去噪分量和一致性偏差，从而强制模型对条件输入保持一致性，使我们能够有效地将模型重新用于3D重建和模拟引导的视频生成。在多物体和流体交互场景的多样化实验中，该方法成功弥合了从单张图像到物理合理视频之间的鸿沟，同时能够在单张消费级GPU上高效运行。该方法在GPT评分、VideoPhy基准和人工评估上均优于现有最先进的基线方法。

</details>


### [54] [EgoKit: Towards Unified Low-Cost Egocentric Data Collection with Heterogeneous Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16797)
*Liuchuan Yu,Erdem Murat,Beichen Wang,Yan Zeng,Tingting Luo,Huizhen Zhou,Shanghao Li,Huining Feng,Zhigen Zhao,Ning Yang,Ke Jing,Yunhao Liu,Ruoya Sheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoKit是一个跨6种异构硬件设备的开源工具箱，统一了第一人称视频（含手腕视角、头部姿态及手部追踪）的录制工作流，解决了碎片化的数据采集问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 不同设备（手机、智能眼镜、XR头显等）之间的SDK、摄像头访问策略和传感器限制各不相同，导致同步采集自我中心视角和手腕视角视频要么绑定单一平台，要么构建一次性硬件方案，缺乏跨设备可迁移的统一工具。

Method: 设计了一套通用的录制工作流，兼容6种异构设备；在XR头显上额外同步记录头部姿态和OpenXR标准的26关节手部追踪数据；配套提供两个手腕摄像头、头带和USB-C集线器等配件，无需定制硬件即可为任意支持设备添加手腕视角采集功能。

Result: 所有支持设备上均提供统一的录制交互和本地存储的视频及日志格式；XR头显上实现了视频流与头部姿态、26关节手部追踪的对齐记录。

Conclusion: EgoKit填补了跨设备第一人称视频采集工具的空白，为机器人学习、活动理解和具身AI研究提供了一个可迁移、低门槛的统一数据采集方案。

Original Abstract: Egocentric video is increasingly used as a data source for robot learning, activity understanding, and embodied AI research, but collecting it at scale remains fragmented in practice: each candidate host device, such as an Android phone, iPhone, iPad, smart glasses, or extended reality (XR) headset, exposes a different SDK, a different policy on raw camera access, and different limitations on external USB cameras and on-device tracking. Synchronized ego-view and wrist-view capture is therefore typically obtained by either committing to a single proprietary platform or building one-off rigs that do not transfer across devices. To address this gap, we present EgoKit, a toolkit that exposes the same egocentric recording workflow across six heterogeneous host devices. Across all supported devices, EgoKit presents the same recording interaction and produces locally stored video with a uniform log format; on XR headsets, it additionally logs head pose and OpenXR-standard 26-joint hand tracking aligned to the video streams. The companion accessories, including two wrist cameras with mounts, a head strap, and a USB-C hub, add wrist-view capture to any supported host without custom hardware fabrication. EgoKit is available at \url{https://egokit.chuange.org/}.

中文摘要翻译: 第一人称视频日益被用作机器人学习、活动理解和具身AI研究的数据来源，但在大规模采集时仍面临碎片化问题：每款候选宿主设备（如Android手机、iPhone、iPad、智能眼镜或扩展现实头显）都暴露不同的SDK、不同的原始摄像头访问策略，以及对外接USB摄像头和机载追踪的不同限制。因此，同步采集自我中心视角和手腕视角视频通常要么绑定单一专有平台，要么构建无法跨设备迁移的一次性硬件装置。为填补这一空白，我们提出了EgoKit，一个在六种异构宿主设备上暴露统一的第一人称录制工作流的工具箱。在所有支持的设备上，EgoKit呈现相同的录制交互方式，并生成以统一日志格式存储的本地视频；在XR头显上，它还额外记录与视频流对齐的头部姿态和OpenXR标准的26关节手部追踪数据。配套的配件（包括两个带支架的手腕摄像头、一条头带和一个USB-C集线器）可在无需定制硬件制造的情况下，为任何支持的宿主设备添加手腕视角采集功能。EgoKit的开源代码和文档可在\url{https://egokit.chuange.org/}获取。

</details>


### [55] [NeuroLiDAR: Adaptive Frame Rate Depth Sensing via Neuromorphic Event-LiDAR Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16805)
*Darshana Rathnayake,Dulanga Weerakoon,Meera Radhakrishnan,Archan Misra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NeuroLiDAR通过融合稀疏激光雷达数据与神经形态事件相机数据，实现了自适应的高帧率深度感知，将有效帧率提升至约66 Hz，并将深度重建误差降低约29%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统激光雷达受硬件限制，在探测距离、空间分辨率和帧率之间存在权衡，通常以低帧率（5-10 Hz）运行以优先保证远距离感知，难以快速响应场景中的剧烈变化。

Method: NeuroLiDAR融合时间稀疏的激光雷达数据和时间密集的神经形态事件相机数据，包含两个核心组件：基于事件的關鍵帧检测和事件引导的深度外推，从而根据场景动态变化自适应调整感知帧率。

Result: 在自建数据集ELiDAR（涵盖室外和室内场景）上，NeuroLiDAR将深度重建误差（RMSE）降低约29%，同时实现了27.8-47.3 Hz的自适应帧率。

Conclusion: 通过融合激光雷达与事件相机，NeuroLiDAR能够在不牺牲感知范围的前提下实现高帧率深度感知，有效提升系统对动态场景的响应能力。

Original Abstract: LiDARs are widely used for 3D depth reconstruction, but their performance is often limited by inherent hardware constraints that impose trade-offs between range, spatial resolution, and frame rate. Many LiDAR systems typically operate at low frame rates (e.g., 5-10 Hz), prioritizing long-range sensing over responsiveness to rapid scene changes. We present NeuroLiDAR, an adaptive depth sensing framework that achieves effective frame rates of up to $\approx$66 Hz by fusing temporally sparse LiDAR data with temporally dense inputs from neuromorphic event cameras. NeuroLiDAR integrates two components: event-based keyframe detection and event-guided depth extrapolation, to dynamically adjust the sensing rate in response to scene dynamics. To evaluate our approach, we introduce ELiDAR, a dataset spanning outdoor and indoor scenarios, and show that NeuroLiDAR reduces depth reconstruction error by $\approx$29\% in RMSE while achieving adaptive frame rates between 27.8-47.3 Hz. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/darshanakgr/neurolidar.

中文摘要翻译: 激光雷达被广泛用于3D深度重建，但其性能常受限于固有的硬件约束，需要在探测距离、空间分辨率和帧率之间进行权衡。许多激光雷达系统通常以低帧率（如5-10 Hz）运行，优先保证远距离感知而非对快速场景变化的响应。我们提出NeuroLiDAR，一种自适应深度感知框架，通过融合时间稀疏的激光雷达数据与时间密集的神经形态事件相机输入，实现高达约66 Hz的有效帧率。NeuroLiDAR集成了两个组件：基于事件的关键帧检测和事件引导的深度外推，从而根据场景动态自适应调整感知帧率。为评估我们的方法，我们引入了ELiDAR数据集，涵盖室外和室内场景，实验表明NeuroLiDAR在实现27.8-47.3 Hz自适应帧率的同时，将深度重建误差（RMSE）降低约29%。我们的代码和数据集已在https://github.com/darshanakgr/neurolidar上开源。

</details>


### [56] [DecoRec: Decomposed 3D Scene Reconstruction from Single-View Images via Object-Level Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16807)
*Yuhan Ping,Yuan Liu,Xiaoxiao Long,Peng Wang,Junhui Hou,Jianyi Zheng,Jia Pan,Xin Li,Cheng Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DecoRec利用扩散模型从单视图2D图像重建分解的3D场景网格，通过单独重建物体并融合优化，实现高质量几何与外观重建。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有单视图场景重建方法依赖物体检索或粗糙的3D体素/表面回归，导致外观和几何不准确，且缺乏大规模高质量场景级数据集，难以直接生成3D场景。

Method: 采用基于扩散模型的单视图物体重建方法分别重建各个物体，再通过可微渲染和扩散引导的精炼流程有效合并重构的物体，优化外观和几何。

Result: DecoRec在几何重建和新视角合成方面实现了高质量的单视图场景重建，对室内设计等下游应用有显著益处。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了单视图3D场景重建的难题，通过分解-合并策略实现了高质量的场景级重建。

Original Abstract: In this paper, we introduce \textit{DecoRec}, a novel system designed to elevate single-view 2D images to a decomposed 3D scene mesh. Current methods for single-view scene reconstruction typically rely on object retrieval or the regression of coarse 3D voxels or surfaces, leading to inaccuracies in capturing the appearance and geometry of the input image. The lack of high-quality large-scale scene-level datasets further complicates direct 3D scene generation from single-view images. To achieve high-quality 3D scene generation from a single-view image, DecoRec takes advantage of recent diffusion-based single-view object reconstruction methods to reconstruct individual objects separately. Subsequently, a refinement pipeline is proposed to effectively merge these reconstructed objects, enhancing appearance and geometry through a differentiable rendering technique and diffusion-guided refinement. Our results demonstrate that DecoRec facilitates high-quality single-view scene reconstruction in both geometry and novel synthesis, offering significant benefits for downstream applications like room interior design.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出DecoRec，一种将单视图2D图像提升为分解的3D场景网格的新系统。当前的单视图场景重建方法通常依赖于物体检索或回归粗粒度的3D体素或表面，导致输入图像的外观和几何捕捉不准确。缺乏高质量的大规模场景级数据集进一步增加了从单视图图像直接生成3D场景的难度。为了从单视图图像实现高质量的3D场景生成，DecoRec利用近期基于扩散的单视图物体重建方法分别重构各个物体。随后，提出一种精炼流程，通过可微渲染技术和扩散引导的精炼有效合并这些重构的物体，增强外观和几何。我们的结果表明，DecoRec在几何重建和新视角合成方面实现了高质量的单视图场景重建，为室内设计等下游应用提供了显著益处。

</details>


### [57] [Coarse Semantic Injection for LLM-Conditioned Structured Indoor Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16832)
*Shuliang Zhu,Tomiwa Adey,Jinjia Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向大语言模型条件化3D室内理解的结构化解码的语义增强方法，通过RGBB语义着色和路由语义偏移模块，在不改变下游解码器的情况下提升对薄壁结构、门窗和家具实例的检测性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LLM作为结构化解码器处理3D点云时，点云编码器在体素化和稀疏池化后容易丢失门窗等薄壁结构元素，且在杂乱场景中可能遗漏单个家具实例，因此需要一种能保留语义信息的增强方法。

Method: 提出RGBB点接口语义增强方法：将点云语义证据简化为四组代码（家具、墙壁、开口、其他），编码为RGB三通道中的四种颜色状态；并引入轻量级路由语义偏移模块（训练时使用辅助头进行比例/预算正则化），在稀疏池化后增强语义线索。整个流程无需改变下游LLM解码器和输出序列化方式。

Result: 在Structured3D、SpatialLM数据集和ARKitScenes上，该方法在开口定位和杂乱场景中逐实例家具检测上指标均有提升，消融实验验证了语义来源、颜色编码、token融合和偏移注入的作用。

Conclusion: RGBB语义增强方法能有效提升大语言模型对3D室内场景的理解能力，尤其对薄壁结构和家具实例的检测效果显著，且颜色/熵的影响不可忽视。

Original Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently been used as structured decoders for indoor understanding from 3D point-token inputs. However, point cloud encoders often under-represent thin structural elements such as doors and windows after voxelization and sparse pooling, and may miss individual furniture instances in cluttered scenes. We propose an interface-preserving semantic augmentation for LLM-conditioned structured decoding. The key idea is to associate semantic evidence with the point-cloud representation, reduce it to a coarse four-group code (furniture, walls, openings, and others), and encode it as an RGBB point interface: red for furniture, green for walls, blue for openings, and black for others, where RGBB denotes four semantic color states represented in three RGB channels rather than an additional fourth channel. This semantic color code is appended to the original raw point attributes before tokenization, so geometry and semantics share the same sparse tokenization path while the downstream language model decoder and output serialization remain unchanged. We further introduce a lightweight routed semantic shift module, with an auxiliary head used only for training-time ratio/budget regularization and analysis, to strengthen semantic cues after sparse pooling. The overall pipeline can use RGB-derived semantic evidence. Under these controlled semantic-source settings, the reported metrics improve across Structured3D, the SpatialLM dataset, and ARKitScenes, especially for opening localization and per-instance furniture detection in cluttered scenes. Ablations clarify the roles of semantic source, color coding, token fusion, and shift injection, while also showing that color/entropy effects remain nontrivial.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型（LLMs）近期已被用作从3D点云token输入进行室内理解的结构化解码器。然而，点云编码器在体素化和稀疏池化后经常欠表示门窗等薄壁结构元素，并可能在杂乱场景中遗漏单个家具实例。我们提出了一种面向LLM条件化结构化解码的界面保持语义增强方法。其核心思想是将语义证据与点云表示关联，将其简化为粗略的四组代码（家具、墙壁、开口和其他），并将其编码为RGBB点接口：红色表示家具，绿色表示墙壁，蓝色表示开口，黑色表示其他，其中RGBB表示用三个RGB通道表示的四种语义颜色状态，而非额外的第四通道。该语义颜色编码在token化之前附加到原始点属性上，使得几何和语义共享相同的稀疏token化路径，同时下游语言模型解码器和输出序列化保持不变。我们进一步引入了一个轻量级的路由语义偏移模块，该模块带有一个仅在训练时用于比例/预算正则化和分析的辅助头，以增强稀疏池化后的语义线索。整个流程可以使用RGB衍生的语义证据。在这些受控语义来源设置下，报告指标在Structured3D、SpatialLM数据集和ARKitScenes上均有提升，尤其是在杂乱场景中的开口定位和逐实例家具检测方面。消融实验阐明了语义来源、颜色编码、token融合和偏移注入的作用，同时也表明颜色/熵效应仍然不可忽视。

</details>


### [58] [Learning Relative Representations for Fine-Grained Multimodal Alignment with Limited Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16834)
*Shiwon Kim,Yu Rang Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于相对表示学习token级跨模态结构的后对齐方法，在少量配对数据下显著优于现有方法，强调了细粒度跨模态结构建模的重要性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有后对齐方法主要关注全局表征对齐，忽略了patch-token关系，限制了需要细粒度跨模态匹配的任务（如零样本分割）的性能。

Method: 通过每个模态空间中的一组可学习锚点，基于token级相似度来表示图像和文本，训练这些锚点以诱导匹配对之间一致的跨模态相似度模式，无需重型投影层。

Result: 在零样本分类、跨模态检索和零样本分割任务上，该方法以显著优势持续超越现有方法。

Conclusion: 在少量配对数据下，建模细粒度跨模态结构对于有效的后对齐多模态学习至关重要。

Original Abstract: Multimodal pre-training demonstrates strong generalization performance, but this paradigm is often impractical in domains where paired data are scarce. A promising alternative is post-hoc multimodal alignment, which aligns separately pre-trained unimodal encoders using a limited number of paired examples. However, existing methods focus primarily on aligning global representations, missing patch-token relations. This may hinder transfer to tasks that require fine-grained cross-modal matching beyond coarse sample-level semantics. To address this issue, we propose a post-hoc alignment method that learns token-level cross-modal structure using relative representations. Specifically, we represent images and texts through their token-level similarities to a set of learnable anchors in each modality space, which are trained to induce consistent cross-modal similarity patterns for matched pairs. Despite learning only the anchors without heavy projection layers, our approach consistently outperforms existing methods in zero-shot classification, cross-modal retrieval, and zero-shot segmentation by a substantial margin. This highlights the importance of modeling fine-grained cross-modal structure for effective post-hoc multimodal alignment with limited paired data.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态预训练展现出强大的泛化性能，但在配对数据稀缺的领域，这种范式往往不切实际。一种有前景的替代方案是事后多模态对齐，即利用有限的配对样本分别对齐各自预训练的单模态编码器。然而，现有方法主要关注全局表征的对齐，忽略了patch-token关系。这可能阻碍向需要超出粗粒度样本级语义的细粒度跨模态匹配任务的迁移。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种事后对齐方法，利用相对表示学习token级的跨模态结构。具体而言，我们通过每个模态空间中与一组可学习锚点的token级相似度来表示图像和文本，这些锚点经过训练以在匹配对上诱导出一致的跨模态相似度模式。尽管仅学习锚点而未使用重型投影层，我们的方法在零样本分类、跨模态检索和零样本分割任务上以显著优势持续超越现有方法。这突显了在有限配对数据下，建模细粒度跨模态结构对于有效的事后多模态对齐的重要性。

</details>


### [59] [Thinking with Patterns: Breaking the Perceptual Bottleneck in Visual Planning via Pattern Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16848)
*Yichang Jian,Boyuan Xiao,Zhenyuan Huang,Yifei Peng,Yao-Xiang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉思维（TWI）的训练免费规划策略，通过逐步构建内部世界模型来增强VLM在复杂视觉规划任务中的能力，并提出了Pattern Inference和Pattern Induction两种策略来平衡准确性与计算效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前VLM在复杂视觉输入下的规划能力受限于其单步感知能力，尽管TWI能力已得到训练，但在规划领域仍存在感知瓶颈。

Method: 将TWI作为逐步构建和反思内部世界模型的工具，提出Pattern Inference（让VLM主动识别已知视觉模式并推断局部世界模型结构）和Pattern Induction（在线归纳学习策略，将视觉模式视为可组合可复用的专家，自动发现并优化经验模式）。

Result: 在FrozenLake、Crafter和CubeBench实验表明，该方法在准确性和效率之间取得了理想的平衡。

Conclusion: 训练免费的TWI规划策略能显著提升VLM解决超出其初始能力范围的任务，但需控制TWI操作数量以避免过高计算开销，所提模式推断与归纳策略有效提升了效率。

Original Abstract: Planning from raw visual input remains a significant challenge for current Vision-Language Models (VLMs), when the complexity of input is beyond their one-step perception capability. Motivated by recent advances in Thinking with Images (TWI), a reasonable solution is to decompose the perception process into simpler steps by iteratively acquiring and incorporating local visual evidence. However, even though current VLMs are well-trained in general TWI ability, their perceptual bottleneck in the planning domain remains. To tackle this challenge, we formulate TWI as a tool to gradually build and reflect an accurate internal world model. We find that the resulting training-free planning strategy enables VLMs to solve tasks that are far beyond their initial capabilities, at the cost that too many TWI operations would significantly increase the computational overhead. To further improve efficiency, we propose Pattern Inference, a novel TWI strategy enabling VLMs to actively recognize known visual patterns in the new tasks and directly infer local world model structures. To obtain these patterns, we propose Pattern Induction, an online inductive learning strategy treating visual patterns as composite and reusable experts, which are autonomously discovered and optimized from experience. Experimental evaluations in FrozenLake, Crafter and CubeBench domains show that our approaches achieve a desirable balance between accuracy and efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 从原始视觉输入进行规划对于当前的视觉-语言模型（VLM）仍然是一个重大挑战，当输入的复杂性超出其单步感知能力时尤为如此。受近期"用图像思考"（Thinking with Images, TWI）进展的启发，一个合理的解决方案是通过迭代获取并整合局部视觉证据，将感知过程分解为更简单的步骤。然而，尽管当前的VLM在通用TWI能力上训练良好，它们在规划领域的感知瓶颈依然存在。为应对这一挑战，我们将TWI构建为一种逐步建立并反思准确内部世界模型的工具。我们发现，由此产生的无需训练的规划策略使VLM能够解决远超其初始能力的任务，但代价是过多的TWI操作会显著增加计算开销。为进一步提升效率，我们提出了模式推理（Pattern Inference）——一种新的TWI策略，使VLM能够主动识别新任务中已知的视觉模式，并直接推断局部世界模型结构。为获取这些模式，我们提出了模式归纳（Pattern Induction）——一种在线归纳学习策略，将视觉模式视为可组合且可复用的专家，从经验中自主发现并优化这些模式。在FrozenLake、Crafter和CubeBench领域的实验评估表明，我们的方法在准确性和效率之间实现了理想的平衡。

</details>


### [60] [VGGT-CD: Training-Free Robust Registration for 3D Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16859)
*Wei Zhang,Songhua Li,Yihang Wu,Qiang Li,Qi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VGGT-CD是一种无需训练的3D变化检测管线，通过粗-细两阶段跨时间配准策略，利用视觉几何基础模型VGGT从无位姿多视角图像生成稠密点云，显著降低了配准误差并生成了高纯度的3D变化图。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D变化检测方法多基于2D域，视点变化易被误判为物理变化，且缺乏深度信息；独立按周期重建时存在尺度模糊、配准-变化悖论（场景变化干扰配准）以及边缘飞点噪声等根本性障碍。

Method: 提出VGGT-CD，一种无需训练的管线：粗阶段通过稀疏关键帧联合推理建立统一度量空间并得到初始Sim(3)先验；细阶段通过隔离静态背景对应关系纯化稠密重建，利用闭式质心对齐锁定尺度和旋转并优化平移，辅以残差自检机制数学保证非退化。

Result: 在World Across Time数据集的11场景基准上，VGGT-CD将室外绝对轨迹误差降低44%，室内降低59%；配准速度提升6倍以上，无需任务特定训练即可生成高纯度3D变化图。

Conclusion: VGGT-CD通过解耦跨时间配准与动态变化干扰，在不依赖训练的情况下有效解决了视角变化误判和深度缺失问题，实现了高效、高精度的3D变化检测。

Original Abstract: 3D change detection from multi-view images is essential for urban monitoring, disaster assessment, and autonomous driving. However, existing methods predominantly operate in the 2D domain, where viewpoint variations are mistaken for physical changes and depth is unavailable. While visual geometry foundation models like VGGT rapidly produce dense point clouds from unposed images, independent per-epoch reconstruction encounters fundamental obstacles: unpredictable inter-epoch scale ambiguity, registration-change paradox where scene changes corrupt alignment, and pervasive edge-flying noise. To address these challenges, we present VGGT-CD, a training-free pipeline decoupling cross-temporal registration from dynamic-change interference. In the Coarse Stage, sparse keyframe joint inference establishes a unified metric space and yields an initial Sim(3) prior. In the Fine Stage, dense reconstructions are purified by isolating static-background correspondences. A closed-form centroid alignment refines the translation while locking scale and rotation, using a residual self-check to mathematically guarantee non-degradation. Evaluated on an 11-scene benchmark from the World Across Time dataset, VGGT-CD reduces Absolute Trajectory Error by 44% outdoors and 59% indoors. It completes registration over 6 times faster, producing high-purity 3D change maps without task-specific training.

中文摘要翻译: 从多视角图像中进行3D变化检测对于城市监测、灾害评估和自动驾驶至关重要。然而，现有方法主要在2D域中运行，导致视点变化被误判为物理变化且深度信息不可用。虽然像VGGT这样的视觉几何基础模型可以从无位姿图像快速生成稠密点云，但独立按周期重建面临根本性障碍：周期间不可预测的尺度模糊、配准-变化悖论（场景变化破坏对齐）以及普遍的边缘飞点噪声。为解决这些挑战，我们提出了VGGT-CD，一种无需训练的管线，将跨时间配准与动态变化干扰解耦。在粗阶段，稀疏关键帧联合推理建立统一的度量空间并得到初始Sim(3)先验。在细阶段，通过隔离静态背景对应关系纯化稠密重建。闭式质心对齐在锁定尺度和旋转的同时优化平移，并利用残差自检机制从数学上保证非退化。在World Across Time数据集的11场景基准上评估，VGGT-CD将室外绝对轨迹误差降低44%，室内降低59%。其配准速度提升6倍以上，无需任务特定训练即可生成高纯度3D变化图。

</details>


### [61] [Prefix-Adaptive Block Diffusion for Efficient Document Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16861)
*Mingxu Chai,Ziyu Shen,Chenyu Liu,Kaidi Zhang,Jiazheng Zhang,Dingwei Zhu,Zhiheng Xi,Ruoyu Chen,Jun Long,Jihua Kang,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PA-BDM通过前缀因果去噪和动态KV缓存提交机制，解决了块扩散模型中固定块边界导致的并行度下降和缓存效率低的问题，在文档解析任务上取得了更高精度和71.6%的吞吐量提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
