Motivation: 现有块扩散模型（BDM）存在三个问题：1）块内去噪时并行度降低；2）生成的token在整块完成前无法缓存；3）块内双向去噪与块间自回归产生信息流不一致，影响结构敏感型识别任务。

Method: 提出PA-BDM，包含三个关键技术：1）将块内双向去噪替换为从前缀到后缀的因果去噪；2）置信门控结构损失（CSL）用于构建低熵前缀；3）渐进式前缀提交（PPC）在推理时动态将最长可靠前缀提交到KV缓存并重置候选范围，恢复大规模并行解码空间。

Result: 3B参数的PA-BDM在多个基准上获得更高识别分数，相比2.5B的MinerU-Diffusion，推理吞吐量提升71.6%。

Conclusion: PA-BDM通过重新设计去噪方向和缓存提交机制，有效解决了传统BDM的并行度和缓存效率瓶颈，在文档解析任务上实现了性能和效率的双重提升。

Original Abstract: Block Diffusion Models (BDMs) support parallel generation, flexible-length output, and KV caching, making them promising for efficient document parsing. However, existing BDMs bind denoising and cache commitment to fixed block boundaries: parallelism shrinks during intra-block denoising, while generated tokens cannot be cached until the whole block is completed. Moreover, intra-block bidirectional denoising conflicts with inter-block autoregression, creating inconsistent information flow that can challenge structure-sensitive recognition. We propose the Prefix-Adaptive Block Diffusion Model (PA-BDM), which replaces intra-block bidirectional denoising with causal denoising from prefix to suffix and treats the block size as a maximum candidate range rather than a fixed commitment unit. PA-BDM uses Confidence-gated Structural Loss (CSL) to build low-entropy prefixes before extending training to longer continuations. During inference, Progressive Prefix Commitment (PPC) then dynamically commits the longest reliable prefix into the KV cache and resets the next candidate range from the updated prefix, restoring a large parallel decoding space at each step. Experiments show that the 3B PA-BDM achieves higher recognition scores on several benchmarks and improves inference throughput by 71.6\% over the 2.5B MinerU-Diffusion.

中文摘要翻译: 块扩散模型（BDM）支持并行生成、可变长度输出和KV缓存，使其在高效文档解析方面具有前景。然而，现有BDM将去噪和缓存提交绑定到固定的块边界上：块内去噪时并行度缩小，且生成的token在整块完成前无法缓存。此外，块内双向去噪与块间自回归产生冲突，造成不一致的信息流，可能挑战对结构敏感识别任务。我们提出了前缀自适应块扩散模型（PA-BDM），它将块内双向去噪替换为从前缀到后缀的因果去噪，并将块大小视为最大候选范围而非固定提交单元。PA-BDM使用置信门控结构损失（CSL）在扩展训练到更长续写前构建低熵前缀。在推理阶段，渐进式前缀提交（PPC）动态地将最长可靠前缀提交到KV缓存中，并从更新后的前缀重置下一个候选范围，从而在每一步恢复大规模并行解码空间。实验表明，3B参数的PA-BDM在多个基准上取得了更高的识别分数，并且相比2.5B的MinerU-Diffusion，推理吞吐量提升了71.6%。

</details>


### [62] [Metric-Guided Feature Fusion of Visual Foundation Models for Segmentation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16864)
*Yachan Guo,JoseLuis Gomez Zurita,Danna Xue,Yi Xiao,AntonioManuel Lopez Pena*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无标签度量引导的多视觉基础模型特征融合方法，通过结构一致性和边缘保真度两套指标评估不同VFM编码器，选择互补的强弱特征对进行主从融合，提升密集预测任务的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 不同视觉基础模型（如SAM2和DINOv3）在表示上存在互补性偏差——有的擅长细粒度边缘，有的擅长物体级结构，但简单融合多个VFM特征往往效果不佳，且缺乏可解释的融合原则。

Method: 设计两套无标签度量指标（结构一致性SC和边缘保真度EF），在特征空间中评估VFM编码器的特性；根据度量分数识别互补的"强边缘-强结构"编码器对，采用主从融合方案进行集成，无需复杂架构修改，单阶段训练。

Result: 在多个密集预测任务上相比基线方法取得一致性能提升，物体级语义更准确，边界定位更精确。

Conclusion: 本文提出的度量引导的多VFM特征融合方法有效、可解释，且无需复杂架构修改，能够可靠地提升密集预测任务性能。

Original Abstract: Although large-scale visual foundation models (VFMs) achieve remarkable performance in semantic understanding, they still underperform in instance-aware dense prediction tasks. They exhibit different biases in representation: for instance, promptable segmentation models (e.g., SAM2) focus on fine-grained region boundaries, while self-supervised models (e.g., DINOv3) emphasize object-level structure. This observation highlights the potential of combining complementary features from different VFMs to enhance downstream dense prediction tasks. However, naive multi-VFM fusion seldom leads to reliable gains, and interpretable principles for leveraging their complementary features are still underexplored. In this work, we propose a metric-guided approach that effectively selects and aggregates complementary features from different VFMs based on explicit assessment scores. Specifically, we design a suite of label-free metrics in feature space across two aspects, Structural Coherence and Edge Fidelity, to assess features of VFM encoders. Guided by these scores, we identify complementary edge-strong and structure-strong encoder pairs, and integrate them via a master-auxiliary fusion scheme. This feature fusion requires no complex architectural changes and is trained only in a single stage. Our model shows consistent performance gains across multiple dense prediction tasks compared with the baselines, with better object-level semantics and more accurately localized boundaries. The code is available at {https://github.com/gyc-code/metric-guided-fusion}.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管大规模视觉基础模型（VFMs）在语义理解方面取得了显著性能，但在实例感知的密集预测任务中仍然表现不佳。它们在表示上表现出不同的偏差：例如，可提示分割模型（如SAM2）关注细粒度的区域边界，而自监督模型（如DINOv3）则强调物体级结构。这一观察突显了融合不同VFM的互补特征以提升下游密集预测任务的潜力。然而，朴素的多元VFM融合很少能带来可靠的性能提升，且利用其互补特征的可解释原则仍未得到充分探索。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种度量引导的方法，基于显式的评估分数有效选择和聚合来自不同VFM的互补特征。具体来说，我们在特征空间中设计了一套无标签度量指标，涵盖结构一致性和边缘保真度两个方面，用于评估VFM编码器的特征。在这些分数引导下，我们识别出互补的强边缘与强结构编码器对，并通过主从融合方案进行集成。这种特征融合无需复杂的架构改动，且仅需单阶段训练。与基线方法相比，我们的模型在多个密集预测任务上取得了一致的性能提升，具有更优的物体级语义和更精确的边界定位。代码已开源：https://github.com/gyc-code/metric-guided-fusion。

</details>


### [63] [Towards Generalized Image Manipulation Localization via Score-based Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16879)
*Yunfei Wang,Bo Du,Zhe Yang,Xin Liu,Zhiyu Lin,Tianxin Xu,Ji-Zhe Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffIML框架，利用基于分数的生成式建模（扩散模型）替代传统判别式方法进行图像篡改定位，通过学习掩码分布的分数函数（对数似然梯度）来捕获内在几何拓扑结构，并针对效率与稳定性进行了两项关键优化，在多个基准上显著超越了现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像篡改定位方法多为判别式模型，学习固定的决策边界，容易过拟合训练集中的特定篡改痕迹，难以泛化到未见过的篡改类型。因此需要一种能捕获掩码分布内在结构、更具鲁棒性的方法。

Method: DiffIML利用扩散模型作为分数函数的数值求解器，通过估计对数似然梯度来学习掩码分布的固有几何拓扑。为提升实用性，设计了两个关键改进：(1) 轻量级掩码专用VAE用于快速潜空间处理，配合解耦架构的轻量去噪UNet；(2) 边缘监督与误差先验以缓解采样过程中的误差累积。

Result: 在两种协议下对八个非生成式基准和三个生成式基准进行了大量实验，结果表明DiffIML一致性地优于现有最先进方法，在多种未见数据集上展现出显著的泛化能力提升。

Conclusion: 基于分数的生成式建模为图像篡改定位提供了一种有效的新范式，通过捕获掩码分布的几何拓扑结构而非学习硬边界，显著提升了泛化性能；所提出的效率与稳定性优化方案使其具备实际应用价值。

Original Abstract: With the rapid evolution of synthetic media, Image Manipulation Localization (IML) has emerged as a critical component in multimedia forensics for ensuring the integrity of digital content. However, generalization remains a core challenge, as existing discriminative methods typically learn a fixed decision boundary that tends to overfit to specific training artifacts and fails to adapt to unseen manipulation types. To address this, we propose DiffIML, a novel framework that introduces score-based generative modeling to IML. Diverging from the direct estimation of hard boundaries, DiffIML approximates the score function, the gradient of the log-likelihood, to capture the intrinsic geometric topology of mask distributions. This paradigm leverages structural priors to iteratively recover coherent masks from noise, thereby circumventing the brittleness associated with discriminative models. Under this formulation, diffusion models serve as an effective numerical solver for the learned score function.To ensure practicality, we respectively resolve the efficiency and stability bottlenecks of standard diffusion by: (1) utilizing a Lightweight Mask-Specific VAE for fast latent-space process and a decoupled architecture with a lightweight denoising UNet, (2) edge supervision and error prior to mitigate error accumulation during sampling. Extensive experiments of two distinct protocols on eight non-generative and three generative benchmarks demonstrate that DiffIML consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, yielding remarkable generalization improvements on diverse unseen datasets. The code will be publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: 随着合成媒体的快速发展，图像篡改定位（IML）已成为多媒体取证中确保数字内容完整性的关键组成部分。然而，泛化能力仍然是一个核心挑战，因为现有的判别式方法通常学习固定的决策边界，容易过拟合特定的训练痕迹，且难以适应未见过的篡改类型。为解决这一问题，我们提出了DiffIML，一种将基于分数的生成式建模引入IML的新框架。与直接估计硬边界不同，DiffIML近似分数函数（对数似然的梯度），以捕获掩码分布的内在几何拓扑结构。该范式利用结构先验从噪声中迭代恢复连贯的掩码，从而规避了判别式模型的脆弱性。在此框架下，扩散模型作为所学分数函数的有效数值求解器。为确保实用性，我们分别解决了标准扩散过程的效率与稳定性瓶颈：(1) 使用轻量级掩码专用VAE实现快速潜空间处理，并结合解耦架构的轻量去噪UNet；(2) 引入边缘监督与误差先验以缓解采样过程中的误差累积。在两种不同协议下对八个非生成式基准和三个生成式基准进行的大量实验表明，DiffIML一致性地优于现有最先进方法，在多种未见数据集上展现出显著的泛化能力提升。代码将公开提供。

</details>


### [64] [Controlling Decision Drift in Multimodal Sentiment Analysis with Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16889)
*Chenglizhao Chen,Yuchen Cao,Xinyu Liu,Mengke Song,Guisheng Zhang,Xiaomin Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种两级参考对齐框架，通过特征表示和情感决策层面的稳定参考，解决多模态情感分析中模态缺失和质量不平衡问题，在CMU-MOSI和CMU-MOSEI上取得SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法利用可用模态生成缺失模态特征，但不同模态的表达机制和情感动态差异会导致生成特征偏离真实分布并误导预测；同时，不可靠模态会主导融合过程，导致跨模态组合的表示偏移和情感表示不稳定。

Method: 提出两级参考对齐框架：第一级利用完整模态样本约束表示，将不同模态组合对齐到共享情感空间；第二级通过原型检索和投票抑制不可靠模态，在决策层强制跨模态一致性。

Result: 在CMU-MOSI和CMU-MOSEI上，多种缺失模态设置下均表现一致提升；全模态输入下ACC分别达86.28%和85.88%，F1分别达86.24%和85.86%，达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 该框架通过在表示层和决策层引入稳定参考，有效缓解了模态缺失导致的特征偏移和表示不稳定问题，在多种缺失模态模式下均能保持稳定可靠的情感预测。

Original Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis relies on textual, acoustic, and visual signals, yet real-world data often suffer from modality missing and quality imbalance. Existing methods generate features for modality missing from available ones, but differences in expression mechanisms and sentiment dynamics across modalities may cause the generated features to deviate from true distributions and mislead prediction. In addition, unreliable modalities may dominate fusion, resulting in representation shift across modality combinations and unstable sentiment representations. To address these challenges, we propose a two-level reference alignment framework. The framework introduces stable references at the feature representation and sentiment decision levels to improve robustness under modality missing. First-level reference alignment leverages complete-modality samples to constrain representations and align different modality combinations into a shared sentiment space. Second-level reference alignment enforces cross-modal consistency at the decision level by suppressing unreliable modalities through prototype retrieval and voting. As a result, the framework maintains stable and reliable sentiment predictions under diverse missing-modality patterns. Experiments on CMU-MOSI and CMU-MOSEI show consistent improvements across various missing-modality settings. Under full-modality input, the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance, with ACC of 86.28% and 85.88%, and F1 of 86.24% and 85.86%.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态情感分析依赖于文本、声学和视觉信号，但真实世界的数据常常面临模态缺失和质量不平衡的问题。现有方法利用可用模态生成缺失模态的特征，然而不同模态之间的表达机制和情感动态差异可能导致生成的特征偏离真实分布并误导预测。此外，不可靠的模态可能主导融合过程，导致跨模态组合的表示偏移和不稳定的情感表示。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种两级参考对齐框架。该框架在特征表示和情感决策层面引入稳定参考，以提高模态缺失下的鲁棒性。第一级参考对齐利用完整模态样本约束表示，将不同的模态组合对齐到共享的情感空间。第二级参考对齐通过原型检索和投票抑制不可靠模态，在决策层面强制跨模态一致性。实验结果表明，该框架在多种缺失模态模式下能够保持稳定可靠的情感预测。在CMU-MOSI和CMU-MOSEI数据集上的实验显示，该框架在各种缺失模态设置下均取得一致改进。在全模态输入下，该方法达到了最先进的性能，ACC分别为86.28%和85.88%，F1分别为86.24%和85.86%。

</details>


### [65] [DriveSafe: A Framework for Risk Detection and Safety Suggestions in Driving Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16892)
*Sainithin Artham,Shankar Gangisetty,Avijit Dasgupta,C. V. Jawahar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveSafe 是一个通过结构化自然语言描述增强自动驾驶风险感知的框架。它首先生成包含运动、空间和深度信息的多模态空间定位描述，然后利用这些描述进行下游风险评估（识别危险物体、位置及不安全行为），并给出安全建议。通过描述-风险配对微调轻量级适配器，将领域知识注入基础大语言模型，在 DRAMA 基准上达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 零样本多模态大语言模型在细粒度、空间定位的风险评估任务上表现不如领域专用方法，因此需要一种能有效注入领域知识的框架来提升自动驾驶场景中的风险感知能力。

Method: 首先生成包含运动、空间和深度线索的多模态空间定位描述；然后将这些描述用于下游风险评估，明确识别危险物体、位置及不安全行为，并提供可操作的安全建议；最后通过描述-风险配对微调轻量级适配器模块，高效地将领域知识注入基础大语言模型。

Result: 在 DRAMA 基准上达到最先进性能；消融实验验证了关键设计选择的有效性；相比零样本 MLLM 和先前的领域专用方法均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过将风险评估显式地建立在语言场景表示之上，DriveSafe 有效弥合了通用多模态大模型与领域专用方法之间的差距，为自动驾驶中的风险感知场景理解提供了有效的框架。

Original Abstract: Comprehensive situational awareness is essential for autonomous vehicles operating in safety-critical environments, as it enables the identification and mitigation of potential risks. Although recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown promise on general vision-language tasks, our findings indicate that zero-shot MLLMs still underperform compared to domain-specific methods in fine-grained, spatially grounded risk assessment. To address this gap, we propose DriveSafe, a framework for risk-aware scene understanding that leverages structured natural language descriptions. Specifically, our method first generates spatially grounded captions enriched with multimodal context, including motion, spatial, and depth cues. These captions are then used for downstream risk assessment, explicitly identifying hazardous objects, their locations, and the unsafe behaviors they imply, followed by actionable safety suggestions. To further improve performance, we employ caption-risk pairings to fine-tune a lightweight adapter module, efficiently injecting domain-specific knowledge into the base LLM. By conditioning risk assessment on explicit language-based scene representations, DriveSafe achieves significant gains over both zero-shot MLLMs and prior domain-specific baselines. Exhaustive experiments on the DRAMA benchmark demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, while ablation studies validate the effectiveness of our key design choices. Project page: https://cvit.iiit.ac.in/ research/projects/cvit-projects/drivesafe

中文摘要翻译: 全面的情境感知对于在安全关键环境中运行的自动驾驶车辆至关重要，它能够识别和缓解潜在风险。尽管最近的多模态大语言模型在通用视觉-语言任务上展现出潜力，但我们的发现表明，零样本多模态大语言模型在细粒度、空间定位的风险评估方面仍不如领域专用方法。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了 DriveSafe，一个利用结构化自然语言描述进行风险感知场景理解的框架。具体而言，我们的方法首先生成空间定位的描述，并融入包括运动、空间和深度线索在内的多模态上下文。这些描述随后用于下游风险评估，明确识别危险物体、它们的位置以及由此暗示的不安全行为，随后提供可操作的安全建议。为了进一步提升性能，我们利用描述-风险配对来微调一个轻量级适配器模块，高效地将领域特定知识注入基础大语言模型。通过将风险评估显式地建立在基于语言的情景表示之上，DriveSafe 在零样本多模态大语言模型和先前的领域专用基线上均取得了显著的性能提升。在 DRAMA 基准上的全面实验展示了最先进的性能，同时消融研究验证了我们关键设计选择的有效性。项目页面：https://cvit.iiit.ac.in/research/projects/cvit-projects/drivesafe

</details>


### [66] [LASAR: Towards Spatio-temporal Reasoning with Latent Cognitive Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16899)
*Jinzhou Tang,Sidi Liu,Waikit Xiu,Weixing Chen,Keze Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对具身AI中智能体是否真正构建了空间结构内部模型的问题，提出了LASAR架构（双记忆系统）和ST-CRL对比学习目标，通过利用时空上下文线索构建认知地图，在VLN-CE和VSI-Bench基准上实现了2%-3.5%的零样本泛化提升，并验证了认知地图的高自一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有具身AI方法（如基于动作的VLN和基于推理的EQA）缺乏强制智能体编码细粒度空间关系（如拓扑结构或距离）的学习信号，导致无法区分智能体是真正构建了空间结构内部模型，还是仅仅模仿了任务特定的专家轨迹。因此，需要一种能促使智能体从长期、碎片化经验中学习并编码空间关系的方法。

Method: 提出LASAR架构，包含双记忆系统（维持情景经验和语义认知地图）；提出ST-CRL（时空上下文表示学习）对比目标，通过模拟环境中带注释的时空上下文生成认知查询，构建样本对来训练该架构，从而从智能体经验中形成内部认知地图。

Result: 在标准VLN-CE和VSI-Bench基准上，零样本泛化性能提升2%-3.5%；所提出的认知地图具有高自一致性。

Conclusion: 通过双记忆系统架构和时空上下文对比学习，可以有效促使智能体从长期碎片化经验中编码细粒度空间关系，构建具有高自一致性的内部认知地图，从而提升具身AI在空间理解任务中的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: A fundamental challenge in embodied AI is verifying if agents build internal models of spatial structure or merely learn to mimic task-specific expert trajectories. This is critical as foundational approaches rooted in action-centric tasks (e.g., VLN) and reasoning-centric tasks (e.g., EQA) often share a common limitation: they lack a learning signal that forces them to encode fine-grained spatial relationships (like topology or distance) over long-range, fragmented experiences. To address this, we first propose LASAR, an architecture featuring a dual-memory system designed to maintain both episodic experiences and a semantic cognitive map. We then introduce Spatio-temporal Contextual Representation Learning (ST-CRL), a contrastive objective designed to train this architecture. ST-CRL leverages spatio-temporal cues from cognitive queries generated through annotated spatio-temporal context in simulation to build sample pairs, thereby forming the internal cognitive map from the agent's experiences. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves 2\%-3.5\% gains in both zero-shot generalization on standard VLN-CE and VSI-Bench benchmarks. We also demonstrate that our proposed cognitive map has high self-consistency.

中文摘要翻译: 具身AI中的一个基本挑战是验证智能体是否构建了空间结构的内部模型，还是仅仅学会了模仿特定任务的专家轨迹。这一点至关重要，因为基于动作的任务（如VLN）和基于推理的任务（如EQA）的 foundational 方法通常共享一个共同局限性：它们缺乏一种学习信号，无法强制智能体从长期、碎片化的经验中编码细粒度的空间关系（如拓扑结构或距离）。为解决这一问题，我们首先提出LASAR，一种具有双记忆系统的架构，旨在同时维持情景经验和语义认知地图。然后我们引入时空上下文表示学习（ST-CRL），一种用于训练该架构的对比学习目标。ST-CRL利用从模拟环境中带注释的时空上下文生成的认知查询中的时空线索来构建样本对，从而从智能体的经验中形成内部认知地图。实验表明，我们的方法在标准VLN-CE和VSI-Bench基准上的零样本泛化性能提升了2%-3.5%。我们还证明了所提出的认知地图具有高自一致性。

</details>


### [67] [CAR-SAM: Cross-Attention Reconstruction for Post-Training Quantization of the Segment Anything Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16901)
*Houji Wen,Jiangyong Yu,Jun Li,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CAR-SAM量化框架，通过MatMul-Aware Compensation缓解注意力消散，通过Joint Cross-Attention Reconstruction抑制重建振荡，实现SAM模型在4-bit量化下性能大幅超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: SAM模型计算和内存需求高，难以部署在资源受限设备上；现有PTQ方法未考虑SAM解码器中的交叉注意力架构，导致量化后性能严重下降，其根源在于注意力消散和重建振荡两个独特挑战。

Method: (1) MatMul-Aware Compensation (MAC)：将MatMul引起的激活量化误差转移到前序线性权重中，缓解注意力消散；(2) Joint Cross-Attention Reconstruction (JCAR)：联合重建耦合的注意力分支，抑制振荡行为，促进稳定收敛。

Result: 在SAM-B和SAM-L上分别以14.6%和6.6% mAP超越现有方法，成功将SAM量化至4-bit精度。

Conclusion: CAR-SAM通过针对SAM特有注意力结构的量化优化，有效克服了注意力消散和重建振荡问题，实现了SAM模型在低比特量化下的鲁棒部署。

Original Abstract: Segment Anything Models (SAMs) are extensively used in computer vision for universal image segmentation, but deploying them on resource-constrained devices is challenging due to their high computational and memory demands. Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) is a widely used technique for model compression and acceleration. However, existing PTQ methods fail to consider the cross-attention architecture in the SAM decoder. This degradation primarily stems from the unique challenges posed by SAMs: (1) Attention dissipation, where the attention information in the decoder, which is crucial for representing segmentation masks, collapses into a diffuse and non-semantic form under low-bit quantization; and (2) Reconstruction oscillation, where bidirectional coupling within the two-way transformer introduces cross-branch error interference and destabilizes convergence. To tackle these issues, we propose CAR-SAM, a unified quantization framework tailored for SAMs. Firstly, to mitigate attention dissipation, we introduce MatMul-Aware Compensation (MAC) mechanism that transfers activation-induced quantization errors from MatMul to preceding linear weights. Secondly, to mitigate oscillation in decoder optimization, we develop a Joint Cross-Attention Reconstruction (JCAR) strategy that jointly reconstructs coupled attention branches, suppressing oscillatory behavior and promoting stable convergence. Extensive experiments show that CAR-SAM robustly quantizes SAM models down to 4-bit precision, surpassing existing methods by 14.6% and 6.6% mAP on SAM-B and SAM-L respectively.

中文摘要翻译: Segment Anything Models (SAMs) 在计算机视觉中被广泛用于通用图像分割，但由于其高昂的计算和内存需求，在资源受限设备上部署面临挑战。训练后量化（PTQ）是一种广泛使用的模型压缩和加速技术。然而，现有的 PTQ 方法未能考虑 SAM 解码器中的交叉注意力架构。这种性能下降主要源于 SAM 带来的独特挑战：（1）注意力消散，即解码器中对于表示分割掩码至关重要的注意力信息，在低比特量化下坍缩为弥散且非语义的形式；（2）重建振荡，即双向变换器内部的双向耦合引入了跨分支错误干扰并破坏了收敛稳定性。为解决这些问题，我们提出了 CAR-SAM，一个专为 SAM 定制的统一量化框架。首先，为了缓解注意力消散，我们引入了矩阵乘法感知补偿（MAC）机制，将矩阵乘法引起的激活量化误差转移到前序线性权重中。其次，为了缓解解码器优化中的振荡，我们开发了联合交叉注意力重建（JCAR）策略，联合重建耦合的注意力分支，抑制振荡行为并促进稳定收敛。大量实验表明，CAR-SAM 能够将 SAM 模型鲁棒地量化至 4-bit 精度，在 SAM-B 和 SAM-L 上分别以 14.6% 和 6.6% mAP 超越现有方法。

</details>


### [68] [WOW-Seg: A Word-free Open World Segmentation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16903)
*Danyang Li,Tianhao Wu,Bin Li,Zhenyuan Chen,Yang Zhang,Yuxuan Li,Ming-Ming Cheng,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WOW-Seg提出了一种无需文本提示的开放世界图像分割模型，通过Mask2Token视觉提示模块和Cascade Attention Mask解耦多实例信息，并在构建的RR-7K基准上超越SOTA，参数量仅为1/8。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统封闭集分割方法难以适应开放世界场景，而SAM等基础分割模型存在强分割能力与弱语义理解之间的显著差距，亟需一种能够同时实现精确分割和语义理解的开放世界分割模型。

Method: 提出WOW-Seg模型，包含两个核心模块：(1) Mask2Token视觉提示模块，将图像掩码转换为视觉token并对齐到VLLM特征空间；(2) Cascade Attention Mask，通过级联注意力掩码解耦不同实例间的信息，减少实例间干扰。

Result: 在LVIS数据集上达到语义相似度89.7和语义IoU 82.4，超越之前SOTA，参数量仅为1/8；同时构建了包含7,662类别的RR-7K区域识别基准，是目前类别最丰富的区域识别数据集。

Conclusion: WOW-Seg通过视觉提示和对齐机制有效弥合了分割能力与语义理解之间的差距，展现出强大的开放世界泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Open world image segmentation aims to achieve precise segmentation and semantic understanding of targets within images by addressing the infinitely open set of object categories encountered in the real world. However, traditional closed-set segmentation approaches struggle to adapt to complex open world scenarios, while foundation segmentation models such as SAM exhibit notable discrepancies between their strong segmentation capabilities and relatively weaker semantic understanding. To bridge these discrepancies, we propose WOW-Seg, a Word-free Open World Segmentation model for segmenting and recognizing objects from open-set categories. Specifically, WOW-Seg introduces a novel visual prompt module, Mask2Token, which transforms image masks into visual tokens and ensures their alignment with the VLLM feature space. Moreover, we introduce the Cascade Attention Mask to decouple information across different instances. This approach mitigates inter-instance interference, leading to a significant improvement in model performance. We further construct an open world region recognition test benchmark: the Region Recognition Dataset (RR-7K). With 7,662 classes, it represents the most extensive category-rich region recognition dataset to date. WOW-Seg attains strong results on the LVIS dataset, achieving a semantic similarity of 89.7 and a semantic IoU of 82.4. This performance surpasses the previous SOTA while using only one-eighth the parameter count. These results underscore the strong open world generalization capabilities of WOW-Seg. The code and related resources are available at https://github.com/AAwcAA/WOW-Seg-Meta.

中文摘要翻译: 开放世界图像分割旨在通过处理现实世界中遇到的无限开放对象类别集，实现对图像中目标的精确分割和语义理解。然而，传统的封闭集分割方法难以适应复杂的开放世界场景，而SAM等基础分割模型在其强大的分割能力与相对较弱的语义理解能力之间存在显著差距。为弥合这些差距，我们提出了WOW-Seg，一种无需文本的开放世界分割模型，用于对开放集类别的对象进行分割和识别。具体而言，WOW-Seg引入了一种新颖的视觉提示模块Mask2Token，该模块将图像掩码转换为视觉token，并确保其与VLLM特征空间的对齐。此外，我们引入了级联注意力掩码（Cascade Attention Mask）来解耦不同实例间的信息。这种方法减轻了实例间的干扰，从而显著提升了模型性能。我们进一步构建了一个开放世界区域识别测试基准：区域识别数据集（RR-7K）。该数据集包含7,662个类别，是迄今为止类别最丰富的区域识别数据集。WOW-Seg在LVIS数据集上取得了优异的结果，语义相似度达到89.7，语义IoU达到82.4。这一性能超越了之前的最优方法，同时参数量仅为八分之一。这些结果凸显了WOW-Seg强大的开放世界泛化能力。代码及相关资源可在https://github.com/AAwcAA/WOW-Seg-Meta获取。

</details>


### [69] [VGGT-Occ: Geometry-Grounded and Density-Aware Gated Fusion for 3D Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16911)
*Xun Chen,Tianchen Deng,Rui Wang,Fangjinhua Wang,Junyi Ma,Hongming Shen,Hesheng Wang,Danwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VGGT-Occ提出了一种将几何token嵌入3D语义占用预测全流程的框架，通过投影感知可变形注意力（PA-DA）和粗到细的级联解码器，在SurroundOcc-nuScenes上以约41M参数量取得了33.64% IoU和21.43% mIoU的SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D语义占用预测方法在2D-to-3D特征提升后，后续的偏移学习、注意力加权和跨相机聚合等操作忽略了几何约束，缺乏对物理世界的几何一致性建模。

Method: 提出VGGT-Occ框架，核心包括：(1) 投影感知可变形注意力（PA-DA），将3D偏移投影回图像平面并利用投影雅可比矩阵作为加性偏置抑制不可靠观测；(2) 视图质量语义门控实现跨视图一致性特征融合；(3) 粗到细的级联解码器与门控融合，从低分辨率到高分辨率逐步精化特征，按信息密度分配计算资源。

Result: 在SurroundOcc-nuScenes数据集上，VGGT-Occ在T=1时达到33.00% IoU和21.08% mIoU，T=2时达到33.64% IoU和21.43% mIoU，超越现有方法，且占用头部仅约41M可训练参数。

Conclusion: 将几何显式嵌入整个pipeline（而非仅初始投影）能显著提升3D语义占用预测的精度与效率，PA-DA和粗到细解码器是有效的设计。

Original Abstract: 3D semantic occupancy prediction requires accurate 2D-to-3D feature lifting, yet current methods restrict camera geometry to initial projections. Subsequent operations like offset learning, attention weighting, and cross-camera aggregation remain geometry-agnostic, ignoring essential physical constraints. We propose VGGT-Occ, a framework that embeds geometric tokens throughout the entire pipeline. We introduce Projection-Aware Deformable Attention (PA-DA) to inject geometry into all attention stages. PA-DA projects 3D offsets back to image planes and leverages the projection Jacobian as an additive bias to suppress unreliable observations. Features are then integrated through a view-quality semantic gate for cross-view consistency. To optimize both efficiency and performance, we employ a sequential coarse-to-fine decoder with gated fusion, where low-resolution features are refined into higher resolutions, allocating computation by information density while substantially reducing decoder cost. Extensive evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness and accuracy of our approach. On SurroundOcc-nuScenes, VGGT-Occ achieves 33.00\% IoU and 21.08\% mIoU ($T{=}1$), and 33.64\% IoU and 21.43\% mIoU with $T{=}2$ inference, outperforming existing methods, with only ${\sim}41$M trainable parameters in the occupancy head. Code will be released publicly.

中文摘要翻译: 3D语义占用预测要求精确的2D-to-3D特征提升，然而现有方法将相机几何约束仅局限于初始投影阶段。后续的偏移学习、注意力加权和跨相机聚合等操作仍然与几何无关，忽略了关键的物理约束。本文提出VGGT-Occ框架，在整个流程中嵌入几何token。我们引入投影感知可变形注意力（PA-DA），将几何信息注入所有注意力阶段。PA-DA将3D偏移投影回图像平面，并利用投影雅可比矩阵作为加性偏置来抑制不可靠的观测。随后，通过视图质量语义门控实现特征的跨视图一致性集成。为了兼顾效率与性能，我们采用带有门控融合的级联粗到细解码器，其中低分辨率特征逐步精化为更高分辨率，按信息密度分配计算量，同时显著降低解码器成本。大量实验证明了该方法的有效性和准确性。在SurroundOcc-nuScenes数据集上，VGGT-Occ在T=1推理时达到33.00% IoU和21.08% mIoU，在T=2推理时达到33.64% IoU和21.43% mIoU，均优于现有方法，且占用头部仅约41M可训练参数。代码将公开发布。

</details>


### [70] [HighSync: High-Quality Lip Synchronization via Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16918)
*Saeed Firouzi Daghigh,Majid Iranpour Mobarekeh,Mostafa Alavi,Mehdi Bagheri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HighSync是一个端到端扩散框架，用于高保真唇音同步，能在512×512原生分辨率下生成与任意输入音频对齐的逼真说话人脸视频，通过识别并消除数据泄露问题，在感知质量和同步精度上均达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法难以兼顾图像质量与同步精度，要么生成视觉退化的输出，要么产生时序不一致的唇部动作，无法满足影视广播等专业制作环境的需求。

Method: 基于扩散的端到端框架，核心创新在于识别并系统消除了先前工作中损害时序建模的"数据泄露"现象，使模型真正依赖音频信号进行唇部运动生成。

Result: 在感知质量和同步精度两个维度的综合评估中均达到最先进水平，且是首个原生支持512×512分辨率的唇音同步模型。

Conclusion: HighSync同时解决了图像质量与同步精度的矛盾，为专业制作环境提供了可行的解决方案。

Original Abstract: We present HighSync, an end-to-end diffusion-based framework for high-fidelity lip synchronization that generates photorealistic talking-face videos aligned with arbitrary input audio. Existing approaches consistently struggle to reconcile image quality with synchronization accuracy, producing either visually degraded outputs or temporally inconsistent lip movements. HighSync addresses both challenges simultaneously and, to our knowledge, is the first lip sync model to operate natively at 512*512 resolution, positioning it as a viable solution for professional production environments such as the film and broadcast industries. Central to our approach is the identification and systematic elimination of a data leakage phenomenon that has silently undermined temporal modeling in prior work, preventing models from developing a genuine dependence on the audio signal. Comprehensive evaluations across both perceptual quality and synchronization accuracy metrics confirm that HighSync achieves state-of-the-art performance on both fronts. Source code, pre-trained models, and supplementary video results are publicly available at: https://github.com/saeed5959/high_sync

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了HighSync，一个基于扩散的端到端高保真唇音同步框架，能够生成与任意输入音频对齐的逼真说话人脸视频。现有方法始终难以兼顾图像质量与同步精度，要么产生视觉退化的输出，要么产生时序不一致的唇部运动。HighSync同时解决了这两个挑战，据我们所知，它是首个原生运行于512×512分辨率的唇音同步模型，使其成为影视广播等专业制作环境的可行解决方案。我们方法的核心在于识别并系统消除了先前工作中一种悄然损害时序建模的数据泄露现象——该现象阻止了模型对音频信号产生真正的依赖。在感知质量和同步精度指标上的综合评估证实，HighSync在这两方面均达到了最先进水平。源代码、预训练模型及补充视频结果已公开于：https://github.com/saeed5959/high_sync

</details>


### [71] [Motion Cues from Image-based Point Tracking for LiDAR Scene Flow Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16922)
*Youngdong Jang,Gyeongrok Oh,Jong Wook Kim,Hyunju Ryu,Hyung-gun Chi,SeungHyeon Kim,Seungryong Kim,Jonghyun Choi,Sangpil Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TrackCue，一种利用点跟踪来改进LiDAR场景流估计中动态目标表示的自监督框架，通过图像空间密集轨迹补偿稀疏几何观测的不足，显著提升了动静态分类精度和场景流学习效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自监督LiDAR场景流估计方法依赖稀疏几何观测进行动静态分类，容易受到数据稀疏和遮挡的影响，产生噪声标签，导致错误的运动引导和场景流学习退化。

Method: TrackCue包含三个核心模块：(1) 利用点跟踪获取以LiDAR点为锚点的密集图像空间轨迹，提供超越稀疏几何观测的运动线索；(2) 视觉一致的运动补偿策略，在图像平面上比较跟踪轨迹与自车诱导的刚性轨迹，分离真实物体运动与自车诱导的表观运动；(3) 视觉运动线索提升模块，将自车补偿后的图像轨迹与LiDAR点关联，用于动静态标签精炼。

Result: 实验结果表明，TrackCue显著提升了动态标签的精度和F1分数，并在自监督场景流估计任务中取得了性能提升。

Conclusion: TrackCue通过引入跟踪引导的框架有效改善了动静态分类的准确性，为场景流学习提供了更可靠的监督信号，证明了结合图像空间运动线索来增强LiDAR场景流估计的有效性。

Original Abstract: LiDAR scene flow estimation is essential for autonomous driving, as it provides 3D motion for each point. Self-supervised approaches use static-dynamic classification to mitigate the imbalance between static and dynamic points, deriving targeted supervision. However, existing methods rely on sparse geometric observations for this classification, making them vulnerable to data sparsity and occlusions. The resulting noisy labels provide incorrect motion guidance and degrade scene flow learning. To address this, we introduce TrackCue, a tracking-guided framework for improving dynamic object representation in LiDAR scene flow estimation. In particular, TrackCue repurposes point tracking to obtain dense image-space trajectories anchored to LiDAR points, providing motion cues beyond sparse geometric observations. Furthermore, we present a visually consistent motion compensation strategy that compares the tracked trajectories with ego-induced rigid trajectories in the image plane, effectively isolating true object motion from ego-induced apparent motion. To transfer these isolated motion cues back to the LiDAR domain, we perform visual motion cue lifting, which associates ego-compensated image trajectories with LiDAR points for static-dynamic label refinement. As a result, TrackCue produces more accurate static-dynamic classification and provides more reliable supervision for scene flow learning. Experimental results show that TrackCue significantly improves the precision and F1 score of dynamic labels, leading to performance gains in self-supervised scene flow estimation.

中文摘要翻译: LiDAR场景流估计对于自动驾驶至关重要，因为它为每个点提供3D运动信息。自监督方法利用动静态分类来缓解静态点和动态点之间的不平衡，从而产生有针对性的监督信号。然而，现有方法依赖稀疏几何观测进行这种分类，使其容易受到数据稀疏性和遮挡的影响。由此产生的噪声标签提供了错误的运动引导，并降低了场景流学习的质量。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了TrackCue，一种跟踪引导的框架，用于改进LiDAR场景流估计中的动态目标表示。具体来说，TrackCue重新利用点跟踪来获取以LiDAR点为锚点的密集图像空间轨迹，提供超越稀疏几何观测的运动线索。此外，我们提出了一种视觉一致的运动补偿策略，在图像平面上比较跟踪轨迹与自车诱导的刚性轨迹，有效分离真实物体运动与自车诱导的表观运动。为了将这些分离的运动线索迁移回LiDAR域，我们执行视觉运动线索提升，将自车补偿后的图像轨迹与LiDAR点关联，用于动静态标签精炼。最终，TrackCue产生了更准确的动静态分类，并为场景流学习提供了更可靠的监督信号。实验结果表明，TrackCue显著提升了动态标签的精度和F1分数，从而在自监督场景流估计中取得了性能提升。

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [72] [Flow Matching with Optimized Subclass Priors for Medical Image Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16469)
*Felix Nützel,Mischa Dombrowski,Bernhard Kainz*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 针对长尾医学影像中罕见病生成增强的难题，提出一种离线策略：在生成模型潜空间用高斯混合模型划分粗标签为一致子模式，并为各子模式学习重标度的类别条件先验分布，配合几何约束提升生成保真度与多样性，进而改善下游分类性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 罕见病在临床数据集中严重不足，分类器在关键检测任务上表现差；现有生成式增强方法使用粗粒度疾病标签，聚合了多种亚型和采集设置，导致生成器偏向主导子模式，且共享高斯先验迫使罕见子群体经过不成比例的长传输路径，生成质量低下。

Method: 提出离线增强策略，在两层引入信息先验：1) 在生成模型潜空间通过高斯混合模型将每个粗标签划分为一致子模式；2) 为每个子模式学习类别条件源分布，重新定心和缩放起始分布以缩短轨迹、降低子模式内离散度。同时施加显式几何控制，适度将归一化位移方向集中于可学习原型附近并截断路径长度异常值。

Result: 在长尾胸部X光（MIMIC-LT、NIH-LT）和CT切片（CT-RATE）基准上，所提方法持续提升了尾部类别的生成保真度与多样性（FID、IRS），作为增强策略可靠地提升了跨模态下游任务的平衡准确率和宏F1分数，优于未增强基线。

Conclusion: 通过潜空间子模式划分与类别条件先验重标度，配合几何正则化，能有效提升罕见病生成质量并改善下游分类性能，为长尾医学影像生成增强提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: Rare diseases dominate the diagnostic challenge in medical imaging yet are severely underrepresented in clinical datasets, causing classifiers to fail on exactly the conditions where reliable detection matters most. Generative augmentation can supply the missing tail-class coverage, but coarse disease labels aggregate diverse subtypes and acquisition settings into multi-modal conditionals that bias generators toward dominant submodes, while a shared Gaussian source forces rare subpopulations through disproportionately long transport paths. We propose an offline strategy that introduces informative priors at two levels: first, we partition each coarse label into coherent submodes via Gaussian mixture modeling in the generative model's latent space; second, we learn subclass-conditioned source distributions that re-center and re-scale the starting distribution per submode, shortening trajectories and reducing within-subclass dispersion. To prevent degenerate solutions we impose explicit geometric control, moderately concentrating normalized displacement directions around learnable prototypes while capping path-length outliers. On long-tailed chest X-ray (MIMIC-LT, NIH-LT) and CT slice (CT-RATE) benchmarks the proposed method consistently improves tail-class generation fidelity and diversity (FID, IRS) and is a promising augmentation strategy that reliably improves downstream balanced accuracy and macro-F1 over a non-augmented baseline across modalities.

中文摘要翻译: 罕见病主导着医学影像中的诊断挑战，但在临床数据集中却严重不足，导致分类器在最需要可靠检测的疾病上失败。生成式增强可以弥补缺失的尾部类别覆盖，但粗粒度的疾病标签将多样的亚型和采集设置聚合为多模态条件，使生成器偏向主导子模式，同时共享的高斯源迫使罕见子群体经过不成比例的长传输路径。我们提出一种离线策略，在两个层面引入信息先验：首先，在生成模型的潜空间通过高斯混合模型将每个粗标签划分为一致的子模式；其次，学习子模式条件源分布，为每个子模式重新定心和缩放起始分布，从而缩短轨迹并减少子模式内离散度。为防止退化解，我们施加显式几何控制，适度将归一化位移方向集中于可学习原型附近，同时截断路径长度异常值。在长尾胸部X光（MIMIC-LT、NIH-LT）和CT切片（CT-RATE）基准上，所提方法持续提升了尾部类别的生成保真度与多样性（FID、IRS），并作为一种有前景的增强策略，可靠地提升了跨模态下游任务的平衡准确率和宏F1分数，优于未增强基线。

</details>


### [73] [Deep Learning for MRI Slice Interpolation: The Critical Role of Problem Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16476)
*Shamit Savant*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用深度学习对前列腺MRI进行切片插值以提升穿透平面分辨率，发现问题公式化（使用相邻切片而非远距切片）对性能的影响远大于架构复杂度，U-Net取得最优结果（PSNR 30.08 dB，SSIM 0.898），而DDPM因随机生成与确定性重建不匹配而表现不佳。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 临床MRI的穿透平面分辨率通常远低于平面内分辨率，限制了诊断效用。本文旨在通过深度学习方法对前列腺MRI进行中间切片插值，有效将穿透平面分辨率提升一倍，改善图像质量以增强临床诊断价值。

Method: 评估了五种深度学习架构（CNN、U-Net、两种GAN变体以及DDPM）用于MRI切片插值。关键发现是将插值任务从使用远距切片(i-2, i+2)重新公式化为使用相邻切片(i-1, i+1)，所有确定性架构的SSIM性能提升58%。

Result: U-Net模型取得最佳结果：PSNR为30.08 dB，SSIM为0.898，相比线性插值基线提升10.1%。问题公式化对性能的影响比架构复杂度高出290倍。DDPM因随机生成与确定性重建需求不匹配，重建质量较差。

Conclusion: 在医学成像任务中，问题公式化对性能的影响远大于架构的精巧程度（可达290倍差异）。DDPM等随机生成模型不适合需要确定性的医学图像重建任务，而U-Net等确定性架构表现更优。

Original Abstract: Through-plane resolution in clinical MRI is typically much coarser than in-plane resolution, limiting diagnostic utility. This work investigates deep learning approaches to interpolate intermediate MRI slices in prostate imaging, effectively doubling through-plane resolution. I evaluated five architectures (CNN, U-Net, two GAN variants, and DDPM) and discovered that problem formulation has dramatically more impact than architectural complexity. By reformulating the interpolation task to use adjacent slices (i-1, i+1) rather than distant slices (i-2, i+2), I achieved a 58% improvement in SSIM performance across all deterministic architectures. The U-Net model achieved the best results with PSNR of 30.08 dB and SSIM of 0.898, representing a 10.1% improvement over linear interpolation baseline. A DDPM was also evaluated but showed poor reconstruction quality due to fundamental mismatch between stochastic generation and deterministic reconstruction requirements. These findings demonstrate that problem formulation can have 290x more impact than architectural sophistication in medical imaging tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 临床MRI的穿透平面分辨率通常远低于平面内分辨率，这限制了诊断效用。本文研究了利用深度学习方法对前列腺成像中的中间MRI切片进行插值，从而有效将穿透平面分辨率提升一倍。我评估了五种架构（CNN、U-Net、两种GAN变体以及DDPM），并发现问题公式化对性能的影响远超架构复杂度。通过将插值任务重新公式化为使用相邻切片(i-1, i+1)而非远距切片(i-2, i+2)，我在所有确定性架构上实现了SSIM性能58%的提升。U-Net模型取得了最佳结果，PSNR达到30.08 dB，SSIM达到0.898，相比线性插值基线提升了10.1%。本文还评估了DDPM，但由于随机生成与确定性重建需求之间存在根本性不匹配，其重建质量较差。这些发现表明，在医学成像任务中，问题公式化的影响可以是架构精妙程度的290倍。

</details>


### [74] [Adaptive Fused Prior Transfer for Controllable Generative Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16817)
*Yifei Pei,Ying Liu,Nam Ling*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应融合先验传输的可控生成式图像压缩方法AFP-GIC，通过从预训练AdaCode模型迁移融合先验，在不传输先验本身的情况下实现先验引导重建，在极低码率下提升感知质量，同时降低解码延迟和参数量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有可控图像压缩方法在极低码率下，解码端必须从有限传输信息中推断缺失细节，但基于单码本书签的先验设计无法有效解决这一重建先验问题。

Method: 从冻结的预训练AdaCode模型中迁移自适应融合先验：编码端融合先验特征引导隐变量形成；解码端从压缩表示和选定控制变量中预测兼容的融合先验，实现先验引导重建而无需传输融合先验本身。理论分析表明融合先验族包含单码本先验作为特例，且解码端融合先验对齐与重建误差上界相关。

Result: 在统一基准下，AFP-GIC相对于DC-VIC降低解码延迟18.1%，参数量减少3110万（20.5%）。在Kodak、CLIC2020、DIV2K数据集上PSNR具竞争力，NIQE得分和极低码率视觉比较中感知质量提升最明显。

Conclusion: AFP-GIC通过自适应融合先验传输有效解决了可控生成式压缩中的解码端重建先验问题，在极低码率下实现更好的感知-失真权衡，且计算效率更优。

Original Abstract: Learned image compression has achieved competitive rate-distortion performance, but very-low-bitrate reconstruction remains difficult because the transmitted representation often cannot preserve fine textures and local structures. Perceptual and generative codecs address this problem by using learned reconstruction priors, and controllable codecs allow one model to cover different bitrate and reconstruction preferences. However, controllability alone does not resolve the decoder-side reconstruction-prior problem: under severe bit constraints, the decoder must infer missing details from limited transmitted information, while existing codebook-based controllable designs generally rely on single-codebook token-based priors. This paper proposes Adaptive Fused Prior Transfer for Controllable Generative Image Compression (AFP-GIC), a controllable codec that transfers an adaptive fused prior from a frozen pretrained AdaCode model. Encoder-side fused-prior features guide latent formation, while the decoder predicts a compatible fused prior from the compressed representation and selected control variables, enabling prior-guided reconstruction without transmitting the fused prior itself. A motivating analysis relates decoder-side fused-prior alignment to a reconstruction-error upper bound and shows that the fused-prior family contains single-codebook choices as special cases. Under the unified benchmark, AFP-GIC reduces decoder latency by 18.1% and the overall parameter count by 31.10 million (20.5%) relative to DC-VIC. Experiments on Kodak, CLIC2020, and DIV2K show competitive PSNR, with the clearest perceptual gains in NIQE scores and very-low-bitrate visual comparisons.

中文摘要翻译: 学习型图像压缩已取得具有竞争力的率失真性能，但在极低码率下重建仍然困难，因为传输的表示往往无法保留精细纹理和局部结构。感知和生成式编解码器通过使用学习到的重建先验来解决这一问题，而可控编解码器允许一个模型覆盖不同的码率和重建偏好。然而，仅靠可控性并不能解决解码端的重建先验问题：在严苛的比特约束下，解码器必须从有限的传输信息中推断缺失的细节，而现有的基于码本的可控设计通常依赖于单码本书签先验。本文提出面向可控生成式图像压缩的自适应融合先验传输方法（AFP-GIC），这是一种可控编解码器，它从冻结的预训练AdaCode模型中迁移自适应融合先验。编码端融合先验特征引导隐变量形成，而解码端从压缩表示和选定的控制变量中预测兼容的融合先验，从而无需传输融合先验本身即可实现先验引导重建。动机分析将解码端融合先验对齐与重建误差上界相关联，并表明融合先验族包含单码本选择作为特例。在统一基准下，AFP-GIC相对于DC-VIC降低解码延迟18.1%，总参数量减少3110万（20.5%）。在Kodak、CLIC2020和DIV2K上的实验展示了具有竞争力的PSNR，在NIQE得分和极低码率视觉比较中感知增益最为明显。

</details>


### [75] [RDDM: A Residual-Driven Drifting Model for High-Fidelity Low-Dose CT Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17188)
*Jianxu Wang,Qing Lyu,Ge Wang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出残差驱动漂移模型（RDDM），通过残差漂移场实现一步式低剂量CT去噪，在保真度、效率和实时性上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LDCT去噪方法中，基于像素级损失的方法易产生过平滑结果，而扩散模型等生成式方法虽效果更好但需要多步迭代推理，计算成本高，难以用于实时临床场景。因此需要一种兼具高保真度和高效率的去噪方法。

Method: 受漂移模型启发，RDDM将多步分布演化融入训练动力学中，通过残差漂移场实现一步去噪。残差漂移场由LDCT与NDCT之间的残差产生的吸引力以及生成残差产生的排斥力共同构成。此外，通过调整参数设置并引入像素级监督，开发了三种变体，覆盖从细节保留到强噪声抑制的不同应用需求。

Result: RDDM在监督基线中达到SOTA去噪性能。其中RDDM-Fine变体与NDCT高度一致，在PSNR和SSIM上表现优异，FID达到5.87，同时保留真实的解剖纹理。单张512×512 LDCT切片去噪仅需约15ms，支持实时推理。

Conclusion: RDDM为临床应用中高保真度和实时的LDCT去噪提供了一种有前景的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Low-dose CT (LDCT) denoising remains an important yet challenging problem in medical imaging. Although recent learning-based methods have shown promising performance, those optimized using classical pixel-level objectives often produce over-smoothed reconstructions. Existing mainstream generative models, such as diffusion models, have improved fidelity at the cost of expensive multi-step iterative inference, which limits their practicality for real-time use. To address this gap, we propose a Residual-Driven Drifting Model (RDDM) for effective, efficient, and high-fidelity LDCT denoising. Inspired by the recently proposed Drifting Models, RDDM incorporates the multi-step distribution evolution into the training dynamics through a residual drifting field, thereby enabling one-step denoising. Specifically, the residual drifting field is formed by an attractive force induced by the residuals between LDCT and normal-dose CT (NDCT) and a repulsive force induced by the generated residuals. In addition, by adjusting the parameter settings and incorporating pixel-level supervision, we develop three RDDM variants, covering application needs from detail preservation to stronger noise suppression. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RDDM achieves state-of-the-art denoising performance among supervised baselines. In particular, RDDM-Fine produces reconstructions that are highly consistent with NDCT, achieving superior PSNR and SSIM together with the best FID of 5.87 while preserving realistic anatomical textures. Moreover, RDDM enables on-the-fly inference, requiring only about 15 ms to denoise a single 512 x 512 LDCT slice. These results establish RDDM as a promising solution for high-fidelity and real-time LDCT denoising in clinical applications.

中文摘要翻译: 低剂量CT（LDCT）去噪在医学成像中仍是一个重要且具有挑战性的问题。尽管近期基于学习的方法表现出了良好的性能，但使用经典像素级目标函数优化的方法往往会产生过平滑的重建结果。现有的主流生成模型，如扩散模型，以高昂的多步迭代推理成本换取了保真度的提升，这限制了它们在实时应用中的实用性。为弥补这一差距，我们提出了一种残差驱动漂移模型（RDDM），用于实现有效、高效且高保真度的LDCT去噪。受近期提出的漂移模型的启发，RDDM通过残差漂移场将多步分布演化融入训练动力学中，从而实现一步去噪。具体而言，残差漂移场由LDCT与正常剂量CT（NDCT）之间的残差所产生的吸引力以及生成残差所产生的排斥力共同构成。此外，通过调整参数设置并引入像素级监督，我们开发了三种RDDM变体，覆盖了从细节保留到更强噪声抑制的应用需求。大量实验表明，RDDM在监督基线中达到了最先进的去噪性能。特别地，RDDM-Fine生成了与NDCT高度一致的重建结果，在取得优异PSNR和SSIM的同时，实现了最佳FID（5.87），并保留了真实的解剖纹理。此外，RDDM支持即时推理，对单张512×512 LDCT切片的去噪仅需约15毫秒。这些结果确立了RDDM作为临床应用中高保真度、实时LDCT去噪的有前景的解决方案。

</details>


### [76] [See Silhouettes in Motion with Neuromorphic Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17984)
*Pei Zhang,Shijie Lin,Zhou Ge,Jinpeng Chen,Wei Pu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用帧与事件双模态协同的轻量级二值化方法，在仅使用CPU的情况下实现实时、高帧率二值化，有效克服运动模糊和光照挑战，适用于资源受限的边缘平台。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统基于帧的成像在无人机、自动驾驶汽车和水下机器人等动态场景中，由于快速运动和恶劣光照容易产生运动模糊，丢失关键细节。现有方法难以在资源受限的边缘设备上实现实时、高帧率二值化。

Method: 基于神经形态视觉的事件相机（具有微秒级时间分辨率和高动态范围），提出一种简单有效的双模态方法，融合帧与事件的协同信息，在仅使用CPU的设备上实现实时、高帧率二值化。采用异步工作流避免传统时间分箱重建中的事件稀疏问题。

Result: 在与领先技术的对比中，该方法在减少运动模糊方面表现优异，并在挑战性光照条件下取得显著改进。异步工作流在极端千赫兹帧率下仍能保持清晰的目标形状，二值化结果可作为可靠表示支持多种下游任务。

Conclusion: 该工作为资源受限边缘平台上的具身智能中轻量级感知与交互铺平了道路。

Original Abstract: Quasi-bimodal objects, such as text, road signs, and barcodes, play a basic yet vital role in daily visual communication. By boiling these down to clear silhouettes, binarization uses a minimal language to convey essential vision cues for maximum downstream efficiency. The catch is that frame-based imaging often struggles on mobile platforms like drones, self-driving cars, and underwater vehicles. In these dynamic scenes, rapid motion and harsh lighting can make it blind, causing severe motion blur and erasing crucial details. To overcome the limits, neuromorphic vision via event cameras, featuring microsecond-level temporal resolution and high dynamic range, steps in as a natural solution. Building upon this event-driven sensing paradigm, we introduce a simple yet effective dual-modal approach that harnesses the synergy between frames and events to achieve real-time, high-frame-rate binarization on CPU-only devices. Extensive evaluations present that it earns competitive performance against leading techniques in reducing motion blur, while delivering impressive improvements under challenging illumination. Besides, our asynchronous workflow bypasses event scarcity that breaks traditional time-binning reconstruction, maintaining clear target shapes even at extreme kilohertz frame rates. Its binary results further serve as reliable representations that facilitate a range of downstream tasks. This work paves the way towards lightweight perception and interaction in embodied intelligence on resource-constrained edge platforms.

中文摘要翻译: 准二值物体（如文本、路标和条形码）在日常视觉交流中扮演着基础且至关重要的角色。通过将这些对象简化为清晰的轮廓，二值化用最简洁的语言传递基本的视觉线索，以实现最大化的下游效率。然而，基于帧的成像在无人机、自动驾驶汽车和水下机器人等移动平台上常常面临挑战。在这些动态场景中，快速运动和恶劣光照可能导致成像系统"失明"，造成严重的运动模糊并丢失关键细节。为了克服这些局限，基于事件相机的神经形态视觉——具有微秒级时间分辨率和高动态范围——作为一种自然的解决方案应运而生。基于这一事件驱动感知范式，我们提出了一种简单而有效的双模态方法，利用帧与事件之间的协同作用，在仅使用CPU的设备上实现实时、高帧率二值化。广泛评估表明，该方法在减少运动模糊方面具有与领先技术相竞争的性能，同时在挑战性光照条件下取得了令人瞩目的改进。此外，我们的异步工作流规避了传统时间分箱重建中破坏性的事件稀疏问题，即使在极端千赫兹帧率下也能保持清晰的目标形状。其二值化结果进一步作为可靠表示，促进了一系列下游任务。这项工作为资源受限边缘平台上具身智能中的轻量级感知与交互铺平了道路。

</details>


### [77] [Inter-LPCM: Learning-based Inter-Frame Predictive Coding for LiDAR Point Cloud Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18006)
*Chang Sun,Hui Yuan,Shiqi Jiang,Chongzhen Tian,Guanghui Zhang,Raouf Hamzaoui*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于学习的帧间预测点云压缩方法Inter-LPCM，在球坐标系下通过对方位角、半径和仰角分别设计预测模型，并结合率失真优化的量化和自适应熵编码，提升了点云几何压缩性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有球坐标系点云压缩方法中，基于G-PCC标准的PredGeom方法虽含帧间预测模式但仅使用简单线性模型，难以捕捉复杂运动模式和结构依赖；而现有的学习型球域压缩方法未利用帧间相关性来减少几何冗余。

Method: 1) 方位角预测采用基于预定义角度分辨率的增量编码策略；2) 半径预测提出Inter-RP模型，利用当前帧和已配准参考帧的邻域点估计当前点的半径；3) 仰角预测设计轻量级注意力预测(LAEP)模型，捕获不同坐标间长程几何相关性；4) 量化采用率失真优化的量化步长选择方法；5) 熵编码为每个球坐标分量设计不同模型以适应各自的统计先验。

Result: 未在摘要中明确给出具体数值结果，但表明该方法在率失真性能上优于传统方法，且代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过引入学习型帧间预测机制，Inter-LPCM有效利用了帧间相关性，在球坐标系下实现了更高效的点云几何压缩，为学习型点云压缩提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: Because LiDAR sensors acquire point clouds with a fixed angular resolution, the resulting data can be systematically parameterized and efficiently compressed in the spherical coordinate system. Traditional spherical coordinate-based point cloud compression methods have demonstrated strong rate-distortion (RD) performance, with the predictive geometry coding (PredGeom) method in the geometry-based point cloud compression (G-PCC) standard being a prominent example. Although PredGeom includes an inter-frame prediction mode, it relies on a simple linear model, which limits its ability to capture complex motion patterns and structural dependencies. Meanwhile, existing learning-based compression methods in the spherical domain do not exploit inter-frame correlations to reduce geometry redundancy. To address these limitations, we propose a learning-based inter-frame predictive coding method, termed Inter-LPCM. For azimuth prediction, we employ a delta coding strategy based on the predefined angular resolution. To improve radius compression, we introduce an inter-frame radius predictive (Inter-RP) model that estimates the current point's radius using neighboring points from both the current frame and the registered reference frame. In addition, we design a lightweight attention-based prediction (LAEP) model to predict elevation angles by capturing long-range geometric correlations across different coordinates. For quantization, we propose an RD-optimized method to select quantization steps in the spherical coordinate system. For entropy coding, we design distinct models for each spherical coordinate component. These models are adapted to the statistical priors of each coordinate, enabling more accurate probability estimation. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/SDUChangSun/Inter-LPCM

中文摘要翻译: 由于LiDAR传感器以固定的角度分辨率获取点云，所得数据可以在球坐标系中被系统参数化并有效压缩。传统的基于球坐标系的点云压缩方法已展现出强大的率失真性能，其中基于几何的点云压缩标准中的预测几何编码方法便是一个突出代表。尽管PredGeom包含帧间预测模式，但它依赖于简单的线性模型，这限制了其捕捉复杂运动模式和结构依赖的能力。同时，现有的球域学习型压缩方法并未利用帧间相关性来减少几何冗余。为解决这些局限，我们提出了一种基于学习的帧间预测编码方法，称为Inter-LPCM。对于方位角预测，我们采用基于预定义角度分辨率的增量编码策略。为改善半径压缩，我们引入了帧间半径预测模型，该模型利用当前帧和已配准参考帧中的邻域点来估计当前点的半径。此外，我们设计了一个轻量级基于注意力的预测模型来预测仰角，通过捕捉不同坐标之间的长程几何相关性。在量化方面，我们提出了一种率失真优化的方法，用于选择球坐标系中的量化步长。在熵编码方面，我们为每个球坐标分量设计了不同的模型，这些模型适应各坐标的统计先验，从而实现更精确的概率估计。我们的源代码已在https://github.com/SDUChangSun/Inter-LPCM公开。

</details>


### [78] [CATRF: Codec-Adaptive TriPlane Radiance Fields for Volumetric Content Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18054)
*Tung-I Chen,Lingdong Wang,Subhransu Maji,Ramesh K. Sitaraman*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: CATRF 是一种将标准编解码器（JPEG/VP9/HEVC/AV1）直接嵌入训练流程的体素辐射场压缩框架，通过对平面分解的辐射场特征进行量化、打包、编解码往返后解包和解量化，并利用直通估计器（STE）处理不可微分的编解码过程，在静态和动态场景中均实现了优于现有方法的率失真性能和更快的解码速度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 体素媒体带宽需求巨大，隐式和混合体素表示虽能减小模型尺寸，但仍需精心编码才能达到类似2D视频的码率；现有方法要么忽略编解码器失真，要么引入额外的可学习编解码参数，缺乏直接适配真实客户端标准编解码器失真的高效方案。

Method: 提出CATRF框架：在训练过程中，先将平面分解的辐射场特征进行量化并打包成适配标准编解码器的画布（canvas），然后执行标准编解码器往返（JPEG/VP9/HEVC/AV1），再对解码后的特征进行解包和解量化，最后进行体渲染；使用直通估计器（STE）将不可微分的标准编解码流程插入训练循环，使辐射场特征直接适应真实的客户端编解码失真，无需引入任何可学习的编解码参数。

Result: 在静态和动态基准测试中，CATRF在率失真权衡上 consistently 优于不感知编解码器的方法和将可学习编解码器置于训练循环中的基线方法；同时在压缩效率和解码速度上均优于近年来提出的压缩3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）方法。

Conclusion: CATRF展示了实现低码率、抗压缩的体素表示用于自由视角视频流的实用路径，验证了将标准编解码器直接融入训练流程的有效性。

Original Abstract: Volumetric media promises next-generation content delivery applications, but its bandwidth demand remains a key bottleneck. Implicit and hybrid volumetric representations reduce model sizes, yet still require careful coding to reach 2D video-like bitrates. We present CATRF, a standard-codec-in-the-loop compression framework for plane-factorized radiance fields. During training, we quantize and pack 2D feature planes into codec-friendly canvases, run a standard codec roundtrip (JPEG/VP9/HEVC/AV1), then unpack and dequantize the decoded features before volume rendering. We use a straight-through estimator (STE) to insert the non-differentiable, standard codec pipeline into the training loop, allowing radiance-field features to adapt directly to the real, client-side codec distortions without introducing any learned codec parameters. On both static and dynamic benchmarks, CATRF consistently achieves a better rate-distortion trade-off over codec-agnostic and learned-codec-in-the-loop baselines, and also outperforms recent compressed 3DGS methods in both compression efficiency and decoding speed. These results highlight a practical path toward low-bitrate, compression-resilient volumetric representations for free-viewpoint video streaming.

中文摘要翻译: 体素媒体有望实现下一代内容分发应用，但其带宽需求仍然是一个关键瓶颈。隐式和混合体素表示虽然减小了模型尺寸，但仍需精心编码才能达到类似2D视频的码率。我们提出了CATRF，一种将标准编解码器纳入训练循环的平面分解辐射场压缩框架。在训练过程中，我们对2D特征平面进行量化并打包成适于编解码器的画布，执行标准编解码器往返（JPEG/VP9/HEVC/AV1），然后在体渲染之前对解码后的特征进行解包和解量化。我们使用直通估计器（STE）将不可微分的标准编解码流水线插入训练循环，使辐射场特征能够直接适应真实客户端侧的编解码失真，而无需引入任何可学习的编解码参数。在静态和动态基准测试中，CATRF在率失真权衡上持续优于不感知编解码器和将可学习编解码器纳入训练循环的基线方法，同时在压缩效率和解码速度上也优于近期提出的压缩3D高斯泼溅方法。这些结果突显了实现低码率、抗压缩的体素表示用于自由视角视频流的一条实用路径。

</details>


### [79] [Evaluating the Effect of Compression on Video Temporal Consistency Using Objective Quality Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18378)
*Peter Zsoldos*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了视频压缩中时间一致性的退化规律，发现时间一致性随着压缩强度增加呈非线性退化，并识别出“可预测性异常”现象：不可预测的动态序列比高但可预测的运动序列遭受更严重的不稳定性，挑战了传统认为运动量是编码难度主要决定因素的假设。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究虽然充分记录了空间质量退化，但压缩强度与时间稳定性之间的关系尚未得到充分表征，特别是时间一致性问题缺乏系统研究。

Method: 利用多种编解码器（AV1、HEVC、VP9、H.264）和不同内容类型，系统研究不同码率区间下帧间一致性误差的演变规律。

Result: 时间一致性随压缩强度增加呈非线性退化；发现“可预测性异常”现象：具有不可预测或不规则动态的序列比具有更高但更可预测运动幅度的序列遭受更严重的不稳定性。

Conclusion: 传统认为运动量大小是编码难度主要决定因素的假设受到挑战；压缩管线中需要纳入时间感知的评估指标。

Original Abstract: While video compression algorithms effectively reduce bitrate, aggressive quantization often compromises temporal coherence, introducing artifacts such as flicker, motion inconsistency, and unstable textures. Although spatial quality degradation is well-documented, the relationship between compression intensity and temporal stability remains insufficiently characterized. This paper systematically examines the progression of frame-to-frame coherence errors across different bitrate regimes, utilizing multiple codecs (AV1, HEVC, VP9, H.264) and content types. Our findings reveal that temporal consistency degrades non-linearly with increasing compression. Most critically, we identify a "Predictability anomaly" where sequences with unpredictable or irregular dynamics experience disproportionately higher instability than sequences with higher, but more predictable, motion magnitude. This challenges the conventional assumption that motion volume alone dictates encoding difficulty and highlights the necessity of temporal-aware metrics in compression pipelines.

中文摘要翻译: 虽然视频压缩算法能有效降低码率，但激进的量化操作常常破坏时间一致性，引入闪烁、运动不一致和不稳定纹理等伪影。虽然空间质量退化已得到充分记录，但压缩强度与时间稳定性之间的关系仍未被充分表征。本文系统研究了不同码率区间下帧间一致性误差的演变规律，利用了多种编解码器（AV1、HEVC、VP9、H.264）和多种内容类型。我们的研究结果表明，时间一致性随压缩强度增加呈非线性退化。最关键的是，我们识别出一种"可预测性异常"现象：具有不可预测或不规则动态的序列比具有更高但更可预测运动幅度的序列遭受更严重的不稳定性。这一发现挑战了传统认为运动量大小是编码难度主要决定因素的假设，并强调了在压缩管线中纳入时间感知评估指标的必要性。

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [80] [Systematic Optimization of Real-Time Diffusion Model Inference on Apple M3 Ultra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16259)
*Yoichi Ochiai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Apple M3 Ultra平台上的扩散模型实时图像生成优化，通过10个阶段的实验，最终结合CoreML转换蒸馏模型SDXS-512与三线程摄像头流水线，在512×512分辨率下达到22.7 FPS的实时相机img2img变换。核心发现是CUDA平台的优化经验在Apple Silicon统一内存架构上大多不适用。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在非CUDA平台（如Apple Silicon）上，扩散模型的系统优化研究极度匮乏，而NVIDIA GPU上的实时图像生成优化已取得显著进展。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索在Apple M3 Ultra上实现实时相机img2img变换的可行路径。

Method: 在Apple M3 Ultra（60核GPU，512 GB统一内存）上开展了10个阶段的系统优化实验，探索了CoreML转换、量化、Token Merging、神经引擎利用、紧凑模型、帧插值、kNN搜索合成、pix2pix-turbo、光流跳帧和知识蒸馏等多种技术路线，最终结合CoreML转换的蒸馏模型SDXS-512与三线程摄像头流水线实现实时推理。

Result: 最终在512×512分辨率下实现22.7 FPS的实时相机img2img变换。关键发现包括：量化在Apple Silicon上无加速效果、并行推理无效、神经引擎不适合大模型，揭示了与NVIDIA GPU截然不同的优化格局。

Conclusion: CUDA平台上的优化策略在Apple Silicon的统一内存架构上并不一定有效；本文系统性地展示了这一差异，并为Apple Silicon上的扩散模型推理提供了实用指导方针。

Original Abstract: While real-time image generation using diffusion models has advanced rapidly on NVIDIA GPUs, systematic optimization research on non-CUDA platforms such as Apple Silicon remains extremely limited. In this study, we conducted comprehensive optimization experiments across 10 phases targeting the Apple M3 Ultra (60-core GPU, 512 GB unified memory) with the goal of achieving real-time camera img2img transformation. We explored a wide range of techniques including CoreML conversion, quantization, Token Merging, Neural Engine utilization, compact model exploration, frame interpolation, kNN search-based synthesis, pix2pix-turbo, optical flow frame skipping, and knowledge distillation, quantitatively evaluating the effectiveness of each approach. Ultimately, by combining CoreML conversion of the distillation-specialized model SDXS-512 with a 3-thread camera pipeline, we achieved real-time camera img2img transformation at 22.7 FPS at 512x512 resolution. The primary contribution of this work is the systematic demonstration that optimization insights established for CUDA are not necessarily effective on Apple Silicon's unified memory architecture. We reveal an optimization landscape fundamentally different from that of NVIDIA GPUs -- including the absence of speedup from quantization, the ineffectiveness of parallel inference, and the unsuitability of the Neural Engine for large-scale models -- and provide practical guidelines for diffusion model inference on Apple Silicon.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管基于扩散模型的实时图像生成在NVIDIA GPU上取得了快速发展，但在非CUDA平台（如Apple Silicon）上的系统优化研究仍然极度有限。在本研究中，我们针对Apple M3 Ultra（60核GPU，512 GB统一内存）开展了涵盖10个阶段的全面优化实验，目标是实现实时相机img2img变换。我们探索了广泛的技术路线，包括CoreML转换、量化、Token Merging、神经引擎利用、紧凑模型探索、帧插值、基于kNN搜索的合成、pix2pix-turbo、光流跳帧以及知识蒸馏，并对每种方法的有效性进行了定量评估。最终，通过将蒸馏专用模型SDXS-512的CoreML转换与三线程摄像头流水线相结合，我们在512×512分辨率下实现了22.7 FPS的实时相机img2img变换。本研究的主要贡献在于系统性地证明：在CUDA上建立的优化见解在Apple Silicon的统一内存架构上并不一定有效。我们揭示了一个与NVIDIA GPU截然不同的优化格局——包括量化无加速效果、并行推理无效、神经引擎不适合大规模模型——并为Apple Silicon上的扩散模型推理提供了实用指导方针。

</details>


### [81] [EmoMind: Decoding Affective Captions from Human Brain fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16739)
*Bilal A. Mohammed,Lin Gu,Ruogo Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EmoMind 是首个从 fMRI 信号端到端解码情感字幕的流程，它先检索语义中性的场景描述，再用从同一 fMRI 记录解码得到的连续34维情感向量重写描述。通过无分类器引导实现语义保真与情感表达之间的平滑插值，并在三个验证轴上显著优于标签提示的 GPT-4。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有脑到文本系统主要恢复语义内容而丢弃情感信息，且语言模型生成情感文本时依赖离散标签，无法捕捉丰富的个体间情感变异性。

Method: EmoMind 首先从 fMRI 信号解码视觉特征并检索语义中性的场景描述，然后使用从同一 fMRI 记录解码的连续34维情感向量重写该描述。通过训练带有无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance）的重写器，并设置保持身份的空分支（identity-preserving null branch），实现语义保真与情感表达之间的可控平衡。

Result: 在两个独立情感 fMRI 数据集上，EmoMind 在三个验证轴（主体特异性、结构几何、因果控制）上均显著优于标签提示的 GPT-4，尤其在需要个体特异性情感结构的指标上优势最大。

Conclusion: 连续脑解码情感可作为个性化情感字幕生成的可行控制信号，为研究个体情感脑组织开辟了新方向。

Original Abstract: Decoding visual experience from brain activity has advanced substantially, but cur- rent brain-to-text systems largely recover semantic content while discarding affect. Additionally, language models can generate emotional text when prompted with categorical labels, but such labels collapse rich inter-subject variability into coarse discrete bins. We present EmoMind, the first end-to-end pipeline for decoding affective captions directly from fMRI signals. EmoMind first retrieves a semanti- cally grounded neutral scene description from brain-decoded visual features, then rewrites it using a continuous 34-dimensional emotion vector decoded from the same fMRI recording. To control the balance between content preservation and affective expression, we train the rewriter with classifier-free guidance against an identity-preserving null branch, enabling smooth interpolation between semantic fidelity and affective expressivity. We evaluate affective caption generation with a three-axis validation framework spanning subject-specificity, structural geometry, and causal control. We further augment this framework with a synthetic-brain substitution test that probes robustness to the measurement apparatus, and we benchmark each axis against GPT-4 prompted with brain-decoded top-5 emotion labels as a strong discrete baseline. Across two independent emotion fMRI datasets, EmoMind significantly outperforms label-prompted GPT-4 on all three axes, with the largest gains on metrics that require person-specific affective structure rather than population-level emotion aggregation. These results establish continuous brain-decoded affect as a viable control signal for individualized affective cap- tion generation and open new directions for studying individual affective brain organisation.

中文摘要翻译: 从大脑活动中解码视觉体验的技术已取得显著进展，但当前的脑到文本系统主要恢复语义内容而丢弃情感信息。此外，语言模型在被提示分类标签时可以生成情感文本，但这种标签将丰富的个体间变异压缩为粗粒度的离散类别。我们提出了 EmoMind，这是首个直接从 fMRI 信号端到端解码情感字幕的流程。EmoMind 首先从脑解码的视觉特征中检索语义中性的场景描述，然后使用从同一 fMRI 记录解码的连续34维情感向量重写该描述。为了控制内容保留与情感表达之间的平衡，我们训练重写器采用无分类器引导，并设置一个保持身份的空分支，从而实现语义保真度与情感表现力之间的平滑插值。我们通过一个三轴验证框架（涵盖主体特异性、结构几何和因果控制）评估情感字幕生成。我们进一步在此框架中增加了合成脑替代测试，用于探究对测量装置的鲁棒性，并以 GPT-4（提示脑解码的前5个情感标签）作为强离散基线对每个轴进行基准测试。在两个独立的情感 fMRI 数据集上，EmoMind 在所有三个轴上都显著优于标签提示的 GPT-4，其中在需要个体特异性情感结构而非群体水平情感聚合的指标上提升最大。这些结果确立了连续脑解码情感作为个性化情感字幕生成的可行控制信号，并为研究个体情感脑组织开辟了新方向。

</details>


### [82] [Reducing Credit Assignment Variance via Counterfactual Reasoning Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16302)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Yeling Peng,Youwei Wang,Guoxiong Zhou,Zijian Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出隐式行为策略优化（IBPO），通过反事实比较的信用分配框架，将稀疏终端奖励转化为步骤级学习信号，提升大语言模型多步推理的稳定性和性能上限。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在多步推理中使用稀疏终端奖励，导致信用分配差、梯度方差高、训练不稳定，阻碍模型持续改进。

Method: 提出基于反事实比较的信用分配框架，对同一输入采样多条推理轨迹，利用轨迹差异隐式近似替代决策，构造隐式过程级优势估计器，将稀疏终端奖励转化为步骤敏感的学习信号，并据此提出隐式行为策略优化（IBPO）算法。

Result: 在数学推理和代码推理基准上，IBPO显著提升了训练稳定性和性能上限。

Conclusion: IBPO为释放大语言模型性能潜力提供了有前景的方向。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning for multi-step reasoning with large language models (LLMs) often relies on sparse terminal rewards, leading to poor credit assignment conditions where the final feedback is evenly propagated across all intermediate decisions. This results in high gradient variance, unstable training, and numerous ineffective updates, ultimately causing the model to fail and preventing sustained improvement. We introduce a counterfactual comparison-based credit assignment framework, which samples multiple reasoning trajectories under the same input. By treating their differences as an implicit approximation of alternative decisions, we construct an implicit process-level advantage estimator that transforms sparse terminal rewards into step-sensitive learning signals. Based on this, we propose Implicit Behavior Policy Optimization (IBPO), which significantly improves training stability and performance upper bounds on mathematical and code reasoning benchmarks, pointing to a promising direction for unlocking the performance potential of LLMs.

中文摘要翻译: 针对大语言模型（LLMs）多步推理的强化学习通常依赖稀疏的终端奖励，导致信用分配条件差——最终反馈被均匀地传播到所有中间决策上。这带来了高梯度方差、不稳定训练以及大量无效更新，最终导致模型失败并阻碍持续改进。我们引入了一种基于反事实比较的信用分配框架，对同一输入采样多条推理轨迹。通过将轨迹间的差异视为替代决策的隐式近似，我们构造了一个隐式过程级优势估计器，将稀疏终端奖励转化为步骤敏感的学习信号。在此基础上，我们提出了隐式行为策略优化（IBPO），该方法在数学推理和代码推理基准上显著提升了训练稳定性和性能上限，为释放大语言模型的性能潜力指出了有前景的方向。

</details>


### [83] [When Actions Disappear: Adversarial Action Removal in Self-Play Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16312)
*Arahan Kujur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了自我对弈强化学习中的对抗性动作掩蔽攻击，即攻击者选择性移除受害者的合法动作。实验表明，这种攻击比随机掩蔽和对抗性扰动造成更大损害，且在不同算法间具有迁移性，揭示了动作可用性是自我对弈RL中一个独特的鲁棒性表面。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有对抗攻击研究多关注观测或动作扰动，而动作移除（掩蔽）这一更根本的威胁尚未被充分探索。作者旨在揭示自我对弈强化学习中，攻击者通过直接移除合法动作来破坏智能体性能的机制与影响。

Method: 提出对抗性动作掩蔽方法，攻击者通过强化学习自我对弈的方式学习选择性移除受害者的合法动作。在多种扑克游戏（信息状态规模从6到5,531）及两个非扑克领域中进行实验，对比了Q-learning、PPO、NFSP、神经NFSP、DQN等多种受害者算法。

Result: 学习式掩蔽比随机掩蔽和对抗性扰动造成显著更大的性能损失；攻击在多种算法间具有迁移性；自我对弈会放大攻击效果；即使经过扩展的掩蔽训练，受害者也无法恢复；攻击者会针对高价值决策点，这可通过可达加权条件动作容量（CAC$_w$）和值加权CAC$_v$来刻画。

Conclusion: 动作可用性是自我对弈强化学习中一个独立的鲁棒性攻击面，现有的对抗训练方法无法有效防御动作掩蔽攻击。

Original Abstract: We study adversarial action masking in self-play reinforcement learning: an attacker selectively removes legal actions from a victim's action set. Unlike observation or action perturbations, removal eliminates decision options before the agent acts. Across poker games scaling from 6 to 5,531 information states and two non-poker domains, learned masking causes substantially more damage than random masking and learned perturbation baselines. The attack persists across Q-learning, PPO, NFSP, neural NFSP, and DQN victims; transfers across agents; is amplified by self-play; and shows no recovery under extended masked training. Mechanistically, the adversary targets high-value decision points, captured by reach-weighted contingent action capacity (CAC$_w$) and a value-weighted refinement CAC$_v$. These results identify action availability as a distinct robustness surface in self-play RL.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了自我对弈强化学习中的对抗性动作掩蔽：攻击者选择性移除受害者动作集合中的合法动作。与观测扰动或动作扰动不同，动作移除会在智能体执行动作之前消除其决策选项。在信息状态规模从6到5,531不等的扑克游戏以及两个非扑克领域的实验中，学习式掩蔽比随机掩蔽和学习式扰动基线造成了显著更大的损害。该攻击在Q-learning、PPO、NFSP、神经NFSP和DQN等受害者模型上均有效；可在不同智能体间迁移；自我对弈会放大攻击效果；且在扩展的掩蔽训练下未见恢复。从机制上看，对抗者针对高价值决策点，这可通过可达加权条件动作容量（CAC$_w$）和值加权改进版CAC$_v$来刻画。这些结果揭示了动作可用性是自我对弈强化学习中一个独特的鲁棒性表面。

</details>


### [84] [A Structural Threshold in Decision Capacity Governs Collapse in Self-Play Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16315)
*Arahan Kujur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在自对弈强化学习中，决策容量存在一个临界阈值：消除所有正向可达的随机性决策点会导致策略快速崩溃至近乎最大损失的确定性地吸引子，而保留至少一个此类决策点即可避免崩溃。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究自对弈强化学习智能体在非对称规则扰动下是否会发生策略崩溃，以及崩溃发生的条件与机制。

Method: 通过消除所有正向可达的随机性决策点（positive-reach contingent decisions）来扰动自对弈学习过程，在多种扑克变体、矩阵博弈、骰子游戏和多种学习算法上进行实验，并设置冻结基线和固定对手对照实验。

Result: 消除所有正向可达的随机性决策点导致策略快速收敛到确定性地吸引子（近最大损失固定点）；保留至少一个此类决策点即可防止崩溃；该现象时间不变、可逆，且在函数近似下加剧；规模化程度通过可达加权决策容量连续变化。

Conclusion: 自对弈强化学习存在一个明确的决策容量临界阈值（零可达加权随机性决策容量），该阈值决定了是否发生策略崩溃，且严重程度随可达加权容量连续变化。

Original Abstract: We show that a threshold in decision capacity determines whether self-play reinforcement learning agents collapse under asymmetric rule perturbations. Across poker variants, matrix games, a dice game, and multiple learning algorithms, eliminating all positive-reach contingent decisions causes rapid convergence to a deterministic exploitation attractor, a fixed point at near-maximal loss. Preserving even a single positive-reach contingent decision point prevents this collapse. A frozen baseline and fixed-opponent control confirm that the mechanism is co-adaptation under constraint, not the perturbation itself. The phenomenon is timing-invariant, fully reversible upon action restoration, and intensifies under function approximation. These results establish a sharp threshold at zero reach-weighted contingent action capacity, with severity scaling continuously via reach-weighted capacity in the tested domains.

中文摘要翻译: 我们证明，自对弈强化学习智能体在非对称规则扰动下是否发生崩溃，取决于一个决策容量阈值。在多种扑克变体、矩阵博弈、骰子游戏以及多种学习算法上，消除所有正向可达的随机性决策点会导致策略快速收敛到一个确定性地吸引子——一个处于近最大损失的固定点。而保留哪怕一个正向可达的随机性决策点即可防止这种崩溃。冻结基线和固定对手对照实验证实，该机制是约束下的共适应现象，而非扰动本身所致。该现象具有时间不变性，在恢复动作后可完全逆转，并在函数近似下加剧。这些结果在零可达加权随机性决策容量处确立了一个尖锐阈值，其严重程度在测试领域内随可达加权容量连续变化。

</details>


### [85] [Forecasting Medium-Horizon Alzheimer's Disease Progression: Residual Gap-Aware Transformers for 24-Month CDR-SB Change from ADNI Clinical and Biomarker Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16319)
*Ran Tong,Tong Wang,Lanruo Wang,Yin Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于锚点分析和残差间隙感知Transformer的模型，用于中度时间跨度的阿尔茨海默病进展预测，在ADNI数据上相比基线方法显著降低了MSE并提升了相关性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 中度时间跨度的阿尔茨海默病进展预测具有挑战性，因为未来临床评分仍与基线严重程度相关，而生物标志物历史数据不规则且存在缺失观测。

Method: 提出残差间隙感知Transformer：1）基于混合效应的统计参考模型（含参与者级随机截距）；2）通过Transformer对锚点前的临床与生物标志物历史进行残差学习；3）使用观测级三元组标记化处理不规则历史；4）在自注意力中引入可学习的非负时间间隙惩罚。

Result: 在5次参与者级随机种子划分下，相比混合效应基线，MSE降低13.1%，预测-观测相关系数提升26.4%；同时在均方误差和相关性上均优于GRU-D和STraTS。

Conclusion: 统计锚定和间隙感知残差学习为中度时间跨度的阿尔茨海默病进展预测提供了有效的结构框架。

Original Abstract: Medium-horizon Alzheimer's disease progression prediction is difficult because future clinical scores can remain tied to baseline severity, while biomarker histories are irregular and incompletely observed. We develop an anchor-based analysis of 24-month Clinical Dementia Rating Sum of Boxes (CDR-SB) change using harmonized Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) tables. Each labeled sample is anchored at a mild cognitive impairment visit, uses only clinical and biomarker history observed at or before that anchor, and defines the response as CDR-SB at the future visit closest to 24 months within an 18--30 month window minus anchor CDR-SB. The analytic cohort contains 2,600 labeled anchors from 858 participants and 7,276 longitudinal rows. We propose a residual gap-aware transformer that combines a mixed-effects statistical reference with transformer-based residual learning from pre-anchor clinical and biomarker histories. The model uses participant-level random intercepts in the mixed-effects reference, observation-level triplet tokenization for irregular histories, and a learned nonnegative time-gap penalty inside self-attention. We compare the proposed model with a Bayesian-information-criterion-selected linear mixed-effects baseline, GRU-D, and STraTS under repeated participant-level train--test splits. Across five participant-level random seeds, the proposed model achieves the best mean test performance across all reported metrics, reducing MSE by 13.1% and increasing prediction--observation correlation by 26.4% relative to the mixed-effects baseline. It also improves over both GRU-D and STraTS in mean error and correlation. These results show that statistical anchoring and gap-aware residual learning provide a useful structure for medium-horizon Alzheimer's disease progression prediction.

中文摘要翻译: 中度时间跨度的阿尔茨海默病进展预测具有挑战性，因为未来临床评分仍与基线严重程度相关，而生物标志物历史数据不规则且不完整。我们开发了一种基于锚点分析的方法，使用统一的阿尔茨海默病神经影像学倡议（ADNI）数据表，对24个月的临床痴呆评定量表总和评分盒（CDR-SB）变化进行分析。每个标记样本以轻度认知障碍访视点为锚点，仅使用该锚点或之前观测到的临床和生物标志物历史，并将响应定义为未来访视点（在18至30个月窗口内最接近24个月）的CDR-SB减去锚点CDR-SB。分析队列包含来自858名参与者的2,600个标记锚点和7,276条纵向记录。我们提出了一种残差间隙感知Transformer，它将混合效应统计参考与基于锚点前临床和生物标志物历史的Transformer残差学习相结合。该模型在混合效应参考中使用参与者级随机截距，对不规则历史使用观测级三元组标记化，并在自注意力内部引入学习的非负时间间隙惩罚。我们将所提出模型与贝叶斯信息准则选择的线性混合效应基线、GRU-D和STraTS在重复的参与者级训练-测试划分下进行比较。在五个参与者级随机种子下，所提出模型在所有报告指标上均取得了最佳平均测试性能，相比混合效应基线将MSE降低了13.1%，并将预测-观测相关性提升了26.4%。它在均方误差和相关性方面也优于GRU-D和STraTS。这些结果表明，统计锚定和间隙感知残差学习为中度时间跨度的阿尔茨海默病进展预测提供了有用的结构框架。

</details>


### [86] [AdaGraph: A Graph-Native Clustering Algorithm That Overcomes the Curse of Dimensionality and Enables Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16320)
*Ahmed Elmahdi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaGraph是一种基于图拓扑的聚类算法，源于结构中心机器学习（SC-ML）新范式，通过kNN图拓扑聚类替代传统距离计算，从根本上解决维度灾难问题。它无需预先指定聚类数k，能原生处理噪声，并配合基于拓扑的聚类有效性指标Graph-SCOPE进行无监督调优。在基因共表达、文本聚类和材料科学三个领域的实验中，AdaGraph显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统基于距离的聚类算法在高维空间中因欧氏距离失效而遭受维度灾难，且需要预先指定聚类数k，对噪声敏感。论文提出结构中心机器学习（SC-ML）新范式，用基于拓扑的计算替代基于几何（距离）的计算，从根本上解决维度灾难问题。

Method: AdaGraph完全在kNN图拓扑内运行，利用高维空间中仍保留有意义关系结构的kNN图表示，无需事先指定聚类数k，原生处理噪声，并通过SLCD（Sample-Learn-Calibrate-Deploy）原型-部署框架实现可扩展性。其无监督调优目标采用Graph-SCOPE，一种基于拓扑的聚类有效性指标。

Result: 在维度d=10到d=5000的10个合成基准上，Graph-SCOPE平均ARI=0.900，在9/10数据集上正确选择k，优于Silhouette、Davies-Bouldin和Calinski-Harabasz指标；在所有维度上Kendall tau>=0.92（Silhouette仅约0.46）。在三个科学领域实验中：肝癌基因共表达发现中识别出WGCNA、ICA、NMF和Spectral Biclustering无法解析的条件特异性基因模块；文本聚类20NG-6cat上ARI=0.751，较HDBSCAN的0.464提升62%；材料科学三个数据集上均取得最高Graph-SCOPE。

Conclusion: AdaGraph作为一种图原生聚类算法，通过基于拓扑的结构中心学习范式有效克服了高维空间中的维度灾难问题，在多个科学领域的实验中展现出优于传统聚类方法的性能，为无监督学习提供了新的方向。

Original Abstract: We present AdaGraph, a graph-native clustering algorithm born from the Structure-Centric Machine Learning (SC-ML) paradigm -- a new field of unsupervised learning that replaces geometry-centric (distance-based) computation with structure-centric (topology-based) computation, fundamentally dissolving the curse of dimensionality. AdaGraph operates entirely within the kNN graph topology, a representation that retains meaningful relational structure in arbitrarily high dimensions where Euclidean distance metrics become uninformative. AdaGraph requires no a priori specification of the number of clusters k, handles noise natively, and scales via the SLCD (Sample-Learn-Calibrate-Deploy) prototype-deployment framework. As its unsupervised tuning objective, AdaGraph pairs with Graph-SCOPE, the topology-based cluster validity index introduced as a separate SC-ML contribution. On 10 synthetic benchmarks spanning d=10 to d=5000, Graph-SCOPE achieves mean ARI=0.900 and correctly selects k on 9/10 datasets -- outperforming Silhouette, Davies-Bouldin, and Calinski-Harabasz -- while maintaining Kendall tau >= 0.92 with ground-truth cluster quality across all dimensionalities (Silhouette: tau ~= 0.46). We validate AdaGraph across three scientific domains: (1) gene co-expression discovery in hepatocellular carcinoma (GSE14520, 10,000 genes, 488 patients, no dimensionality reduction), where AdaGraph identifies condition-specific gene modules that WGCNA, ICA, NMF, and Spectral Biclustering fail to resolve; (2) natural language text clustering, where AdaGraph achieves ARI=0.751 on 20NG-6cat versus HDBSCAN's 0.464 (62% relative improvement); (3) materials science clustering of superconductors (145-dimensional Magpie features), perovskites, and JARVIS-DFT materials, where AdaGraph achieves the highest Graph-SCOPE on all three datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出AdaGraph，一种图原生聚类算法，诞生于结构中心机器学习（SC-ML）范式——这是一个新的无监督学习领域，用基于结构（拓扑）的计算替代基于几何（距离）的计算，从根本上消解了维度灾难。AdaGraph完全在kNN图拓扑内运行，这种表示在任意高维空间中仍能保留有意义的关系结构，而高维空间中欧氏距离度量已失去信息价值。AdaGraph无需预先指定聚类数k，能原生处理噪声，并通过SLCD（Sample-Learn-Calibrate-Deploy）原型-部署框架实现可扩展性。作为其无监督调优目标，AdaGraph与Graph-SCOPE配对，后者是作为独立的SC-ML贡献引入的基于拓扑的聚类有效性指标。在维度范围d=10到d=5000的10个合成基准上，Graph-SCOPE实现了平均ARI=0.900，并在9/10个数据集上正确选择了k——优于轮廓系数、Davies-Bouldin和Calinski-Harabasz指标——同时在所有维度上保持与真实聚类质量的Kendall tau>=0.92（轮廓系数：tau约0.46）。我们在三个科学领域验证了AdaGraph：（1）肝细胞癌的基因共表达发现（GSE14520，10,000个基因，488名患者，无降维处理），AdaGraph识别出了WGCNA、ICA、NMF和Spectral Biclustering无法解析的条件特异性基因模块；（2）自然语言文本聚类，AdaGraph在20NG-6cat上达到ARI=0.751，而HDBSCAN为0.464（相对提升62%）；（3）超导体（145维Magpie特征）、钙钛矿和JARVIS-DFT材料的材料科学聚类，AdaGraph在所有三个数据集上均取得了最高的Graph-SCOPE。

</details>


### [87] [Orth-Dion: Eliminating Geometric Mismatch in Distributed Low-Rank Spectral Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16341)
*Tatsuhiro Nakamori,Laura Gomezjurado Gonzalez,Ganesh Talluri,Ansh Tiwari,Hideyuki Kawashima,Ioannis Mitliagkas,Guillaume Rabusseau,Hiroki Naganuma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Orth-Dion 方法，通过将 Dion 中的列归一化替换为 QR 正交化，解决了低秩梯度压缩中因几何失配导致的收敛速度损失问题，在保持相同通信成本下匹配全秩谱方法的收敛速率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Dion 方法虽能兼容全分片数据并行训练，但收敛速度慢于全秩谱方法。作者发现这一差距源于几何原因：列归一化无法得到低秩谱几何所需的极因子，导致方向违反了对偶范数约束，收敛速率额外增加了 √r 因子。

Method: 提出 Orth-Dion，将 Dion 中对右因子的列归一化替换为 QR 正交化，使得低秩因子满足极因子约束。理论分析采用自洽不动点论证消除了误差反馈中常见的有限漂移假设，并使用时间平均收缩技术使误差序列仅在平均意义上收缩。

Result: 在非欧几里得光滑性假设下，Orth-Dion 达到 O(√(L_r/T)) 的收敛速率，匹配精确谱方法；实验验证了 √r 的缩放规律，并表明 Orth-Dion 在 Dion 的通信成本下弥合了与 Muon 的收敛差距。

Conclusion: Orth-Dion 通过 QR 正交化修复了几何失配问题，在不增加通信开销的前提下实现了与全秩谱方法相当的收敛性能。

Original Abstract: Low-rank gradient compression reduces communication in distributed training by representing updates with rank-$r$ factors. Dion is a recent method that approximates Muon, a spectral optimizer that orthogonalizes momentum, using one step of power iteration followed by column normalization (rescaling each column of the right factor to unit length). This makes it compatible with fully sharded data parallel training, but it converges more slowly than full-rank spectral methods. We show that this gap is geometric: column normalization does not yield the rank-$r$ polar factor that Muon implicitly targets, so the resulting direction violates the dual-norm constraint of the low-rank spectral geometry, and the rate picks up an extra factor of $\sqrt{r}$ even though the low-rank approximation of the gradient itself is accurate. The same mismatch enters the smoothness term and the error-feedback recursion in the analysis, which has a knock-on effect on empirical performance. We propose Orth-Dion, which replaces column normalization with QR orthogonalization of the right factor. Under non-Euclidean smoothness, with $L_r$ the curvature constant along rank-$r$ directions, Orth-Dion attains rate $O(\sqrt{L_r/T})$, matching exact spectral methods at the same per-step communication cost as Dion. The proof removes the bounded-drift assumption common in prior error-feedback analyses via a self-consistent fixed-point argument, and uses a time-averaged contraction that only requires the error sequence to contract on average rather than at every step. Experiments on large-scale language model pre-training validate the predicted $\sqrt{r}$ scaling and show that Orth-Dion closes the convergence gap to Muon at Dion's communication cost.

中文摘要翻译: 低秩梯度压缩通过用秩-r因子表示更新量来减少分布式训练中的通信开销。Dion 是一种近期提出的方法，它通过一步幂迭代后接列归一化（将右因子的每一列重新缩放到单位长度）来近似 Muon——一种对动量进行正交化的谱优化器。这使得 Dion 兼容全分片数据并行训练，但其收敛速度慢于全秩谱方法。我们证明这一差距源于几何原因：列归一化无法得到 Muon 隐式目标中的秩-r极因子，因此所得方向违反了低秩谱几何的对偶范数约束，即使梯度本身的低秩近似是精确的，收敛速率仍然额外增加了 √r 因子。同样的失配还会影响平滑项和误差反馈递归中的分析，进而对实证性能产生连锁效应。我们提出 Orth-Dion，将列归一化替换为右因子的 QR 正交化。在非欧几里得光滑性假设下，若 L_r 为沿秩-r方向的曲率常数，Orth-Dion 达到 O(√(L_r/T)) 的收敛速率，在每步通信成本与 Dion 相同的情况下匹配精确谱方法。理论证明通过自洽不动点论证消除了先前误差反馈分析中常见的有限漂移假设，并使用时间平均收缩技术，仅要求误差序列在平均意义上收缩而非每步收缩。大规模语言模型预训练实验验证了预测的 √r 缩放规律，并表明 Orth-Dion 在 Dion 的通信成本下弥合了与 Muon 的收敛差距。

</details>


### [88] [LoopQ: Quantization for Recursive Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16343)
*Rui Fang,Hsi-Wen Chen,Ming-Syan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了循环语言模型（LoopLM）在后训练量化（PTQ）中的脆弱性问题，并提出LoopQ框架——一种循环感知的PTQ方法，通过激活缩放、选择性变换、跨循环状态对齐和轨迹感知优化，在W4A4量化下平均下游准确率提升68.8%，困惑度降低87.7%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 循环语言模型（LoopLM）通过递归复用Transformer块来提升参数效率，但这种参数复用使得模型在后训练量化（PTQ）中更加脆弱，而此前尚无针对LoopLM量化的系统研究。

Method: 提出LoopQ框架，包含四个关键技术：1）激活缩放（activation scaling）；2）选择性变换（selective transformation）；3）跨循环状态对齐（cross-loop state alignment）；4）轨迹感知优化（trajectory-aware optimization）。该方法在保持共享量化骨干的同时引入轻量级适配，以减少循环内的分布失配和跨循环的误差累积。

Result: 在七个基准测试上的实验表明，在W4A4量化条件下，与最强的静态PTQ基线相比，LoopQ将平均下游准确率提升了68.8%，平均困惑度降低了87.7%。

Conclusion: LoopQ有效解决了LoopLM在PTQ中的三个关键挑战（角色间分布偏移、循环转换间的状态复用、递归误差累积），显著提升了量化后的模型性能。

Original Abstract: Looped language models (LoopLMs) improve parameter efficiency by recursively reusing Transformer blocks, enabling deeper computation under a fixed model size. However, this reuse makes LoopLMs more fragile under post-training quantization (PTQ). We present the first systematic study of quantization in LoopLMs and identify three challenges: distribution shift across roles, state reuse across loop transitions, and recursive error accumulation. To address these challenges, we propose LoopQ, a loop-aware PTQ framework that preserves a shared quantized backbone while introducing lightweight adaptations. LoopQ combines activation scaling, selective transformation, cross-loop state alignment, and trajectory-aware optimization to reduce distributional mismatch within loops and error accumulation across loops. Experiments across seven benchmarks show that, under W4A4 quantization, LoopQ improves average downstream accuracy by 68.8% and reduces average perplexity by 87.7% compared with the strongest static PTQ baseline.

中文摘要翻译: 循环语言模型（LoopLM）通过递归复用Transformer块来提升参数效率，从而在固定模型尺寸下实现更深层的计算。然而，这种复用使得LoopLM在后训练量化（PTQ）下更加脆弱。我们首次对LoopLM中的量化问题进行了系统性研究，并识别出三个挑战：角色间分布偏移、循环转换间的状态复用以及递归误差累积。为解决这些问题，我们提出LoopQ——一种循环感知的PTQ框架，它在保持共享量化骨干的同时引入轻量级适配。LoopQ结合了激活缩放、选择性变换、跨循环状态对齐和轨迹感知优化，以减少循环内的分布失配和跨循环的误差累积。在七个基准测试上的实验表明，在W4A4量化条件下，与最强的静态PTQ基线相比，LoopQ将平均下游准确率提升了68.8%，平均困惑度降低了87.7%。

</details>


### [89] [Goal-Conditioned Supervised Learning for LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16345)
*Shijun Li,Kaiwen Dong,Xiang Gao,Joydeep Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于目标条件监督学习（GCSL）的离线微调框架，通过将反馈信号直接作为显式目标，并引入跨质量阈值的目标公式，使LLM仅通过监督学习即可实现比SFT和DPO更好的对齐效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LLM对齐方法中，在线方法（如基于RL的对齐）成本高、部署难；离线方法（如SFT和DPO）存在局限：SFT将分级反馈压缩为二元监督，DPO依赖难以获取的配对偏好数据。因此需要一种既高效又无需复杂数据要求的离线对齐方法。

Method: 提出目标条件监督学习（GCSL）框架，将反馈信号直接作为显式目标进行监督学习。引入新的目标公式：学习持续追求超过目标质量阈值的结果，而非模仿选定的高质量子集。同时提出自然语言目标表示方法以利用LLM的语义理解能力。

Result: 在无毒性生成、代码生成和LLM推荐三个任务上，该方法一致性地优于标准离线微调基线（SFT、DPO），同时保持了监督学习的效率、可扩展性和简单的数据需求。

Conclusion: GCSL作为一种离线微调框架，通过创新的目标设计和监督学习范式，有效克服了SFT和DPO的局限性，为LLM对齐提供了一种高效、可扩展且数据要求简单的替代方案。

Original Abstract: Large language models often require fine-tuning to better align their behavior with user intent at deployment. Existing approaches are commonly divided into online and offline paradigms. Online methods, such as RL-based alignment, can directly optimize outcome quality but typically rely on external reward models and iterative rollouts, making them costly and difficult to deploy in many cases. Offline methods are more efficient, but prevailing approaches such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and direct preference optimization (DPO) remain limited: SFT typically collapses graded feedback into binary supervision, while DPO depends on paired preference data that is often unavailable or expensive to construct.
  In this paper, we propose goal-conditioned supervised learning (GCSL) as an offline fine-tuning framework for LLMs. Our core idea is to treat feedback signals directly as an explicit goal and train the model, purely through supervised learning, to generate responses that achieve that goal. To better exploit graded feedback, we further introduce a novel goal formulation that defines learning as consistently pursuing outcomes above a target quality threshold, rather than imitating samples from a selected high-quality subset. This design mitigates the bounded-learning effect of SFT and classic GCSL by explicitly guiding the model to learn the directional progression of quality. We also propose natural-language goal representations to better leverage the semantic understanding and reasoning capabilities of LLMs. We evaluate our method on three tasks: non-toxic generation, code generation, and LLM for recommendation. Results show that our approach consistently outperforms standard offline fine-tuning baselines while retaining the efficiency, scalability, and simple data requirements of supervised learning.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型通常需要微调以使其行为更好地对齐用户意图。现有方法通常分为在线范式和离线范式。在线方法（如基于强化学习的对齐）可以直接优化结果质量，但通常依赖外部奖励模型和迭代 rollout，导致成本高昂且在许多情况下难以部署。离线方法更高效，但主流方法如监督微调（SFT）和直接偏好优化（DPO）仍存在局限：SFT通常将分级反馈压缩为二元监督，而DPO依赖于配对偏好数据，这类数据往往难以获取或构建成本高昂。本文提出目标条件监督学习（GCSL）作为LLM的离线微调框架。核心思想是将反馈信号直接作为显式目标，通过纯粹的监督学习训练模型生成达成该目标的响应。为更好地利用分级反馈，我们进一步引入一种新颖的目标公式，将学习定义为持续追求超过目标质量阈值的结果，而非模仿来自选定高质量子集的样本。该设计通过显式引导模型学习质量的定向提升，缓解了SFT和经典GCSL的有界学习效应。我们还提出自然语言目标表示方法，以更好地利用LLM的语义理解和推理能力。我们在无毒性生成、代码生成和LLM推荐三个任务上进行了评估。结果表明，我们的方法在保持监督学习效率、可扩展性和简单数据要求的同时，一致性地优于标准离线微调基线。

</details>


### [90] [PropGuard: Safeguarding LLM-MAS via Propagation-Aware Exploration and Remediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16346)
*Bingyu Yan,Xiaoming Zhang,Jinyu Hou,Chaozhuo Li,Ziyi Zhou,Xiaozhe Zhang,Litian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PropGuard 是一个针对 LLM 多智能体系统的传播感知防御框架，通过构建双视图时空图、利用 GE-GRPO 训练的顺序检测器恢复可疑传播子图，并结合子图感知诊断与源引导修复，有效降低攻击成功率并保持高任务级防御成功率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的多智能体系统（LLM-MAS）在协作过程中存在安全风险，恶意指令可通过消息、工具或记忆跨智能体和轮次传播，导致系统级损害。现有防御方法（如局部过滤或基于图的异常检测）无法追踪细粒度传播路径，也无法在不干扰良性协作的情况下修复被污染状态。

Method: PropGuard 提出双视图时空图，融合响应中心风险估计与完整状态证据保存；基于风险先验，使用 GE-GRPO 训练的检测器顺序探索全状态图以恢复紧凑的可疑传播子图；通过子图感知诊断验证有害传播，并应用源引导修复来纠正上游污染并重放受影响的下游交互。
