Result: 在四种通信架构和五种攻击设置下的实验表明，PropGuard 能够持续降低攻击成功率，同时保持高任务级防御成功率，实现了效果与效率的良好平衡。

Conclusion: PropGuard 是一种传播感知的 LLM-MAS 安全防御框架，能够有效追踪和修复恶意指令的传播路径，在保证任务性能的同时显著提升系统安全性。

Original Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS) have become a promising paradigm for solving complex tasks through role specialization, tool use, memory, and collaborative reasoning. However, these interactions create new security risks that malicious instructions injected through messages, tools, or memories can propagate across agents and rounds, causing system-level compromise. Existing defenses largely rely on local filtering or graph-based anomaly detection, but they often fail to trace fine-grained propagation paths or remediate contaminated states without disrupting benign collaboration. We propose PropGuard, a propagation-aware framework for safeguarding LLM-MAS. PropGuard constructs a dual-view spatio-temporal graph that combines response-centric risk estimation with full-state evidence preservation. Guided by these risk priors, a GE-GRPO trained inspector sequentially explores the full-state graph to recover compact suspicious propagation subgraphs. PropGuard then verifies harmful propagation through subgraph-aware diagnosis and applies source-guided remediation to correct upstream contamination and replay affected downstream interactions. Experiments across four communication architectures and five attack settings demonstrate that PropGuard consistently lowers attack success while maintaining high task-level defense success, achieving a favorable effectiveness--efficiency trade-off.

中文摘要翻译: 基于大语言模型的多智能体系统（LLM-MAS）通过角色专业化、工具使用、记忆和协作推理，已成为解决复杂任务的有前景范式。然而，这些交互带来了新的安全风险：通过消息、工具或记忆注入的恶意指令能够在智能体和回合之间传播，导致系统级损害。现有防御主要依赖局部过滤或基于图的异常检测，但往往无法追踪细粒度的传播路径，也无法在不中断良性协作的情况下修复被污染状态。我们提出 PropGuard，一个传播感知的 LLM-MAS 安全防护框架。PropGuard 构建了一个双视图时空图，将响应中心的风险估计与完整状态证据保存相结合。在这些风险先验的引导下，一个经过 GE-GRPO 训练的检测器顺序探索全状态图，以恢复紧凑的可疑传播子图。随后，PropGuard 通过子图感知诊断验证有害传播，并应用源引导修复来纠正上游污染，重放受影响的下游交互。在四种通信架构和五种攻击设置下的实验表明，PropGuard 能够持续降低攻击成功率，同时保持高任务级防御成功率，实现了效果与效率的良好平衡。

</details>


### [91] [HPC-LLM: Practical Domain Adaptation and Retrieval-Augmented Generation for HPC Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16347)
*Nourin Shahin,Izzat Alsmadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 HPC-LLM，一个基于检索增强与领域适配的助手，专门支持 HPC 工作流（如 Slurm 调度、MPI 执行、GPU 使用等），通过 QLoRA 微调 Llama 3.1 8B 并构建 HPC 语料库，使小模型在性能上接近大模型，同时显著降低 GPU 资源需求。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 研究人员在使用 HPC 基础设施（集群、调度器、GPU、并行框架）时面临显著操作障碍，而通用大语言模型虽能提供编码帮助，但缺乏 HPC 领域特定的运维知识，无法可靠支持 HPC 工作流。

Method: 采用检索增强生成（RAG）与领域适配结合的框架：自动文档摄入、稠密检索、使用 QLoRA 进行轻量化领域微调、本地推理，构建模块化编排流水线。从大学 HPC 文档、操作示例和合成指令-答案对中构建约 9,000–24,000 条 HPC 训练数据，微调 Llama 3.1 8B。

Result: 微调后的 8B 模型在 JetStream2 基础设施上，在检索增强设置下性能与显著更大的通用模型（如 Qwen 2.5 14B）相当，同时 GPU 内存需求和推理延迟大幅降低。

Conclusion: 通过检索增强与轻量化领域适配，小规模专用模型可以在 HPC 任务上达到接近大模型的性能，兼顾效率与效果，为 HPC 领域的 LLM 应用提供了可行的低成本方案。

Original Abstract: Modern scientific research increasingly depends on High-Performance Computing (HPC) infrastructures, yet many researchers face significant operational barriers when interacting with cluster environments, job schedulers, GPU resources, and parallel computing frameworks. General-purpose large language models (LLMs) provide useful coding assistance but often lack the domain-specific operational knowledge required for reliable HPC support. This paper presents HPC-LLM, a retrieval augmented and domain-adapted assistant designed to support common HPC workflows including Slurm scheduling, MPI execution, GPU utilization, filesystem management, and cluster troubleshooting. The proposed framework integrates automated documentation ingestion, dense retrieval, lightweight domain adaptation using QLoRA, and local inference within a modular orchestration pipeline. To support domain adaptation, we construct an HPC-oriented corpus from publicly available university HPC documentation, curated operational examples, and synthetic instruction-answer pairs generated from retrieved HPC content. The resulting dataset contains approximately 9,000 to 24,000 HPC-focused training examples spanning job scheduling, GPU computing, distributed training, storage systems, and cluster administration topics. We fine-tune Llama 3.1 8B using QLoRA and evaluate the resulting model against several open weight baselines under retrieval-augmented settings on JetStream2 infrastructure. Experimental results indicate that the adapted 8B model achieves performance comparable to substantially larger general-purpose models while operating under significantly lower GPU memory requirements and inference latency. In particular, the adapted model approaches the performance of Qwen 2.5 14B while requiring substantially fewer computational resources.

中文摘要翻译: 现代科学研究日益依赖高性能计算（HPC）基础设施，然而许多研究人员在与集群环境、作业调度器、GPU 资源以及并行计算框架交互时，常常面临显著的操作障碍。通用大语言模型（LLM）虽能提供有用的编码辅助，但往往缺乏可靠支持 HPC 所需的领域特定运维知识。本文提出了 HPC-LLM，一个基于检索增强和领域适配的助手，专为支持常见 HPC 工作流而设计，包括 Slurm 调度、MPI 执行、GPU 利用、文件系统管理以及集群故障排除。所提出的框架集成了自动文档摄入、稠密检索、基于 QLoRA 的轻量化领域适配以及本地推理，构成一个模块化的编排流水线。为支持领域适配，我们基于公开的大学 HPC 文档、精选的操作示例以及从检索到的 HPC 内容生成的合成指令-答案对，构建了一个面向 HPC 的语料库。最终数据集包含约 9,000 到 24,000 条聚焦 HPC 的训练样本，涵盖作业调度、GPU 计算、分布式训练、存储系统和集群管理等多个主题。我们使用 QLoRA 对 Llama 3.1 8B 进行微调，并在 JetStream2 基础设施上，在检索增强设置下将结果模型与多个开源基线模型进行对比评估。实验结果表明，适配后的 8B 模型在显著降低的 GPU 内存需求和推理延迟条件下，达到了与规模大得多的通用模型相媲美的性能。特别地，该适配模型在所需计算资源大幅减少的情况下，性能接近 Qwen 2.5 14B。

</details>


### [92] [Flow-Direct: Feedback-Efficient and Reusable Guidance for Flow Models via Non-Parametric Guidance Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16348)
*Kim Yong Tan,Yueming Lyu,Ivor Tsang,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flow-Direct提出了一种通过持久化引导场（persistent guidance field）来指导扩散/流模型生成过程的无训练框架，利用非参数估计器聚合所有奖励评估样本，实现高效反馈利用和多目标组合生成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无训练引导方法在反馈效率上存在严重不足：奖励反馈仅被临时用于局部梯度近似或离散搜索决策，之后便被丢弃，导致大量奖励信息被浪费。

Method: 从理论上推导出基于基分布与奖励加权目标分布之间对数密度比的引导场，该场将预训练分布迁移到目标分布；实践中采用非参数估计器，从所有累积的奖励评估样本中构建该引导场，并随着样本增多而不断优化。

Result: 1) 高反馈效率：每个评估样本都用于优化全局引导场，不浪费任何奖励信息；2) 天然可复用性：优化完成后，收集的数据集可直接用于生成新样本而无需额外奖励评估，多个引导场可组合实现多目标生成。

Conclusion: Flow-Direct通过持久化而非临时性的引导机制，从根本上解决了无训练引导的反馈效率问题，并赋予框架可复用和可组合的额外优势。

Original Abstract: Training-free guidance enables pre-trained diffusion and flow models to optimize application-specific objectives using feedback from external black-box reward functions. However, existing methods are feedback-inefficient because reward feedback is used only transiently to inform a localized gradient approximation or a discrete search decision, and is subsequently discarded. To address this limitation, we propose Flow-Direct, a framework that guides the generation process via a persistent guidance field. Theoretically, this guidance field is analytically derived from the log-density ratio between the base and reward-weighted target distributions; it transports the pre-trained distribution to the target distribution. In practice, the field is implemented as a non-parametric estimator constructed from all accumulated reward-evaluated samples. As more samples are collected during optimization, this empirical guidance field becomes increasingly accurate. This persistent formulation yields two major advantages. First, Flow-Direct is highly feedback-efficient: because every evaluated sample is used to refine the global guidance field, no reward information is wasted. Second, the framework is naturally reusable: once optimization is complete, the collected dataset defines a reusable guidance field for generating novel target samples without additional reward evaluations, and distinct guidance fields can be combined to generate samples that simultaneously satisfy multiple objectives.

中文摘要翻译: 无训练引导使得预训练扩散模型和流模型能够利用外部黑箱奖励函数的反馈来优化特定应用的目标。然而，现有方法的反馈效率低下，因为奖励反馈仅被临时用于局部梯度近似或离散搜索决策，随后便被丢弃。针对这一局限，我们提出Flow-Direct框架，通过持久化引导场（persistent guidance field）来指导生成过程。理论上，该引导场从基分布与奖励加权目标分布之间的对数密度比解析推导得出，能够将预训练分布迁移至目标分布。实践中，该场通过一种非参数估计器实现，该估计器由所有累积的奖励评估样本构建而成。随着优化过程中收集的样本增多，这一经验引导场的精度不断提升。这种持久化的形式带来了两大优势。首先，Flow-Direct具有极高的反馈效率：由于每个评估样本都被用于优化全局引导场，没有任何奖励信息被浪费。其次，该框架天然具有可复用性：一旦优化完成，收集的数据集便定义了一个可复用的引导场，无需额外的奖励评估即可生成新的目标样本；此外，不同的引导场可以组合使用，以生成同时满足多个目标的样本。

</details>


### [93] [Geometric Asymmetry in MoE Specialization: Functional Decorrelation and Representational Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16349)
*Feilong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的Jacobian-PCA-Grassmann框架来分析MoE层的几何结构，发现专家之间存在功能去相关与表征子空间重叠共存的非对称结构，且路由稀疏性是塑造该几何结构的关键因素。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 混合专家（MoE）架构通过稀疏路由实现了可扩展的容量，但专家专业化的几何结构仍未被充分理解。

Method: 提出统一的Jacobian-PCA-Grassmann框架，在函数空间和表示空间两个层面分析MoE层的几何结构。通过跨专家Jacobian对齐度量功能去相关，通过子空间分析度量表示重叠，并进行控制路由实验（对比top-k路由与全软路由）。

Result: 专家表现出强功能去相关（跨专家Jacobian对齐度持续低值接近零），同时其路由表示占据不同但部分重叠的子空间；top-k路由导致更清晰的功能分离和更大的子空间散度，而全软路由导致更纠缠的专家结构。

Conclusion: MoE层可被解释为在共享表示流形上实现局部去相关算子的重叠子流形，该工作为研究现代Transformer中的条件计算提供了通用的诊断框架。

Original Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures achieve scalable capacity through sparse routing, yet the geometric structure of expert specialization remains poorly understood. We introduce a unified Jacobian-PCA-Grassmann framework for analyzing MoE layers in both function space and representation space. Across pretrained MoE Transformers (Mistral, Qwen), we find a consistent structural asymmetry: experts exhibit strong functional decorrelation (consistently low, near-zero cross-expert Jacobian alignment) while their routed representations occupy distinct but partially overlapping subspaces. This indicates that functional decorrelation and representation overlap coexist rather than coincide in MoE specialization. Controlled routing experiments further indicate that routing sparsity appears to be a key factor shaping this geometry: top-k routing induces sharper functional separation and larger subspace divergence, whereas fully soft routing yields more entangled expert structure. Together, these results suggest a geometric interpretation in which MoE layers may be viewed as implementing locally decorrelated operators over overlapping submanifolds on a shared representation manifold, and provide a general diagnostic framework for studying conditional computation in modern Transformer architectures.

中文摘要翻译: 混合专家（MoE）架构通过稀疏路由实现了可扩展的容量，但专家专业化的几何结构仍未被充分理解。我们引入了一个统一的Jacobian-PCA-Grassmann框架，用于在函数空间和表示空间两个层面分析MoE层。在预训练的MoE Transformer（Mistral、Qwen）上，我们发现了一致的结构不对称性：专家表现出强功能去相关（跨专家Jacobian对齐度持续低值、接近零），而其路由表示则占据不同但部分重叠的子空间。这表明在MoE专业化中，功能去相关与表示重叠是共存而非重合的。受控路由实验进一步表明，路由稀疏性是塑造这种几何结构的关键因素：top-k路由导致更清晰的功能分离和更大的子空间散度，而全软路由则产生更纠缠的专家结构。综合这些结果表明，MoE层可被解释为在共享表示流形上的重叠子流形上实现局部去相关算子的几何结构，并为研究现代Transformer架构中的条件计算提供了通用的诊断框架。

</details>


### [94] [Federated Nested Learning: Collaborative Training of Self-Referential Memories for Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16350)
*Hong Chen,Pengcheng Wu,Yuanguo Lin,Peilin Zhao,Xiuze Zhou,Fan Lin,Han Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出联邦嵌套学习（FedNL），将联邦学习重构为三层嵌套优化系统，利用基于Titans的线性注意力机制实现客户端轻量级零样本测试时自适应，在非独立同分布数据上取得竞争性表现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有联邦学习主要关注静态模型的协作训练，难以有效处理客户端非独立同分布（Non-IID）数据。作者从嵌套学习视角重新审视FL，认为核心挑战在于如何协作学习优化规则而不仅仅是静态模型。

Method: 提出FedNL框架，将FL重构为三层嵌套优化系统，并嵌入基于Titans的线性注意力机制，使客户端能够通过将delta规则视为在线梯度步骤，执行轻量级零样本测试时自适应。

Result: 在非独立同分布MMLU和长上下文基准测试上，FedNL在短上下文推理中达到竞争性性能，提升了长上下文检索和流式交叉熵的性能，并维持恒定的推理内存。

Conclusion: FedNL通过嵌套学习范式有效解决了FL中Non-IID数据挑战，同时实现了轻量级自适应和恒定内存开销，在长短上下文任务上均表现优异。

Original Abstract: We rethink Federated Learning (FL) from a nested learning perspective, framing the core challenge as how to collaboratively learn optimization rules, not just static models, to tackle Non-IID client data. To address this, we propose Federated Nested Learning (FedNL), a novel framework that reformulates FL as a three-level nested optimization system. FedNL embeds Titans-based linear attention into FL, enabling clients to perform lightweight, zero-shot test-time adaptation by treating a delta rule as an online gradient step. Experiments on Non-IID MMLU and long-context benchmarks show that FedNL achieves competitive performance in short-context reasoning, enhances the performance of long-context retrieval and streaming Cross-Entropy, and maintains constant inference memory.

中文摘要翻译: 我们从嵌套学习的视角重新审视联邦学习（FL），将核心挑战定义为如何协作学习优化规则而不仅仅是静态模型，以应对客户端非独立同分布（Non-IID）数据。为此，我们提出联邦嵌套学习（FedNL），一种新颖的框架，将FL重构为三层嵌套优化系统。FedNL将基于Titans的线性注意力机制嵌入FL中，通过将delta规则视为在线梯度步骤，使客户端能够执行轻量级的零样本测试时自适应。在非独立同分布MMLU和长上下文基准测试上的实验表明，FedNL在短上下文推理中达到了具有竞争力的性能，提升了长上下文检索和流式交叉熵的性能，并维持了恒定的推理内存。

</details>


### [95] [Augmenting Human Evaluation with LLM Judges: How Many Human Reviews Do You Need?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16354)
*Jane Paik Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM评估者从替代人类评分的角色转变为辅助角色，采用两阶段抽样设计，通过双重稳健估计器结合少量人工评分与大量LLM评分，并给出样本量确定方法和研究设计优化策略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前使用LLM作为自动评估者的方法缺乏正式的研究设计基础，仅依赖人机一致性指标来替代人类评分，且对如何保留人工监督缺乏指导。

Method: (1) 将LLM评估者的角色从替代性(substitutive)转为辅助性(auxiliary)；(2) 提出两阶段抽样设计：第一阶段对所有样本进行LLM评估，第二阶段对子样本进行人工评估；(3) 采用缺失数据文献中的双重稳健估计器，利用缺失模型已知的特性，对预测模型具有稳健性；(4) 基于估计量的渐近方差，提出确定人机评分样本量的方法以及优化研究设计（对LLM可预测性低的评估类型分配更多人工评分）的策略。

Result: 提出了基于双重稳健估计量的两阶段抽样框架，证明了该方法的稳健性，给出了样本量确定和功率分析的计算方法，并展示了通过差异化分配人工评分来优化设计效率的策略。

Conclusion: LLM评估者应作为人类评估的辅助而非替代；通过两阶段抽样设计和双重稳健估计，可以在保留必要人工监督的前提下高效地利用LLM评估，并为基准验证中应保留多少人工监督提供了定量指导。

Original Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as automated evaluators of AI systems, including in high-stakes applications. In this role, LLMs are used to generate judgments about the quality, appropriateness, or even safety of model outputs. This approach is motivated by practical constraints. Expert human ratings are costly and difficult to scale, whereas LLM ratings can be produced quickly at low cost. However, current approaches to deploying LLM evaluators are ad hoc, typically limited to reporting agreement metrics between human and LLM judges as a justification for substitution of human ratings, and lack a formal basis for study design. This paper (1) shifts the role of the LLM judge from substitutive to auxiliary, and (2) formulates the LLM-as-a-judge paradigm as one of augmenting human evaluation through a two-stage sampling design, where LLM evaluations are measured for all observations at the first stage and human ratings are partially observed for a subsample at the second stage. We propose to use a doubly robust estimator from the missing data literature, which takes advantage of the robustness property against the prediction model, since the missingness model is known by design. Using the asymptotic variance of this estimator, we propose how sample sizes of human and LLM ratings can be determined to achieve a targeted level of power. We also show that a study can be efficiently designed by allocating more human ratings for types of evaluations where the predictability of LLM ratings is not high. To the best of our knowledge, there is very little guidance on how much human oversight should be retained when validating benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型（LLM）越来越多地被用作AI系统的自动评估者，包括在高风险应用中。在这种角色下，LLM被用于生成关于模型输出的质量、适当性甚至安全性的判断。这种方法受到实际约束的驱动：专家人工评分成本高昂且难以规模化，而LLM评分可以低成本快速生成。然而，当前部署LLM评估者的方法是临时的，通常仅限于报告人工与LLM评判者之间的一致性指标，以此作为替代人工评分的理由，缺乏研究设计的正式基础。本文(1)将LLM评判者的角色从替代性转变为辅助性，以及(2)将LLM作为评判者的范式形式化为两阶段抽样设计下增强人类评估的方法：第一阶段对所有观测进行LLM评估，第二阶段对子样本部分观测人工评分。我们提出使用来自缺失数据文献的双重稳健估计器，该估计器利用了对预测模型的稳健性优势，因为缺失模型是设计已知的。利用该估计器的渐近方差，我们提出了如何确定人工和LLM评分的样本量以达到目标功效水平。我们还表明，通过将更多人工评分分配给LLM评分可预测性不高的评估类型，可以高效地设计研究。据我们所知，目前关于在验证基准时应保留多少人工监督的指导非常缺乏。

</details>


### [96] [TailedTS: Benchmark Dataset for Heavy-Tailed Time Series Prediction and Periodicity Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16361)
*Xinyu Chen,HanQin Cai,Lijun Ding,Jinhua Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TailedTS 是一个基于2024年维基百科每小时页面浏览量构建的大规模时间序列基准数据集，专门用于测试模型在重尾、零膨胀和非高斯条件下的表现，包含约246.9亿数据点，揭示了高流量页面周期性弱、高斯损失函数失效等问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准数据集（如M4、M5、UCI电力数据集）缺乏对重尾、零膨胀和非高斯分布等真实世界极端波动场景的充分覆盖，无法有效评估时间序列预测模型在极端条件下的鲁棒性。

Method: 基于稀疏自回归（带稀疏性和非负性约束）的周期性量化框架，以及一套非高斯损失函数（ℓ1范数、Huber、分位数、ℓp范数）下的标准化预测基准评估方法。

Result: 高浏览量页面的周期性结构显著弱于低浏览量页面；标准高斯损失函数在高流量页面类别上性能严重退化，而鲁棒损失函数在所有流量规模上均能提供一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: TailedTS为时间序列预测在重尾分布场景下提供了严格的测试平台，并证明了针对非高斯特性的鲁棒损失函数在真实世界大规模流量预测中的必要性。

Original Abstract: We present TailedTS, a large-scale benchmark dataset derived from Wikipedia hourly page view observations throughout 2024, specifically designed to test time series forecasting models under heavy-tailed, zero-inflated, and non-Gaussian conditions. The dataset comprises approximately 24.69 billion data points spanning roughly 3 million unique Wikipedia pages per month, stored in high-efficiency Apache Parquet format. Wikipedia traffic follows a pronounced power-law distribution where roughly 5% of pages account for over 70% of total page views, creating a natural and rigorous testbed for model robustness against extreme volatility that are absent from or underrepresented in existing benchmarks such as M4, M5, and UCI electricity datasets. TailedTS enables several research tasks. First, we introduce a periodicity quantification framework based on sparse autoregression with sparsity and non-negativity constraints, revealing that frequently-viewed pages exhibit significantly weaker periodic structure than their less-viewed counterparts, showing direct implications for server allocation and traffic forecasting on large digital platforms. Second, we provide standardized prediction benchmarks evaluated under a suite of non-Gaussian loss functions, including $\ell_1$-norm, Huber, quantile, and $\ell_p$-norm losses, demonstrating that standard Gaussian-based estimators degrade substantially on high-volume page categories, while robust alternatives provide consistent gains across all traffic scales. TailedTS is publicly available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.17070469.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了TailedTS，这是一个基于2024年维基百科每小时页面浏览量观测数据构建的大规模基准数据集，专门用于测试时间序列预测模型在重尾、零膨胀和非高斯条件下的表现。该数据集包含约246.9亿个数据点，每月覆盖约300万个独特的维基百科页面，并以高效的Apache Parquet格式存储。维基百科流量遵循显著的幂律分布，约5%的页面贡献了超过70%的总页面浏览量，这为模型在极端波动下的鲁棒性提供了一个自然且严格的测试平台，而此类极端波动在M4、M5和UCI电力数据集等现有基准中是不存在或未被充分代表的。TailedTS支持多项研究任务。首先，我们引入了一个基于稀疏自回归（带有稀疏性和非负性约束）的周期性量化框架，揭示了高浏览量页面的周期性结构显著弱于低浏览量页面，这对大型数字平台的服务器分配和流量预测具有直接意义。其次，我们提供了一套在非高斯损失函数（包括ℓ1范数、Huber、分位数和ℓp范数损失）下评估的标准化预测基准，证明了基于高斯的估计器在高流量页面类别上性能严重退化，而鲁棒替代方法在所有流量规模上均能提供一致的性能提升。TailedTS公开访问地址为https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.17070469。

</details>


### [97] [When Is Rank-1 Steering Cheap? Geometry, Granularity, and Budgeted Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16362)
*John T. Robertson,Jianing Zhu,Haris Vikalo,Zhangyang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出激活几何可以指导大语言模型的秩-1控制向量搜索，大幅降低搜索成本，并引入“概念粒度”指标解释为什么某些概念即使优化搜索仍然昂贵，最终构建了GRACE框架来诊断和解决激活控制难题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 先前工作发现激活控制在不同概念上效果差异很大，往往归因于许多概念不能被单个方向向量捕获。本文认为这种差异很大程度上源于搜索困难——有用的秩-1干预往往存在，但找到它代价高昂。动机是理解并降低这种搜索成本。

Method: (1) 将秩-1激活控制形式化为预算约束下的优化问题；(2) 发现提示边界方向对齐性（prompt-boundary directional alignment）能预测有效干预的位置，实现几何引导的高效搜索；(3) 引入“概念粒度”（concept granularity）量化方向异质性，区分全局稳定方向和局部旋转方向的概念；(4) 构建GRACE框架，利用激活几何诊断困难来源、选择补救措施并分配优化资源。

Result: 几何引导的搜索在三个模型家族上将恢复95%最佳效用的所需试验次数平均减少39.8%。概念粒度与收敛速度（r=0.44）和最佳效用（r=-0.46）显著相关，高粒度概念更昂贵且性能更差。

Conclusion: 应当将问题从“秩-1何时失败”转向“秩-1何时廉价且稳定”，激活几何可以成为LLM控制的可操作先验，而不仅仅是描述性工具。

Original Abstract: Activation steering offers a lightweight way to control LLMs without retraining, but its effectiveness varies sharply across concepts. Prior work often reads this variability as evidence that many concepts are not captured by a single steering direction. We argue instead that much of it reflects search difficulty: a useful rank-1 intervention often exists, but finding it can be expensive.
  We formalize rank-1 steering as a budget-constrained optimization over intervention layer and coefficient. Across concepts and model families, prompt-boundary directional alignment predicts where effective interventions occur, enabling geometry-guided search that reaches high utility with substantially fewer evaluations, reducing the trials needed to recover 95\% of best-found utility by 39.8\% on average across three model families. To explain why some concepts remain expensive even under better search, we introduce \emph{concept granularity}, a measure of directional heterogeneity across contrastive contexts. Granularity distinguishes concepts whose difference vectors share a stable global direction from those where prompts agree locally within each input but the utility-maximizing direction rotates systematically across inputs. Higher granularity is associated with slower convergence and lower best-found performance (Pearson $r{=}0.44$ with trials-to-95\%, $r{=}{-}0.46$ with best-found utility, both $p<0.001$).
  We present \textit{GRACE}, a Granularity- and Representation-Aware Concept Engineering framework that uses activation geometry to diagnose the dominant source of steering difficulty, select the appropriate remedy, and allocate optimization effort efficiently. Our results shift the frame from ``\textit{when does rank-1 fail?}'' to ``\textit{when is rank-1 cheap and stable?}'', turning activation geometry from a descriptive tool into an actionable prior for LLM control.

中文摘要翻译: 激活操控提供了一种轻量级的方式来控制大语言模型而无需重新训练，但其有效性在不同概念之间差异显著。先前工作常将此差异解读为许多概念不能被单一操控方向所捕获的证据。我们则认为，这种差异在很大程度上反映了搜索困难：有用的秩-1干预往往存在，但找到它可能代价高昂。我们将秩-1操控形式化为一个关于干预层和系数的预算约束优化问题。在多个概念和模型家族中，提示边界方向对齐性可以预测有效干预发生的位置，从而实现几何引导的搜索，该搜索以显著更少的评估次数达到高效用，在三个模型家族上将恢复95%最佳效用的试验次数平均减少39.8%。为了解释为什么即使采用更好的搜索，某些概念仍然昂贵，我们引入了概念粒度——一种衡量对比上下文之间方向异质性的指标。粒度能够区分那些差异向量共享稳定全局方向的概念与那些在每输入内局部一致但效用最大化的方向在输入间系统性旋转的概念。更高的粒度与更慢的收敛速度和更低的最佳性能相关（与达到95%效用所需试验次数的Pearson r=0.44，与最佳效用的r=−0.46，两者p<0.001）。我们提出了GRACE，一个粒度和表示感知的概念工程框架，该框架利用激活几何来诊断操控困难的主要来源、选择适当的补救措施并高效分配优化计算资源。我们的结果将问题从“秩-1何时失效”转变为“秩-1何时廉价且稳定”，将激活几何从描述性工具转变为LLM控制的可操作先验。

</details>


### [98] [ORACLE: Anticipating Scams from Partial Trajectories in Streaming App Usage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16363)
*Wenbo Gao,Songbai Tan,Zhongan Wang,Fei Shen,Gang Xu,Huiping Zhuang,Yunyun Yang,Ming Li,Xiaofeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ORACLE框架，用于从流式应用使用轨迹中早期预测多阶段、跨应用的智能手机诈骗。包含自我演化的上下文管理器和在线策略自蒸馏方案，在真实长时序数据集上实现了更早的诈骗预警和更低的误报率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 智能手机诈骗日益猖獗，表现为多阶段、跨应用的过程，意图逐步显现。有效的干预需要在诈骗意图变得明确之前进行预测，但这依赖于部分轨迹和时域分布的证据，极具挑战性。现有方法缺乏针对流式应用使用轨迹的早期诈骗预测框架。

Method: 提出ORACLE框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 自演化上下文管理器，自适应地随时间整合以实体为中心的交互，从部分观测中重建跨时间证据；(2) 在线策略自蒸馏方案，教师模型基于技能总结的反诈骗反思和线索进行监督，学生模型则不接触这些反思，从而蒸馏证据信息知识，提升从部分轨迹中对新兴诈骗模式的识别能力。

Result: 在包含12种诈骗类型、平均跨度15天、涉及95个应用的流式应用使用轨迹基准上，ORACLE在早期诈骗预测任务中持续取得改进，能够提供及时的预警同时减少虚假警报。

Conclusion: ORACLE是首个用于从流式应用使用轨迹中进行早期诈骗预测的智能体框架，通过自适应上下文管理和在线知识蒸馏，有效应对了证据碎片化和早期信号微弱等挑战，在真实流式场景中实现了更早、更准确的诈骗预警。

Original Abstract: Smartphone scams are increasingly prevalent and typically manifest as multi-stage, cross-application processes with gradually emerging intent. Effective intervention thus requires anticipating scams before the intent becomes explicit. This is inherently challenging, as decisions must rely on partial trajectories with temporally distributed evidence. In this paper, we propose \textbf{ORACLE} Online Reasoning for Anticipating Cross-temporal Latent thrEats, the first agentic framework for early scam anticipation from \textit{streaming app-usage} trajectories. To support this setting, we curate a real-world long-horizon benchmark of streaming app-usage trajectories, covering 12 scam types, spanning extended periods (15 days on average), involving diverse applications (95 apps), and interleaving normal and scam behaviors. To address fragmented evidence, we introduce a self-evolving context manager that adaptively consolidates entity-centric interactions over time, enabling more effective reconstruction of cross-temporal evidence from partial observations. To enhance sensitivity to latent early-stage signals, we propose an on-policy self-distillation scheme in which a teacher model, conditioned on summarized anti-scam reflections and clues by skills, supervises a student model without access to such reflections. This scheme thereby distills evidence-informed knowledge and improves recognition of emerging fraud patterns from partial trajectories. Experiments show that \method{} consistently improves early scam anticipation, yielding timely warnings while reducing false alerts in realistic streaming scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 智能手机诈骗日益猖獗，通常表现为多阶段、跨应用的过程，且意图逐步显现。因此，有效的干预需要在诈骗意图变得明确之前进行预测。这本质上极具挑战性，因为决策必须依赖于部分轨迹及在时间上分布的证据。本文提出\textbf{ORACLE}（跨时间潜在威胁的在线推理与预测框架），这是首个用于从\textit{流式应用使用}轨迹中进行早期诈骗预测的智能体框架。为支持这一设定，我们构建了一个真实的长期流式应用使用轨迹基准，涵盖12种诈骗类型，时间跨度长（平均15天），涉及多样化的应用（95个应用），并交织了正常行为与诈骗行为。为解决证据碎片化问题，我们引入了一个自演化上下文管理器，能够自适应地随时间整合以实体为中心的交互，从而从部分观测中更有效地重建跨时间证据。为增强对潜在早期信号的敏感性，我们提出了一种在线策略自蒸馏方案，其中教师模型基于技能总结的反诈骗反思和线索进行监督，学生模型则不接触这些反思。该方案从而蒸馏了证据信息知识，提升了从部分轨迹中识别新兴诈骗模式的能力。实验表明，\method{}在早期诈骗预测上持续取得改进，在真实流式场景中提供及时的预警，同时减少虚假警报。

</details>


### [99] [Machine Learning-Based Pre-Test Risk Stratification for PCR-Confirmed Chlamydia Using Patient-Reported Data and Urine Biomarkers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16365)
*Mehrab Mahdian,Marko Lehes,Katrin Krolov,Tamas Pardy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了基于机器学习模型的检测前风险分层（PTRS）用于沙眼衣原体感染筛查的可行性，发现尿液生物标志物与患者自述信息互补，特征融合可提升模型鲁棒性，为资源有限的筛查场景提供了优化方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 早期识别沙眼衣原体感染高风险个体，可在资源有限的筛查场景中优化分子检测的使用，实现检测优先级排序。

Method: 使用93份尿液样本的PCR参考标签数据集，基于三类特征组（患者自述病史与症状、尿液生物标志物、两者组合）训练五种有监督分类器，采用分层5折交叉验证及out-of-fold概率估计评估模型性能，通过Bootstrap置信区间量化不确定性。

Result: 仅使用患者自述数据的模型AUC最高达0.72；尿液生物标志物模型峰值略低但表现更一致，集成方法效果最佳；特征组合边际提升了峰值AUC并降低了性能波动。

Conclusion: 尿液生物标志物为PTRS提供了可靠且与患者自述信息互补的预测信号，特征整合可增强鲁棒性。该研究支持将非侵入性、常规可获取的信息整合到筛查流程中，包括分散化或居家PCR检测场景，以优化检测优先级。

Original Abstract: Early identification of individuals at elevated risk of Chlamydia trachomatis infection may enable optimal use of molecular testing in resource-aware screening. We evaluate the feasibility of pre-test risk stratification (PTRS) using machine-learning models trained on routinely available, non-invasive clinical data.
  A curated dataset of 93 urine samples with PCR reference labels was analyzed using three feature groups: patient-reported history and symptoms, urine biomarkers from standard urinalysis, and their combination. Five supervised classifiers were evaluated using stratified 5-fold cross-validation with out-of-fold probability estimates. Performance was assessed using area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) and threshold-dependent metrics, with uncertainty quantified via bootstrap confidence intervals.
  Models using only patient-reported data showed moderate discrimination (AUC up to 0.72). Urine biomarker-based models demonstrated slightly lower peak discrimination but more consistent performance, with ensemble methods yielding the strongest results. Combining feature groups marginally increased the peak AUC and reduced performance variability across models, indicating improved robustness.
  Findings indicate that urine biomarkers provide a reliable predictive signal for PTRS that is complementary to patient-reported information, while feature integration enhances robustness. This work supports the integration of non-invasive, routinely available information for PTRS into screening workflows, including decentralized or home-based PCR contexts, to optimize testing prioritization.

中文摘要翻译: 早期识别沙眼衣原体感染高风险个体，有助于在资源感知型筛查中优化分子检测的使用。本研究评估了基于机器学习模型的检测前风险分层（PTRS）的可行性，这些模型使用常规可获取的非侵入性临床数据进行训练。对包含93份尿液样本及其PCR参考标签的精选数据集进行了分析，使用三类特征组：患者自述病史与症状、标准尿液分析中的尿液生物标志物，以及两者的组合。采用五种有监督分类器，通过分层5折交叉验证及out-of-fold概率估计进行评估。使用受试者工作特征曲线下面积（AUC）和阈值相关指标评估性能，并通过Bootstrap置信区间量化不确定性。仅使用患者自述数据的模型表现中等（AUC最高0.72）。基于尿液生物标志物的模型峰值判别能力略低，但表现更一致，其中集成方法取得了最强结果。组合特征组边际提升了峰值AUC并降低了各模型间的性能波动，表明鲁棒性有所增强。研究结果表明，尿液生物标志物为PTRS提供了与患者自述信息互补的可靠预测信号，而特征整合可增强鲁棒性。本研究支持将非侵入性、常规可获取的信息整合到筛查工作流程中，包括分散化或居家PCR检测场景，以优化检测优先级排序。

</details>


### [100] [M$^2$FedAQI: Multimodal Federated Learning for Air Quality Prediction on Heterogeneous Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16375)
*Manjil Nepal,Kimsie Phan,Tamoghna Ojha,Aritra Dutta,M Krishna Siva Prasad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级多模态联邦学习框架M²FedAQI，用于分布式异构边缘设备上的空气质量指数预测，通过特征调制融合机制整合视觉和表格数据，在多个基准数据集上显著优于现有方法，并实现了高效资源利用和安全通信。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有空气质量预测方法大多依赖集中式学习范式，存在可扩展性差、隐私保护不足和通信开销大等问题；同时，当前基于联邦学习的解决方案主要使用单模态数据，难以捕捉复杂的环境模式。

Method: 提出了M²FedAQI框架，通过特征调制融合机制实现视觉和表格模态的高效跨模态交互，在保持低计算开销的同时实现多模态融合；采用联邦学习架构支持分布式异构边缘设备部署；并引入基于TLS的身份认证增强通信安全。

Result: 在PM25Vision和TRAQID两个基准数据集上，分类任务Accuracy提升11.0%、AUC提升3.53%、F1-score提升12.2%；回归任务R²提升18.0%，MAE降低25.4%、RMSE降低20.4%；在异构边缘设备上实现了高效的通信开销、内存占用和计算成本。

Conclusion: M²FedAQI通过轻量级多模态联邦学习框架有效解决了分布式IoT环境中空气质量预测的可扩展性、隐私和通信问题，在性能上全面超越现有方法，并具备实际部署的可行性和安全性。

Original Abstract: Accurate air quality prediction is essential for public health, environmental monitoring, and industrial safety. However, most existing approaches rely on centralized learning paradigms, which introduce challenges related to scalability, privacy preservation, and communication overhead in distributed Internet of Things (IoT) environments. Moreover, current federated learning (FL) based solutions predominantly utilize unimodal data, limiting their capability to capture complex environmental patterns. To address these limitations, we propose M$^2$FedAQI, a lightweight multimodal federated framework for decentralized Air Quality Index (AQI) prediction across heterogeneous edge devices. The proposed framework integrates visual and tabular modalities through a feature modulation based fusion mechanism that enables efficient cross-modal interaction while maintaining low computational overhead. M$^2$FedAQI is evaluated on two benchmark datasets, PM25Vision and TRAQID, for both classification and regression tasks under centralized and federated settings. Experimental results demonstrate that M$^2$FedAQI consistently outperforms existing approaches, achieving improvements of up to 11.0\% in Accuracy, 3.53\% in AUC, 12.2\% in F1-score, and 18.0\% in $R^2$, while reducing MAE and RMSE by up to 25.4\% and 20.4\%, respectively, compared with the strongest baselines. Furthermore, deployment on heterogeneous edge devices demonstrates efficient resource utilization in terms of communication overhead, memory footprint, and computational cost. To enhance communication security, TLS-based authentication is incorporated to ensure secure client participation and protect the FL communication channel from unauthorized third-party access without modifying the underlying FL protocol.

中文摘要翻译: 准确的空气质量预测对于公共卫生、环境监测和工业安全至关重要。然而，大多数现有方法依赖集中式学习范式，这在分布式物联网环境中带来了可扩展性、隐私保护和通信开销方面的挑战。此外，当前基于联邦学习的解决方案主要使用单模态数据，限制了其捕获复杂环境模式的能力。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出了M²FedAQI，一种用于异构边缘设备上分布式空气质量指数预测的轻量级多模态联邦框架。该框架通过基于特征调制的融合机制整合视觉和表格模态，在保持低计算开销的同时实现高效的跨模态交互。M²FedAQI在PM25Vision和TRAQID两个基准数据集上，针对分类和回归任务在集中式和联邦设置下进行了评估。实验结果表明，与最强基线相比，M²FedAQI在Accuracy上提升高达11.0%，AUC提升3.53%，F1-score提升12.2%，R²提升18.0%，同时MAE和RMSE分别降低25.4%和20.4%。此外，在异构边缘设备上的部署展示了其在通信开销、内存占用和计算成本方面的高效资源利用。为了增强通信安全性，系统集成了基于TLS的身份认证机制，确保客户端安全参与，并保护联邦学习通信信道免受未授权第三方访问，同时无需修改底层联邦学习协议。

</details>


### [101] [Mixing Times of Glauber Dynamics on Masked Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16378)
*Suvadip Sana,Sami Wolf,Neer Mehta,Alina Shah,Aitzaz Shaikh,Janna Goodman,Lionel Levine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了掩码语言模型(MLM)的条件分布与全局联合分布之间的不一致性，将迭代掩码标记重采样建模为Glauber动力学马尔可夫链，从理论和实验两方面分析了其混合行为、亚稳态相变及语义结构。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: MLM定义的局部条件分布通常不对应任何一致的联合分布，这引发了一个根本性问题：当这些条件分布被迭代用于生成时，会诱导出怎样的全局分布行为？现有研究缺乏对这一问题的理论理解。

Method: (1) 提出矩形测试(rectangle test)用于验证MLM条件分布的不兼容性；(2) 将迭代掩码标记重采样建模为Glauber动力学马尔可夫链；(3) 在有限交叉标记影响下推导高温收缩结果；(4) 在均匀局部边缘条件下证明低温亚稳态；(5) 通过实验验证温度与序列长度的相变行为。

Result: (1) 经验验证了现代MLM普遍存在条件分布不兼容性；(2) 高温下链在O(n log n)时间内混合；(3) 低温下链表现出亚稳态，从语义盆地逃逸呈指数级缓慢；(4) 观察到与理论预测一致的相变行为；(5) 识别出长寿命陷阱和循环语义盆地等持久结构。

Conclusion: MLM的局部条件不兼容性导致其采样链在不同温度下呈现截然不同的行为：高温时快速混合，低温时被困于语义盆地。这一发现对理解MLM的生成行为及其潜在风险（如政治内容偏向）具有重要启示。

Original Abstract: Masked language models (MLMs) define local conditional distributions over tokens but do not, in general, correspond to any consistent joint distribution over sequences. This raises a fundamental question: what global distributional behavior is induced when such conditionals are used iteratively for generation? We address this question by modeling iterative masked-token resampling as a Glauber dynamics Markov chain on the discrete space of token sequences. We first show that MLM conditionals are intrinsically incompatible: we introduce a rectangle test that certifies this incompatibility and empirically verify its prevalence across modern MLMs. We then provide a theoretical analysis of the induced Markov chain. Under bounded cross-token influence, we establish a high-temperature contraction result implying $O(n\log n)$ mixing time where $n$ is the sequence length. In contrast, we prove that under a uniform local margin condition, the chain exhibits metastability, with exponentially slow escape from semantic basins at low temperatures. Empirically, we demonstrate a phase transition in mixing behavior as a function of temperature and sequence length, consistent with the theoretical predictions. We further characterize the induced stationary behavior through semantic trajectories, identifying persistent structures such as long-lived traps and recurrent semantic basins, with political content serving as a measurable case study.

中文摘要翻译: 掩码语言模型(MLM)定义了标记上的局部条件分布，但通常不对应任何一致的序列联合分布。这引发了一个根本性问题：当这些条件分布被迭代用于生成时，会诱导出什么样的全局分布行为？我们通过将迭代掩码标记重采样建模为标记序列离散空间上的Glauber动力学马尔可夫链来回答这一问题。我们首先证明MLM条件分布具有内在的不兼容性：我们引入了一个矩形测试来验证这种不兼容性，并通过实验证实了其在现代MLM中的普遍性。接着，我们对诱导出的马尔可夫链进行理论分析。在有限交叉标记影响的条件下，我们建立了一个高温收缩结果，表明混合时间为O(n log n)，其中n为序列长度。相反，我们证明在均匀局部边缘条件下，该链表现出亚稳态，低温下从语义盆地逃逸呈指数级缓慢。实验上，我们展示了作为温度和序列长度函数的混合行为中的相变，与理论预测一致。我们进一步通过语义轨迹来刻画诱导的稳态行为，识别出诸如长寿命陷阱和循环语义盆地等持久结构，并以政治内容作为可测量的案例研究。

</details>


### [102] [An Information-Theoretic Criterion for Efficient Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16379)
*Hanyu Li,Zhengqi Sun,Xiaotie Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息论角度解释了合成数据在LLM训练中效果不一致的原因：当生成-训练循环是信息开放（受外部信号影响）时才能提升模型，信息封闭时因数据处理不等式必然导致信息减少和模型崩溃。同时指出监督信号的粒度影响泛化能力，并提出了学习收敛到最信息高效信号分量的指导性论点。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 合成数据在大型语言模型训练中被广泛使用，但其效果高度不一致，缺乏统一的解释框架。作者希望从信息论角度对这一现象提供理论解释。

Method: 采用信息论分析框架，运用数据处理不等式（Data Processing Inequality）等工具，区分信息开放循环（有外部信号如验证器、环境、评分标准）和信息封闭循环（仅依赖模型自身输出），并从监督信号的粒度（粗粒度如二值正确性 vs. 细粒度）角度分析泛化性能差异。

Result: 信息封闭循环中，数据处理不等式确保任务相关信息只会减少，导致模型崩溃；信息开放循环中，粗粒度信号（如二值正确性）因将所有可接受输出视为等价，不受特定领域或表面形式约束，因而能自然跨任务和领域泛化。

Conclusion: 学习过程优先收敛到信息最高效的信号分量，当该分量是预期目标时加速学习，但当虚假模式更简单时会导致奖励黑客（reward hacking）问题。

Original Abstract: Synthetic data becomes crucial for large language model training, but its effectiveness is highly inconsistent. We provide an information-theoretic account of this inconsistency: synthetic data improves a model only when the generation-training loop is information-open, i.e., shaped by external signals (verifiers, environments, or rubrics) that inject task-relevant information beyond the model's current distribution. When the loop is information-closed (relying on the model's own outputs without such signals), the data processing inequality ensures that task-relevant information can only decrease, making collapse a predicted outcome. Among information-open pipelines, both efficiency and generalization hinge on the meta-level of supervision: a coarser signal such as binary correctness treats all acceptable outputs as equivalent, so the behavior it teaches is not tied to any particular domain or surface form and generalizes naturally across tasks and domains. These observations lead to a guiding thesis: learning preferentially converges to the most information-efficient signal component available, which accelerates learning when that component is the intended one, but causes reward hacking when a spurious pattern happens to be simpler.

中文摘要翻译: 合成数据对大型语言模型训练至关重要，但其效果高度不一致。我们从信息论角度解释了这种不一致性：只有当生成-训练循环是信息开放的——即由外部信号（验证器、环境或评分标准）引导，注入超出模型当前分布的任务相关信息时——合成数据才能改进模型。当循环是信息封闭的（仅依赖模型自身输出而无外部信号）时，数据处理不等式确保了任务相关信息只会减少，从而使模型崩溃成为可预测的结果。在信息开放的流程中，效率和泛化能力都取决于监督的元层次：像二值正确性这样的粗粒度信号将所有可接受的输出视为等价，因此它所教化的行为不绑定于任何特定领域或表面形式，能够在不同任务和领域间自然泛化。这些观察引出了一个指导性论点：学习过程优先收敛到信息效率最高的信号分量，当该分量是预期目标时加速学习，但当虚假模式恰好更简单时则会导致奖励黑客问题。

</details>


### [103] [ReTAMamba: Reliability-Aware Temporal Aggregation with Mamba for Irregular Clinical Time Series Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16380)
*Jinwoong Kim,Sangjin Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReTAMamba模型，通过可靠性感知的时间聚合和Mamba架构，有效处理不规则采样的临床时间序列，在三个公开数据集上显著提升了预测性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法使用观测掩码和时间间隔信息，但未能持续捕捉历史观测的衰减可靠性，也未能在聚合过程中将多分辨率信息组织到连贯的时间上下文中。

Method: 将临床时间序列重构为时间可变令牌序列，估计观测可靠性（基于缺失性和经过时间），用统计描述符增强区间摘要；使用时间顺序编织（Chronological Weaving）整合短长期时间信息，并采用预算令牌路由器约束序列长度同时保留信息摘要。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV、eICU和PhysioNet 2012上，AUPRC分别提升7.51%、7.80%和10.15%；eICU分析表明，心率、血压等动态信号的学习平均衰减比实验室检验变量等相对静态信号大24.3%。

Conclusion: 有效预测不规则临床时间序列不仅需要建模测量了什么，还需要建模观测的时间和方式，包括信息新鲜度和观测及时性。

Original Abstract: Clinical time-series data are difficult to model with methods designed for regular sequences because they exhibit irregular sampling, frequent missing values, and heterogeneous observation patterns across variables. Existing approaches commonly use observation masks and time-gap information, but they do not continuously capture the decaying reliability of past observations or consistently organize multi-resolution information within a coherent temporal context during aggregation. To address these limitations, we propose Reliability-aware Temporal Aggregation with Mamba (ReTAMamba), which reconstructs clinical time series as time-variable token sequences, estimates observation reliability from missingness and elapsed time, and augments interval summaries with statistical descriptors. Chronological Weaving is used to integrate short- and long-term temporal information within a coherent temporal context, and a budgeted token router is applied to constrain sequence length while preserving informative summaries. Experiments on MIMIC-IV, eICU, and PhysioNet 2012 show that ReTAMamba consistently improves AUPRC over strong baselines, with average relative gains of 7.51%, 7.80%, and 10.15%, respectively. Cohort-level and patient-level analyses on eICU further showed that the learned mean decay for more dynamic signals, such as heart rate and blood pressure, was 24.3% larger than that for relatively static signals, such as laboratory test variables. These findings suggest that effective prediction in irregular clinical time series requires modeling not only what was measured, but also when and how it was observed, including information freshness and observation timeliness.

中文摘要翻译: 面向规则序列设计的方法难以对临床时间序列数据进行建模，因为这些数据存在不规则采样、频繁缺失值以及不同变量间异质的观测模式。现有方法通常使用观测掩码和时间间隔信息，但未能持续捕捉历史观测的衰减可靠性，也未能在聚合过程中将多分辨率信息一致地组织到连贯的时间上下文中。为解决这些局限性，我们提出了基于Mamba的可靠性感知时间聚合（ReTAMamba），该方法将临床时间序列重构为时间可变令牌序列，从缺失性和经过时间中估计观测可靠性，并用统计描述符增强区间摘要。采用时间顺序编织（Chronological Weaving）在连贯的时间上下文中整合短长期时间信息，并引入预算令牌路由器来约束序列长度同时保留信息摘要。在MIMIC-IV、eICU和PhysioNet 2012上的实验表明，ReTAMamba在AUPRC指标上持续优于强基线模型，平均相对增益分别为7.51%、7.80%和10.15%。在eICU上的队列级和患者级分析进一步显示，心率、血压等动态信号的学习平均衰减比实验室检验变量等相对静态信号大24.3%。这些发现表明，对不规则临床时间序列的有效预测不仅需要建模测量了什么，还需要建模观测的时间和方式，包括信息新鲜度和观测及时性。

</details>


### [104] [A Theory of Training Profit-Optimal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16430)
*Sophie Hao,William Merrill*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文结合缩放定律与微观经济学理论，构建了大语言模型（LLM）训练企业的经济模型，分析模型质量提升如何转化为收入，以及利润最优的模型规模、训练预算与硬件/数据效率之间的关系。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究已证明扩大LLM规模能可靠提升模型质量（损失下降、下游任务表现提升），但尚不清楚这些质量提升如何转化为潜在收入，以及收入增长能否抵消更大规模训练和推理的成本。作者希望为批判性地审视行业声明和长期经济决策提供理论基础。

Method: 将缩放定律与微观经济学理论结合，构建LLM训练企业的利润最大化模型。模型假设：模型质量随参数量和训练token增加而提高，消费者根据质量阈值决定是否采用；模型同时考虑参数和训练token带来的成本。分别在计算受限（compute-bound）和数据受限（data-bound）两种情境下分析利润最大化问题。

Result: 1）在计算受限情境下，最优模型规模和token预算与硬件效率E（FLOPs/$）呈近线性关系；总训练成本随E次二次方增长。2）数据效率提升会激励更大的模型和更高的训练支出。3）在数据受限情境下（最多D个数据），利润最优的训练支出与D²/E成正比。4）当前行业训练支出趋势与计算受限情境下最宽松的模型变体一致，但在数据受限情境或假设硬件进步停滞时并非利润最优。

Conclusion: 本文建立了利润最优的LLM训练理论，为批判性审视行业声明和长期经济决策提供了分析框架。

Original Abstract: Scaling LLMs requires tremendous computational resources, and recent advances in AI have gone hand in hand with massive amounts of capital expenditure. While it is established that scaling up LLMs reliably increases model quality (quantified in terms of loss or downstream evaluations), it is unclear how these quality improvements translate to potential revenue, and whether revenue increases would offset costs of larger-scale training and inference. In this work, we develop an economic model for characterizing the rational behavior of an LLM training firm by combining scaling laws with microeconomic theory. Under our model of firm behavior, LLM quality can be increased with more parameters and training tokens, leading to more potential adoption by consumers, who each have a quality threshold for using the LLM. On the other hand, additional parameters and training tokens both incur additional costs. We analyze the profit maximization problem for this model under compute-bound and data-bound regimes. In the compute-bound regime, optimal model size and token budget track hardware efficiency $E$ (FLOPs/\$) at a near-linear rate; total training cost then scales sub-quadratically in $E$. Data efficiency improvements incentivize larger models and training expenditure. When we are limited to $D$ data, profit-optimal training expenditure scales as $D^2/E$, i.e, increase with data and decreases with hardware efficiency (as well as data efficiency). Finally, we analyze practical trends in training expenditure: current trends are consistent with our most permissive model variants in the compute-bound regime, but are not profit-optimal in the data-bound regime or assuming hardware advances will stall. Overall, our results provide a theory of profit-optimal LLM training, providing a foundation for engaging critically with industry statements and supporting long-term economic decision making.

中文摘要翻译: 扩展大语言模型（LLM）需要巨大的计算资源，而近期人工智能的进步与大规模资本支出密不可分。虽然已有研究证实扩大LLM规模能可靠地提升模型质量（以损失或下游评估指标衡量），但尚不清楚这些质量改进如何转化为潜在收入，以及收入增长能否抵消更大规模训练和推理的成本。本文通过将缩放定律与微观经济学理论相结合，构建了一个描述LLM训练企业理性行为的经济模型。在我们的企业行为模型下，LLM质量可通过增加参数和训练token来提升，从而使更多消费者（每位消费者有使用LLM的质量阈值）有潜在可能采用该模型。另一方面，额外的参数和训练token都会带来额外成本。我们分别在计算受限和数据受限两种情境下分析了该模型的利润最大化问题。在计算受限情境中，最优模型规模和token预算与硬件效率$E$（FLOPs/\$）呈近线性关系；总训练成本随后以$E$的次二次方规模增长。数据效率的提升会激励更大的模型规模和更高的训练支出。当数据量限制为$D$时，利润最优的训练支出与$D^2/E$成比例，即随数据增加而增加，随硬件效率（以及数据效率）提高而降低。最后，我们分析了训练支出的实际趋势：当前趋势与计算受限情境下最宽松的模型变体一致，但在数据受限情境或假设硬件进步停滞的情况下并非利润最优。总体而言，本文的结果提供了利润最优的LLM训练理论，为批判性审视行业声明并支持长期经济决策奠定了基础。

</details>


### [105] [QuChaTeR: A Hybrid Quantum-Chaotic Temporal Framework for Earthquake Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16454)
*Emir Kaan Özdemir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QuChaTeR，一种结合小波预处理、混沌映射和变分量子电路的混合架构，用于增强地震信号的时间特征提取，在真实地震数据集上相比多种经典和量子基线方法实现了更快的收敛和更优的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 地震信号具有高度非线性和混沌动力学特性，传统深度学习模型（如LSTM、CNN）仅能捕捉局部时间特征，量子模型虽能提供更丰富的状态表示，但如何将混沌驱动机制与量子模型有效集成尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出QuChaTeR混合架构，包含：基于小波变换的预处理模块、混沌映射模块、变分量子电路与循环结构的集成模块，在PyTorch和PennyLane框架中实现。

Result: 在真实地震数据集上，QuChaTeR相比LSTM、GRU、RNN、1D-CNN、储层计算和量子LSTM等基线方法，收敛更快且在所有评估指标上均表现更优。

Conclusion: 量子-混沌混合方法为更准确、更鲁棒的地震预测提供了一条可行路径，但可扩展性和量子硬件限制仍是当前面临的挑战。

Original Abstract: Seismic prediction remains challenging due to the highly nonlinear and chaotic dynamics of earthquake signals. While classical deep learning models such as LSTMs and CNNs capture local temporal features, and quantum models offer richer state representations, their integration with chaos-driven mechanisms is underexplored. We introduce QuChaTeR, a hybrid architecture that combines wavelet-based preprocessing, chaotic maps, and variational quantum circuits with recurrent structures to enhance temporal feature extraction. Implemented in PyTorch and PennyLane, QuChaTeR is benchmarked against classical (LSTM, GRU, RNN, 1D-CNN, Reservoir Computing) and quantum-inspired (Quantum LSTM) baselines. On real-world seismic datasets, QuChaTeR consistently converges faster and achieves superior performance across multiple evaluation criteria. Despite promising results, scalability and quantum hardware limitations remain challenges. Overall, this work demonstrates how quantum-chaotic hybridization provides a practical pathway toward more accurate and robust earthquake prediction.

中文摘要翻译: 地震预测仍然具有挑战性，因为地震信号具有高度非线性和混沌动力学特性。虽然LSTM和CNN等经典深度学习模型能够捕捉局部时间特征，量子模型也能提供更丰富的状态表示，但将它们与混沌驱动机制相结合的研究尚未充分展开。我们提出了QuChaTeR，一种混合架构，结合了小波预处理、混沌映射以及变分量子电路与循环结构，以增强时间特征提取。该模型在PyTorch和PennyLane中实现，并与经典模型（LSTM、GRU、RNN、1D-CNN、储层计算）和量子启发模型（量子LSTM）进行了基准对比。在真实地震数据集上，QuChaTeR始终收敛更快，并在多个评估标准上实现了更优的性能。尽管结果令人鼓舞，但可扩展性和量子硬件的局限性仍然是挑战。总体而言，这项工作展示了量子-混沌混合方法如何为更准确、更鲁棒的地震预测提供一条可行路径。

</details>


### [106] [Edge-AI-Driven Learning-to-Rank for Decentralized Task Allocation in Circular Smart Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16433)
*Mohammadhossein Ghahramani,Yan Qiao,Mengchu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于排序感知协商的边缘AI驱动去中心化任务分配框架，将轻量级决策智能嵌入机器层级，在高负载和紧期限场景下实现低延迟协调，并通过将学习目标与排序决策对齐，显著提升了延迟、截止时间遵守率、吞吐量和能效表现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 智能制造系统中的任务分配面临去中心化决策、动态工作负载和共享资源约束等挑战，尤其在循环制造场景下还需平衡运营效率与资源能源可持续性。现有基于学习的方法多关注绝对性能指标预测，而忽略了去中心化分配本质上依赖候选机器的相对排序，导致预测结果难以转化为实际的分配优化。

Method: 提出了一种基于排序感知协商的边缘AI驱动去中心化任务分配框架。该框架逐步构建：首先建立资源感知启发式去中心化投标结构，然后利用边缘AI回归模型进行局部投标近似，最后设计排序感知的损失函数重塑学习目标，使其与胜选者选择的排序特性对齐。每台机器利用局部信息（处理能力、队列状态、资源争用）评估到达任务。

Result: 通过离散事件仿真在高负载和紧期限场景下评估，实验结果表明该方法在高负载下显著改善了延迟和截止时间遵守率，在更紧的约束下提升了能效，实现了更符合循环制造目标的资源高效运行。

Conclusion: 将学习目标与去中心化决策结构对齐，对于实现高效的基于协商的任务分配至关重要，这为循环制造中的可持续智能调度提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: Task allocation in smart manufacturing systems needs to operate under decentralized decision-making, dynamic workloads, and shared resource constraints. In circular manufacturing settings, these challenges are further intensified by the need to balance operational efficiency with resource and energy sustainability. While learning-based approaches have been explored, many focus on predicting absolute performance metrics that do not necessarily translate into improved allocation outcomes, since decentralized assignment is governed by the relative ordering of candidate machines. This work proposes an Edge-AI-driven decentralized task allocation framework based on ranking-aware negotiation, where lightweight decision intelligence is embedded at the machine level to enable low-latency coordination without centralized control. The framework is developed progressively: a resource-aware heuristic first establishes the decentralized bidding structure, an Edge-AI-based regression model then provides learned local bid approximation, and a ranking-aware formulation finally reshapes the learning objective to align with the ordering-based nature of winner selection. Each machine evaluates incoming tasks using local information, including processing capability, queue state, and resource contention. The framework is evaluated via discrete-event simulation under high-load and tight-deadline scenarios using delay, deadline violations, throughput, and energy consumption. Results show improved delay and deadline adherence under high load, and enhanced energy efficiency under tighter constraints, leading to more resource-efficient operation aligned with circular manufacturing objectives. These findings demonstrate that aligning learning objectives with decentralized decision structures is critical for effective negotiation-driven task allocation.

中文摘要翻译: 智能制造系统中的任务分配需要在去中心化决策、动态工作负载和共享资源约束下运行。在循环制造场景中，这些挑战因需要在运营效率与资源和能源可持续性之间取得平衡而进一步加剧。尽管已有研究探索了基于学习的方法，但许多方法侧重于预测绝对性能指标，这些指标并不一定能转化为更好的分配结果，因为去中心化分配是由候选机器的相对排序决定的。本文提出了一种基于排序感知协商的边缘AI驱动去中心化任务分配框架，将轻量级决策智能嵌入机器层级，实现无需集中控制的低延迟协调。该框架逐步构建：首先建立资源感知启发式去中心化投标结构，然后利用边缘AI回归模型进行学习驱动的局部投标近似，最后设计排序感知的损失函数重塑学习目标，使其与胜选者选择的排序特性对齐。每台机器利用局部信息（包括处理能力、队列状态和资源争用）评估到达任务。通过离散事件仿真在高负载和紧期限场景下评估该框架，考察指标包括延迟、截止时间违反率、吞吐量和能耗。结果表明，在高负载下延迟和截止时间遵守率得到改善，在更紧的约束下能效得到提升，从而实现了更符合循环制造目标的资源高效运行。这些发现表明，将学习目标与去中心化决策结构对齐，对于实现高效的基于协商的任务分配至关重要。

</details>


### [107] [GPU-Accelerated Deep Learning for Heatwave Prediction and Urban Heat Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16435)
*Adis Alihodžić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于GPU的深度学习框架，利用ConvLSTM和混合损失函数预测城市热条件并生成热风险地图，在萨拉热窝数据集上取得了良好效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 城市热浪问题日益严重，气候变化加剧了这一挑战，亟需高效的预测工具来评估城市热风险。

Method: 基于GPU的深度学习框架，使用MODIS地表温度数据和Open-Meteo预测数据，对比了卷积模型和时空模型，其中ConvLSTM配合混合损失函数表现最佳，采用混合精度训练加速执行。

Result: ConvLSTM模型取得了MAE=0.2293, RMSE=0.3089, R²=0.8877的效果；更长时间序列和更多气象变量可提升性能；GPU混合精度训练显著缩短了执行时间。

Conclusion: 该框架可结合热危害、暴露度和脆弱性数据生成城市热风险地图，为城市热分析提供实用的基础工具。

Original Abstract: Heatwaves are an important problem in cities, and climate change makes this problem more difficult. In this paper, we present a GPU-based deep learning framework for next-day prediction of urban thermal conditions and for heat risk assessment. The study was carried out in Sarajevo by using MODIS land surface temperature data and Open-Meteo forecast data. We tested several models, including convolutional models and spatiotemporal models. Among them, ConvLSTM with a mixed loss function gave the best results. The obtained values were MAE = 0.2293, RMSE = 0.3089, and R2 = 0.8877. The experiments also showed that results can be improved by using longer temporal series and additional meteorological variables. Since the framework was implemented on a GPU and trained with mixed precision, the execution time was reduced. Based on the predicted temperature fields, it was also possible to combine hazard information with exposure and vulnerability data in order to generate city heat risk maps. The proposed framework can be used as a practical basis for city heat analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 热浪是城市中的一个重要问题，而气候变化使这一问题变得更加严峻。本文提出了一个基于GPU的深度学习框架，用于城市热条件的次日预测和热风险评估。研究以萨拉热窝为案例，使用了MODIS地表温度数据和Open-Meteo预测数据。我们测试了多种模型，包括卷积模型和时空模型。其中，采用混合损失函数的ConvLSTM模型表现最佳，取得了MAE=0.2293、RMSE=0.3089、R²=0.8877的结果。实验还表明，使用更长时间序列和更多气象变量可以进一步改善结果。由于该框架在GPU上实现并采用混合精度训练，执行时间得以缩短。基于预测的温度场，还可以将热危害信息与暴露度和脆弱性数据相结合，生成城市热风险地图。所提出的框架可作为城市热分析的实用基础。

</details>


### [108] [Byzantine-Resilient Federated Learning via QUBO-Based Client Selection on Quantum Annealers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16438)
*Andras Ferenczi,Sutapa Samanta,Dagen Wang,Jason Qizhe Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于量子退火（QUBO）的拜占庭鲁棒聚合方法，将客户端选择建模为二次无约束二元优化问题，并通过MultiSignal多信号集成框架扩展到大规模联邦学习场景，在最具挑战性的拜占庭攻击上显著优于MultiKrum。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有拜占庭鲁棒聚合方法（如MultiKrum）基于逐客户端贪婪评分，依赖近邻距离度量，容易被保持统计特性的恶意更新绕过（如Advanced LIE），缺乏全局联合优化能力。

Method: 1) 将客户端选择建模为QUBO问题，通过量子退火求解全局最优的m个相互距离最近的客户端子集；2) 提出MultiSignal集成框架，利用欧氏距离和余弦距离的Krum分数差距构建双特征路由门，将攻击分为四个区间，对规避攻击采用疑虑惩罚QUBO+一致投票机制，实现从15到100客户端的可扩展部署。

Result: 小规模(15客户端)下，QUBO对Advanced LIE检测准确率在MNIST上为95.11% vs MultiKrum的81.33%，CIFAR-10上为97.78% vs 75.56%。大规模(100客户端)下，MultiSignal平均检测准确率95.3% vs MultiKrum的91.8%，在Sparse Lie上提升23.2个百分点(72.0%→95.2%)，Advanced Lie上提升4.8个百分点(80.4%→85.2%)。

Conclusion: 基于QUBO的量子退火方法与MultiSignal框架结合，是一种原理性且可扩展的防御手段，能有效应对联邦学习中最具挑战性的拜占庭攻击策略，并且与MultiKrum互补。

Original Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) trains a global model across decentralized clients while preserving data privacy, but at scale it is vulnerable to malicious updates. Byzantine-resilient aggregation methods such as MultiKrum score gradients against their nearest neighbors and can miss malicious updates that preserve the statistical properties of honest ones. We propose a quantum annealing approach that reformulates client selection as a Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) problem, encoding pairwise distances into a cost function solved by quantum annealers (QA). Unlike MultiKrum's greedy per-client scoring, the QUBO formulation jointly optimizes over all subsets to find the mutually closest group of $m$ clients. At small scale (15 clients), QUBO outperforms MultiKrum on the most challenging Byzantine attacks: e.g., Advanced LIE is detected with 95.11% accuracy versus 81.33% on MNIST and 97.78% versus 75.56% on CIFAR-10. QUBO fares poorly on simpler attacks where MultiKrum excels, so the two methods are complementary. QUBO quality also degrades as the number of clients grows. To address this, we introduce a MultiSignal ensemble that uses a dual-feature routing gate based on Euclidean and cosine Krum score gaps to classify attacks into four regimes and routes evasion attacks to a suspicion-penalized QUBO with agreement voting. At 100 clients on MNIST, MultiSignal achieves 95.3% average detection accuracy versus 91.8% for classical MultiKrum, with the largest gains on Sparse Lie (72.0% to 95.2%, +23.2 points) and Advanced Lie (80.4% to 85.2%, +4.8 points). These results show that QUBO-based quantum annealing with MultiSignal is a principled and scalable defense against the most challenging Byzantine strategies in federated learning.

中文摘要翻译: 联邦学习（FL）在去中心化客户端上训练全局模型同时保护数据隐私，但在大规模场景下容易受到恶意更新的攻击。拜占庭鲁棒聚合方法（如MultiKrum）根据近邻距离对梯度进行评分，可能会遗漏那些保持了诚实客户端统计特性的恶意更新。我们提出一种量子退火方法，将客户端选择重新建模为二次无约束二元优化（QUBO）问题，将成对距离编码到由量子退火器（QA）求解的代价函数中。与MultiKrum贪婪的逐客户端评分不同，QUBO公式对所有子集进行联合优化，以寻找相互距离最近的m个客户端组。在小规模（15个客户端）实验中，QUBO在最具挑战性的拜占庭攻击上优于MultiKrum：例如，在MNIST上Advanced LIE的检测准确率为95.11%对比81.33%，在CIFAR-10上为97.78%对比75.56%。在MultiKrum擅长的简单攻击上QUBO表现较差，因此两种方法具有互补性。随着客户端数量增加，QUBO的质量也会下降。为解决这一问题，我们引入MultiSignal集成方法，该方法使用基于欧氏距离和余弦Krum分数差距的双特征路由门，将攻击分为四个区间，并将规避攻击路由到带有疑虑惩罚的QUBO与一致投票机制。在MNIST上100个客户端场景下，MultiSignal的平均检测准确率达到95.3%，优于经典MultiKrum的91.8%，最大提升出现在Sparse Lie（从72.0%提升到95.2%，提升23.2个百分点）和Advanced Lie（从80.4%提升到85.2%，提升4.8个百分点）。这些结果表明，基于QUBO的量子退火与MultiSignal结合是一种原理性且可扩展的防御手段，能够应对联邦学习中最具挑战性的拜占庭策略。

</details>


### [109] [GenTS: A Comprehensive Benchmark Library for Generative Time Series Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17804)
*Chenxi Wang,Xiaorong Wang,Peiyang Li,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了GenTS，一个面向生成式时间序列模型的综合性基准库，包含统一数据预处理、多种生成模型和全景评估指标，并通过实验为模型选择和未来研究方向提供建议。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有时间序列库主要面向判别式模型，采用标准化工作流（如优化MSE），无法兼容生成式模型（如对抗训练、扩散过程）的复杂范式。因此亟需一个专为生成式时间序列模型设计的系统性评估基准。

Method: 构建了GenTS基准库，包含三大核心模块：统一的数据预处理流程、多种生成式模型（覆盖GAN、扩散模型等）的集合，以及全景式评估指标体系；采用模块化设计，支持研究者灵活自定义数据集和模型。

Result: 基于GenTS在多种时间序列任务（合成、预测、插补等）上开展了基准实验，给出了模型选择建议，并指出了未来研究的有前景方向。

Conclusion: GenTS为生成式时间序列模型提供了一个系统、可扩展的评估平台，弥补了现有库的不足，有助于推动该领域的标准化研究与模型选型。

Original Abstract: Generative models have demonstrated remarkable potential in time series analysis tasks, like synthesis, forecasting, imputation, etc. However, offering limited coverage for generative models, existing time series libraries are mainly engineered for discriminative models, with standardized workflows for specific tasks, such as optimizing Mean Squared Errors for time series forecasting. This rigid structure is fundamentally incompatible with the distinct and often complex paradigms of generative models (e.g., adversarial training, diffusion processes), which learn the underlying data distribution rather than a direct input-output mapping. To this end, we proposed GenTS, a comprehensive and extensible benchmark library designed for systematic assessment on generative time series models. GenTS features a unified data preprocessing pipeline, a collection of versatile models, and panoramic evaluation metrics. Its modular design also enables the researchers to flexibly customize beyond our built-in datasets and models. Based on GenTS, we conducted benchmarking experiments under diverse tasks, accordingly offering suggestions for model selection and identifying potential directions for future research. Our codes are open-source at https://github.com/WillWang1113/GenTS. The official tutorials and document are available at https://willwang1113.github.io/GenTS/.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式模型在时间序列分析任务（如合成、预测、插补等）中已展现出显著潜力。然而，现有的时间序列库主要为判别式模型设计，对生成式模型的覆盖有限，且采用针对特定任务的标准化工作流（例如为时间序列预测优化均方误差）。这种僵化的结构从根本上与生成式模型（如对抗训练、扩散过程）独特且通常复杂的范式不兼容，后者学习的是底层数据分布而非直接的输入-输出映射。为此，我们提出了GenTS，一个全面且可扩展的基准库，专用于生成式时间序列模型的系统性评估。GenTS集成了统一的数据预处理流程、多样化的模型集合以及全景式评估指标。其模块化设计还使研究者能够灵活地自定义超出我们内置数据集和模型之外的内容。基于GenTS，我们在多种任务下开展了基准实验，据此为模型选择提供了建议，并指出了未来研究的潜在方向。我们的代码已开源至https://github.com/WillWang1113/GenTS。官方教程和文档请见https://willwang1113.github.io/GenTS/。

</details>


### [110] [DeepArrhythmia: Segment-Contextualized ECG Arrhythmia Classification via Selective Evidence Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16441)
*Jiahui Li,Ruili Fang,Zishuai Liu,WenZhan Song,Jin Lu,Fei Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeepArrhythmia是一个基于工具的多模态框架，通过结合原始ECG信号和波形图像，利用节律上下文和选择性证据获取实现拍级心律失常分类。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有拍级心律失常检测系统将每个心拍视为孤立的局部实例，忽略了心拍标签依赖于多拍节律上下文（包括时序、代偿间歇和拍间形态一致性）这一关键事实。

Method: 提出DeepArrhythmia框架，包含：(1) 多模态输入：结合原始ECG信号和波形图像；(2) 专用工具集：心拍定位、数值节律-形态提取、形态学文本分析；(3) 分段级置信度路由机制：根据置信度在最小证据和丰富证据状态间切换，实现选择性证据获取。

Result: 框架通过解耦生理测量与证据整合、引入节律上下文和显式生理学基础，实现了更准确的拍级心律失常分类。

Conclusion: DeepArrhythmia的智能体设计有效整合了节律上下文、显式生理学基础和选择性证据获取，为拍级ECG心律失常检测提供了新的范式。

Original Abstract: Beat-level Electrocardiography (ECG) arrhythmia detection aims to assign an arrhythmia class to each beat in a recording, yet many existing systems treat beats as isolated local instances. This is limiting because beat labels often depend on multi-beat rhythm context, including timing, compensatory pauses, and beat-to-beat morphological consistency. We present DeepArrhythmia, a tool-grounded multimodal framework for segment-contextualized beat-level ECG arrhythmia classification. Given a multi-beat ECG segment, DeepArrhythmia combines the raw ECG signal and a rendered waveform image, localizes R peaks to identify beat instances, and produces structured beat-level predictions. The framework decouples physiological measurement from evidence integration using specialized tools for beat localization, numerical rhythm--morphology extraction, and morphology-focused textual analysis. DeepArrhythmia uses segment-level confidence to route between minimal and rich evidence states, since richer physiological evidence is not uniformly useful. This agentic design integrates rhythm context, explicit physiological grounding, and selective evidence acquisition for decision making.

中文摘要翻译: 拍级心电图心律失常检测旨在为记录中的每个心拍分配一个心律失常类别，然而许多现有系统将心拍视为孤立的局部实例。这种处理方式具有局限性，因为心拍标签通常依赖于多拍节律上下文，包括时序、代偿间歇以及拍间形态一致性。本文提出DeepArrhythmia，一个基于工具的、面向分段上下文的拍级心电图心律失常分类多模态框架。给定一个多拍ECG分段，DeepArrhythmia结合原始ECG信号与渲染波形图像，定位R峰以识别心拍实例，并生成结构化的拍级预测。该框架通过专用工具（心拍定位、数值节律-形态提取和形态学文本分析）将生理测量与证据整合相解耦。DeepArrhythmia利用分段级置信度在最小证据状态和丰富证据状态之间进行路由，因为更丰富的生理证据并非在所有情况下都同样有效。这种智能体设计整合了节律上下文、显式生理学基础和选择性证据获取，以支持决策制定。

</details>


### [111] [Membership Inference Attacks on Discrete Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16445)
*Shailesh Kasivelrajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）的成员推断攻击（MIA），发现其比当前灰盒基线所显示的要脆弱得多。通过从四个掩码比率的重建损失中提取46维特征向量，训练XGBoost和MLP分类器，在MIMIR基准上达到平均AUC 0.878，超越SAMA基线0.062。消融实验表明ELBO轨迹贡献最大，注意力特征几乎无用。此外，设计了一种影子模型迁移攻击，仅用3个来自无关领域的替代MDLM即可达到0.858的平均AUC，接近白盒oracle水平。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: MDLM的隐私属性在很大程度上尚未被研究，现有基线方法低估了其脆弱性，因此需要更深入的成员推断攻击研究来揭示MDLM的真实隐私风险。

Method: 从MDLM在四个不同掩码比率下的重建损失中提取46维特征向量，训练XGBoost和MLP分类器进行成员推断攻击；同时设计了影子模型迁移攻击，使用K=3个来自无关领域的替代MDLM生成分类器标签，无需访问目标领域数据。

Result: XGBoost在MIMIR基准六个文本领域上平均AUC为0.878，在Pile CC上峰值达0.930，超越SAMA灰盒基线0.062 AUC；ELBO轨迹移除导致AUC下降0.130，而注意力特征贡献低于0.003；影子模型迁移攻击达到0.858平均AUC，仅比白盒oracle低0.020。

Conclusion: MDLM比先前研究显示的更加容易受到成员推断攻击；ELBO轨迹是攻击成功的关键特征，注意力特征几乎无贡献；影子模型迁移攻击是一种实用且近乎同等有效的攻击途径。

Original Abstract: Masked Diffusion Language Models MDLMs replace autoregressive generation with iterative demasking and their privacy properties are largely unstudied. We study membership inference attacks MIA on fine tuned MDLMs and show they are significantly more vulnerable than current grey box baselines suggest. We extract a 46 dimensional feature vector from the models reconstruction loss at four masking ratios and train XGBoost and MLP classifiers on top. On the MIMIR benchmark across six text domains XGBoost achieves mean AUC 0.878 peaking at 0.930 on Pile CC and beats the SAMA grey box baseline by 0.062 AUC on average. A leave one signal out ablation shows that the ELBO trajectory alone drives most of this with a mean drop of 0.130 when removed while attention features add almost nothing below 0.003. We also design a shadow model transfer attack where K equals 3 surrogate MDLMs trained on data from unrelated domains generate classifier labels with no access to the target domain. This achieves 0.858 mean AUC within 0.020 of the white box oracle and establishes shadow model transfer as a practical and near equally effective attack path.

中文摘要翻译: 掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）用迭代去掩码替代了自回归生成，其隐私属性在很大程度上尚未被研究。我们研究了针对微调后MDLM的成员推断攻击（MIA），并表明它们比当前灰盒基线所暗示的更加脆弱。我们从模型在四个掩码比率下的重建损失中提取了一个46维特征向量，并在其上训练XGBoost和MLP分类器。在涵盖六个文本领域的MIMIR基准上，XGBoost达到了平均AUC 0.878，在Pile CC上峰值达到0.930，平均比SAMA灰盒基线高出0.062 AUC。留一信号消融实验表明，ELBO轨迹单独贡献了大部分性能，移除后平均下降0.130，而注意力特征几乎毫无贡献（低于0.003）。我们还设计了一种影子模型迁移攻击，其中K=3个在无关领域数据上训练的替代MDLM生成分类器标签，无需访问目标领域。该攻击达到了0.858的平均AUC，仅比白盒oracle低0.020，并将影子模型迁移确立为一种实用且近乎同等有效的攻击途径。

</details>


### [112] [Avoiding Structural Failure Modes in Tabular Fair SSL: Online Primal-Dual Allocation under Confidence Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16446)
*Hangchun Liang,Changchun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在线原始-对偶分配（OPDA）方法，用于解决表格数据公平半监督学习中静态公平权重导致的掩蔽坍塌和平凡饱和两种失效模式，无需逐数据集调参即可实现非退化运行点。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在高风险的表格数据应用（医疗、信贷、再犯预测）中，半监督学习虽能利用少量标签进行预测，但需要统计公平性保障。现有方法在置信门控伪标签机制下，矩匹配公平正则化器会引发两种失效模式：公平性侵蚀置信度导致伪标签匮乏（掩蔽坍塌），或模型漂移至常数预测器（平凡饱和）。因此需要一种无需逐数据集调参的自适应控制方法。

Method: 提出在线原始-对偶分配（OPDA），一个在线控制器，利用违反信号、风险信号和伪标签健康信号来调度公平性和基于熵的稳定性惩罚，避免了在诊断区间内为每个数据集选择固定公平权重。

Result: 在Adult、ACSIncome和COMPAS三个表格基准上，OPDA缓解了静态权重和简单单信号自适应基线下的退化模式。在Adult和COMPAS上达到与静态λ经验前沿竞争的非退化运行点；在ACSIncome上以更宽的公平-效用权衡保持了效用。相较于简化版OPDA-lite，完整控制器在ACSIncome上向更高效用偏移，Adult上则体现了两种变体间的公平-效用权衡。

Conclusion: OPDA定位为一种无需校准的控制器，能够在表格公平半监督学习中实现非退化运行点，且不需要逐数据集调参。

Original Abstract: Semi-supervised learning (SSL) enables prediction with limited labels, but high-stakes tabular applications (medical, credit, recidivism) require statistical fairness guarantees. We identify a structural conflict in tabular fair SSL through a diagnostic stress test: under confidence-gated pseudo-labeling, moment-matching fairness regularizers can trigger two failure modes -- Masking Collapse (fairness erodes confidence, starving pseudo-labels) and Trivial Saturation (drift to constant predictors). We propose Online Primal-Dual Allocation (OPDA), an online controller that schedules fairness and entropy-based stability penalties using violation, risk, and pseudo-label health signals, avoiding per-dataset selection of a fixed fairness weight within this diagnostic regime. On the evaluated tabular benchmarks (Adult, ACSIncome, COMPAS), OPDA mitigates the degenerate regimes observed under static weighting and simple single-signal adaptive baselines. On Adult and COMPAS, it yields non-degenerate operating points competitive with the empirical static-$λ$ frontier; on ACSIncome, it preserves utility with a wider fairness-utility spread. Relative to OPDA-lite, the full controller mainly shifts the operating point toward higher utility on ACSIncome, while Adult highlights the fairness-utility trade-off between the two variants. These results position OPDA as a calibration-free controller for non-degenerate operating points in tabular fair SSL without per-dataset tuning.

中文摘要翻译: 半监督学习（SSL）能够利用有限标签进行预测，但在高风险表格应用（医疗、信贷、再犯预测）中需要统计公平性保障。我们通过诊断性压力测试发现表格公平半监督学习中存在结构性冲突：在置信门控伪标签机制下，矩匹配公平正则化器会触发两种失效模式——掩蔽坍塌（公平性侵蚀置信度，导致伪标签匮乏）和平凡饱和（漂移至常数预测器）。我们提出在线原始-对偶分配（OPDA），一种在线控制器，利用违反信号、风险信号和伪标签健康信号来调度公平性和基于熵的稳定性惩罚，避免在该诊断区间内为每个数据集选择固定的公平权重。在评估的表格基准（Adult、ACSIncome、COMPAS）上，OPDA缓解了静态权重和简单单信号自适应基线下的退化模式。在Adult和COMPAS上，它取得了与静态-λ经验前沿竞争的非退化运行点；在ACSIncome上，它以更宽的公平-效用权衡保留了效用。相较于OPDA-lite，完整控制器主要在ACSIncome上将运行点向更高效用偏移，而Adult则突出了两种变体之间的公平-效用权衡。这些结果将OPDA定位为一种无需校准的控制器，能够在表格公平半监督学习中实现非退化运行点，且无需逐数据集调参。

</details>


### [113] [Nested Spatio-Temporal Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16447)
*Yinghao Ai,Yukai Zhou,Ruoxi Jiang,Junyi An,Chao Qu,Zhijian Zhou,Shiyu Wang,Fenglei Cao,Zenglin Xu,Furao Shen,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种嵌套式时空预测框架，通过谱聚类构建语义一致的宏观区域，利用未来宏观趋势引导微观细粒度预测，有效过滤噪声并捕捉动态异常，在多个高维数据集上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有时空预测方法主要依赖历史空间先验，无法捕捉随时间演化的动态相关性，且在噪声和非平稳条件下容易产生系统性误差。

Method: 采用谱聚类构建语义一致的宏观区域（理论+实验证明可过滤系统噪声并保留关键趋势），再设计渐进式从粗到细的预测器，将宏观趋势特征融入微观推理过程，实现未来宏观引导的嵌套预测。

Result: 在多个高维数据集上，该方法一致性地优于现有最先进基线模型，验证了未来宏观引导的嵌套预测的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过未来宏观趋势自上而下地指导微观细粒度预测，能够有效应对噪声和动态异常（如周期性偏移），提升时空预测的鲁棒性和准确性。

Original Abstract: Spatiotemporal forecasting is critical for real-world applications like traffic management, yet capturing reliable interactions remains challenging under noisy and non-stationary conditions. Existing methods primarily rely on historical spatial priors, often failing to account for evolving temporal correlations and suffering from systematic errors. In this work, we propose a nested forecasting framework that couples future macro-level regional trends with micro-level historical observations, enabling top-down guidance from abstract future representations for fine-grained forecasting. Specifically, we employ a spectral clustering-based approach to construct semantically coherent regions, providing both theoretical and empirical evidence that this representation effectively filters systematic noise while preserving essential trends. Building on this, we develop a progressive coarse-to-fine predictor to integrate these representative features into the inference process. This enables the model to leverage trend predictions to anticipate dynamic anomalies, such as periodic offsets, in advance. Furthermore, extensive experiments on multiple high-dimensional datasets demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, validating the effectiveness of future macro-guided nested forecasting.

中文摘要翻译: 时空预测对于交通管理等实际应用至关重要，但在噪声和非平稳条件下，捕捉可靠的交互仍然具有挑战性。现有方法主要依赖历史空间先验，往往无法考虑随时间演化的相关性，并遭受系统性误差。本文提出一种嵌套式预测框架，将未来的宏观区域趋势与微观历史观测耦合，实现从抽象的未来表示自上而下地指导细粒度预测。具体来说，我们采用基于谱聚类的方法构建语义一致的区域，并从理论和实证两方面证明该表示能有效过滤系统性噪声同时保留关键趋势。在此基础上，我们开发了一个渐进式从粗到细的预测器，将这些代表性特征融入推理过程。这使得模型能够利用趋势预测提前预判动态异常（如周期性偏移）。此外，在多个高维数据集上的大量实验表明，我们的方法一致性地优于最先进的基线模型，验证了未来宏观引导的嵌套预测的有效性。

</details>


### [114] [Physics-Guided Geometric Diffusion for Macro Placement Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16451)
*Jongho Yoon,Jinsung Jeon,Seokhyeong Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MacroDiff+提出了一种物理引导的几何扩散框架用于VLSI宏布局，通过双域去噪架构和物理引导采样策略，在ISPD2005基准上实现了6.1-6.2%的线长缩减，并在大规模设计中展现出优越的稳定性和可扩展性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据驱动的布局方法难以处理序列依赖关系，且在平衡拓扑连接性与物理约束方面存在不足。

Method: 提出MacroDiff+物理引导几何扩散框架，包含双域去噪架构（异构GNN编码拓扑连接 + Transformer建模全局几何上下文）和物理引导采样推理策略（利用显式梯度引导生成，确保统计合理性和物理有效性）。

Result: 在ISPD2005 MMS基准上，MacroDiff+相比最先进基线方法实现6.1-6.2%的线长缩减；在大规模设计中展现出优越的稳定性和可扩展性，而先前方法无法收敛。

Conclusion: MacroDiff+通过物理引导的扩散模型有效解决了宏布局中的拓扑-物理约束平衡问题，在性能和稳定性上均优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: Macro placement is a pivotal stage in VLSI physical design, fundamentally determining the overall chip performance. Recent data-driven placement methods have demonstrated significant potential, yet they often struggle to handle sequential dependencies and to balance topological connectivity with physical constraints. To bridge this gap, we propose MacroDiff+, a physics-guided geometric diffusion framework. Specifically, we design a dual-domain denoising architecture that couples topological connectivity encoded by heterogeneous GNNs with global geometric context modeled by a Transformer. Furthermore, we introduce Physics-Guided Sampling, an inference strategy that actively steers the generation using explicit gradients to ensure both statistical plausibility and physical validity. On the ISPD2005 MMS benchmarks, MacroDiff+ outperforms state-of-the-art baselines with a 6.1-6.2% reduction in wirelength. Notably, it exhibits superior stability and scalability on large-scale designs where prior methods fail to converge. The source code is available at https://github.com/jhy00n/MacroDiff-plus.

中文摘要翻译: 宏布局是VLSI物理设计中的关键阶段，从根本上决定了芯片的整体性能。近年来，数据驱动的布局方法展示了巨大潜力，但它们往往难以处理序列依赖关系，并且在平衡拓扑连接性与物理约束方面存在不足。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了MacroDiff+——一种物理引导的几何扩散框架。具体而言，我们设计了一种双域去噪架构，将异构GNN编码的拓扑连接与Transformer建模的全局几何上下文相结合。此外，我们引入了物理引导采样推理策略，该策略利用显式梯度主动引导生成过程，以确保统计合理性和物理有效性。在ISPD2005 MMS基准上，MacroDiff+相比最先进的基线方法实现了6.1-6.2%的线长缩减。值得注意的是，在大规模设计中，当先前方法无法收敛时，MacroDiff+展现出优越的稳定性和可扩展性。源代码已公开于https://github.com/jhy00n/MacroDiff-plus。

</details>


### [115] [Peak-Detector: Explainable Peak Detection via Instruction-Tuned Large Language Models in Physiological Sign](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16452)
*Jiahui Li,Yida Zhang,Zixuan Zeng,Jiayu Chen,Yingjian Song,Yin Xiao,Nishan Dong,Junjie Lu,Younghoon Kwon,Xiang Zhang,Jin Lu,Wenzhan Song,Fei Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Peak-Detector框架，利用指令微调的大语言模型，通过“峰值表示”技术实现跨模态（ECG/PPG/BCG/BSG）的可解释心电信号峰值检测，在7个数据集上达到最优或持平性能，并具备自动错误分析与验证能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统峰值检测算法依赖专家知识、通用性差；深度学习方法缺乏可解释性，限制了专家验证和人机交互。亟需一种跨模态、鲁棒且可解释的峰值检测方法。

Method: 提出Peak-Detector框架：1）设计“峰值表示”（peak-representation）技术，将时序数据压缩为保留关键事件信息的紧凑格式，提供归纳偏置；2）采用两阶段优化：监督微调（SFT）+ 强化学习（RL），使用多目标奖励函数；3）构建Peak-Explanation数据集微调模型，赋予模型自解释能力。

Result: 在ECG、PPG、BCG、BSG四种模态、7个数据集（6个公开基准+1个真实世界队列）上，Peak-Detector实现了跨模态强性能，在临床相关时间容忍度下达到最优或持平检测结果，且生成的推理文本可揭示失败模式，支持验证与错误分析。

Conclusion: Peak-Detector成功结合大语言模型的推理能力与专用信号表示，实现了跨模态、可解释的峰值检测，在保证准确性的同时提供了透明的推理过程，为专家验证和人机协同提供了新途径。

Original Abstract: Accurate peak detection across diverse cardiac physiological signals, including the Electrocardiogram (ECG), Photoplethysmogram (PPG), Ballistocardiogram (BCG), and Bodyseismography (BSG), is fundamental for cardiovascular monitoring but is often hindered by artifacts and signal variability. Conventional algorithms are typically engineered with expert knowledge for a single signal modality, limiting their generalizability. Conversely, deep learning-based methods often lack interpretability, limiting transparency for expert verification and hindering expert-computer interaction. To address these limitations, we introduce Peak-Detector, a novel framework that leverages instruction-tuned Large Language Models (LLMs) for robust, cross-modal, and explainable peak detection. A core innovation of our framework is a "peak-representation" technique that transforms time-series data into a condensed format, preserving critical event information while significantly reducing signal length. This representation provides a crucial inductive bias, guiding the LLM to reason over physiologically meaningful events rather than raw, noisy data. The model is optimized through a two-stage process: supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by reinforcement learning (RL) with a multi-objective reward function. The model's self-explanation capabilities are cultivated by fine-tuning on a custom-built Peak-Explanation dataset. Across four modalities-ECG, PPG, BCG, and BSG-spanning seven datasets (six public benchmarks plus one real-world cohort), Peak-Detector demonstrates strong cross-modal performance, achieving best or tied-best detection under clinically relevant temporal tolerance. Beyond accuracy, the generated rationales surface failure modes and support verification and error analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 准确的心跳峰值检测是心血管监测的基础，但常受伪迹和信号变异性的干扰。传统算法通常依赖专家知识针对单一信号模态设计，通用性有限；而基于深度学习的方法往往缺乏可解释性，限制了专家验证的透明度，阻碍了人机交互。为解决这些问题，我们提出了Peak-Detector，一个利用指令微调的大语言模型实现鲁棒、跨模态且可解释峰值检测的新框架。该框架的核心创新是一种“峰值表示”技术，将时间序列数据转化为紧凑格式，在显著缩短信号长度的同时保留关键事件信息。这种表示为模型提供了关键的归纳偏置，引导其基于具有生理意义的事件进行推理，而非直接处理原始噪声数据。模型通过两阶段流程优化：监督微调后接强化学习，并配有多目标奖励函数。通过在自建的Peak-Explanation数据集上进行微调，模型培养了自解释能力。在涵盖ECG、PPG、BCG和BSG四种模态的七个数据集（六个公开基准加一个真实世界队列）上，Peak-Detector展现了强大的跨模态性能，在临床相关时间容忍度下达到了最优或持平的检测结果。此外，模型生成的推理文本能够揭示失败模式，支持验证和错误分析。

</details>


### [116] [Identifiable Token Correspondence for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16457)
*Youngin Kim,Ray Sun,Inho Kim,Bumsoo Park,Hyun Oh Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对基于Transformer的世界模型在长程 rollout 中存在时间不一致性（物体复制、消失、变异）的问题，本文通过将下一帧预测建模为带有潜在 token 对应变量的结构化概率推理问题，提出了显式建模跨时间 token 对应关系的方法，在4个基准上取得了SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的世界模型将下一帧预测纯粹视为token生成问题，缺乏对跨时间token之间对应关系的显式建模，导致长程 rollout 中出现时间不一致性（物体复制、消失、变异）。

Method: 将下一帧预测形式化为带有潜在 token 对应变量的结构化概率推理问题，每个下一帧 token 要么通过从上一帧复制一个 token 来解释，要么通过生成一个新 token 来解释。

Result: 在4个挑战性基准上达到SOTA；在Craftax-classic基准上，回报达72.5%，得分达35.6%，显著超越之前最优的67.4%和27.9%。

Conclusion: 显式建模跨时间token对应关系能有效解决世界模型长程 rollout 中的时间不一致性问题，显著提升性能。

Original Abstract: Transformer-based world models have shown strong performance in visual reinforcement learning, but often suffer from temporal inconsistency in long-horizon rollouts, including object duplication, disappearance, and transmutation. A key reason is that most existing approaches treat next-frame prediction purely as a token generation problem, without explicitly modeling correspondence between tokens across time. We formulate next-frame prediction as a structured probabilistic inference problem with latent token correspondence variables, deriving a model in which each next-frame token is explained either by copying a token from the previous frame or by generating a new token. Our experiments show state-of-the-art performance on 4 challenging benchmarks. The proposed method achieves a return of 72.5% and a score of 35.6% on the Craftax-classic benchmark, significantly surpassing the previous best of 67.4% and 27.9%. We release our source code on https://github.com/snu-mllab/Identifiable-Token-Correspondence.

中文摘要翻译: 基于Transformer的世界模型在视觉强化学习中展现出强劲性能，但在长程 rollout 中常出现时间不一致性问题，包括物体复制、消失和变异。一个关键原因是，现有大多数方法将下一帧预测纯粹视为token生成问题，而未显式建模跨时间的token对应关系。本文将下一帧预测形式化为一个带有潜在token对应变量的结构化概率推理问题，推导出一个模型，其中每个下一帧token要么通过从上一帧复制一个token来解释，要么通过生成一个新token来解释。我们的实验在4个挑战性基准上取得了最先进的性能。所提出的方法在Craftax-classic基准上实现了72.5%的回报率和35.6%的得分，显著超越了之前最优的67.4%和27.9%。我们在https://github.com/snu-mllab/Identifiable-Token-Correspondence上公开了源代码。

</details>


### [117] [Seeking the Unfamiliar but Memorable: Conceptual Creativity as Meta-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16477)
*Mengye Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种创造者-评估者（Creator-Appraiser）框架，通过元学习让冻结的扩散模型无需额外文本条件即可生成新颖且可快速学习的视觉概念，包括风格变化和概念组合。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有生成模型在相同提示下重复采样只能产生风格相似、内容雷同的变体，缺乏真正创造新颖概念的能力。作者希望定义并实现一种能生成对观察者而言陌生但可快速学习的新颖刺激的创造力机制。

Method: 构建Creator-Appraiser元学习框架：Creator（扩散模型）生成候选样本，Appraiser（自适应编码器）通过少量内循环学习步骤适应该样本，Appraiser的损失改善量作为奖励信号反向优化Creator。在MNIST上使用自编码器Appraiser，在自然图像上使用带低秩适配器（LoRA）的CLIP Appraiser。扩散模型保持冻结且无额外语言条件。

Result: 仅凭元学习梯度信号，冻结的扩散模型就能生成其基模型原本无法独立产生的风格变体和概念组合，证明了无需语言条件即可通过内循环学习驱动创造性生成。

Conclusion: 创造力可以被形式化为一个元学习过程——生成器产生对新异刺激的快速适应能力，该框架为理解和实现机器创造力提供了新的理论视角和实用方法。

Original Abstract: What does it mean to create a new concept, rather than retrieve a familiar one? Repeatedly sampling a generative model at the same prompt produces variations with similar styles and typical content. We propose that creativity is the production of stimuli that are unfamiliar to an adaptive observer at first sight, but quickly learnable from a few exposures. We formalize this as a Creator-Appraiser pair: a Creator generates a candidate, an Appraiser adapts to it for a few inner-loop learning steps, and the Appraiser's improvement becomes the reward the Creator optimizes through. We instantiate the framework with diffusion as the Creator, an autoencoder Appraiser on MNIST, and a CLIP Appraiser with a low-rank adapter for natural images. The diffusion model remains frozen with no additional language conditioning; the meta-learning gradient is enough to produce both stylistic variations and concept compositions that the base model does not generate on its own.

中文摘要翻译: 创造一个全新的概念意味着什么？它与检索一个熟悉的概念有何不同？在相同提示下重复采样生成模型会产生风格相似、内容雷同的变体。我们提出，创造力是指生成那些对自适应观察者而言乍看之下陌生、但经过少量接触即可快速学习的刺激。我们将此形式化为一个创造者-评估者（Creator-Appraiser）对：创造者生成候选样本，评估者通过少量内循环学习步骤适应该样本，评估者的改进幅度作为奖励信号供创造者优化。我们在MNIST上以扩散模型作为创造者、自编码器作为评估者进行实例化，在自然图像上以扩散模型作为创造者、带低秩适配器的CLIP作为评估者。扩散模型保持冻结且无需额外语言条件；仅凭元学习梯度就足以产生基模型本身无法生成的风格变体和概念组合。

</details>


### [118] [Global Convergence of Sampling-Based Nonconvex Optimization through Diffusion-Style Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16520)
*Zeji Yi,Chaoyi Pan,Guanya Shi,Guannan Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过平滑视角建立了基于抽样的优化（SBO）的非渐近收敛分析，揭示了平滑带来的覆盖-最优性权衡，并提出了可证明收敛到全局最优的退火SBO算法（DIDA）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: SBO算法（如交叉熵方法、进化算法）在无梯度非凸优化中取得了很多成功，但其收敛性理论尚不清晰，缺乏非渐近收敛分析。

Method: (1) 将SBO重新解释为在平滑目标上的梯度下降，类似于扩散模型中的噪声条件得分上升；(2) 对平滑目标进行景观分析，揭示覆盖-最优性权衡；(3) 提出扩散启发的双退火（DIDA）算法，实现可证明的全局收敛。

Result: (1) 揭示了平滑如何帮助逃离局部极小值，以及覆盖-最优性权衡；(2) 建立了SBO算法收敛到全局最优邻域的非渐近保证；(3) 数值实验验证了景观分析结果，并展示了DIDA相比其他无梯度优化方法的优越性能。

Conclusion: 论文为SBO提供了严格的理论基础，并提出了可证明全局收敛的退火算法DIDA，同时讨论了结果对扩散模型的启示。

Original Abstract: Sampling-based optimization (SBO), like cross-entropy method and evolutionary algorithms, has achieved many successes in solving non-convex problems without gradients, yet its convergence is poorly understood. In this paper, we establish a non-asymptotic convergence analysis for SBO through the lens of smoothing. Specifically, we recast SBO as gradient descent on a smoothed objective, mirroring noise-conditioned score ascent in diffusion models. Our first contribution is a landscape analysis of the smoothed objective, demonstrating how smoothing helps escape local minima and uncovering a fundamental coverage-optimality trade-off: smoothing renders the landscape more benign by enlarging the locally convex region around the global minimizer, but at the cost of introducing an optimality gap. Building on this insight, we establish non-asymptotic convergence guarantees for SBO algorithms to a neighborhood of the global minimizer. Furthermore, we propose an annealed SBO algorithm, Diffusion-Inspired Dual-Annealing (DIDA), which is provably convergent to the global optimum. We conduct extensive numerical experiments to verify our landscape results and also demonstrate the compelling performance of DIDA compared to other gradient-free optimization methods. Lastly, we discuss implications of our results for diffusion models.

中文摘要翻译: 基于抽样的优化（SBO），如交叉熵方法和进化算法，在无需梯度的非凸问题求解中取得了许多成功，但其收敛性尚不明确。在本文中，我们通过平滑的视角建立了SBO的非渐近收敛分析。具体地，我们将SBO重新表述为平滑目标上的梯度下降，这与扩散模型中的噪声条件得分上升相呼应。我们的第一个贡献是对平滑目标的景观分析，展示了平滑如何帮助逃离局部极小值，并揭示了一个基本的覆盖-最优性权衡：平滑通过扩大全局最优解周围的局部凸区域使景观更加良性，但代价是引入了最优性间隙。基于这一见解，我们建立了SBO算法收敛到全局最优解邻域的非渐近保证。此外，我们提出了一种退火SBO算法——扩散启发双退火（DIDA），该算法可证明收敛到全局最优解。我们进行了大量数值实验来验证我们的景观分析结果，并展示了DIDA相比其他无梯度优化方法的显著性能。最后，我们讨论了研究结果对扩散模型的启示。

</details>


### [119] [Hypergraph Pattern Machine: Compositional Tokenization for Higher-Order Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16527)
*Kyrie Zhao,Zehong Wang,Tianyi Ma,Fang Wu,Xiangru Tang,Pietro Lio,Sheng Wang,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出超图模式机（HGPM），将超图学习范式从消息传递转变为子集组合模式学习，通过标记化组合子集、构建包含偏序DAG并训练包含感知Transformer，有效建模高阶交互的组合性信号，在十个基准上达到SOTA，并能在药物副作用预测中区分特征相同的候选药物。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有超图学习方法仅通过消息传递在观测超边上传播信息，忽略了交互组合性这一核心结构信号——即高阶关系是组合性的、涌现性的还是抑制性的。在多药联合场景中，这种信号的缺失会导致危险的药物组合被误分类，例如无法区分抑制副作用的药物添加与无关药物添加。

Method: HGPM包含三个步骤：（1）将组合性子集进行Token化，将超边分解为子集序列；（2）将这些子集组织成包含偏序有向无环图（Inclusion DAG）以捕获子集之间的包含关系；（3）训练一个包含感知的Transformer（Inclusion-aware Transformer），通过掩码重建（Masked Reconstruction）任务进行学习，从而建模高阶交互的组合性模式。

Result: 在十个超图基准数据集上，HGPM达到或超越现有最先进方法。在真实的药物不良反应预测案例中，HGPM能够正确识别出抑制副作用的药物添加，而现有方法在特征相同的候选药物中无法做出这种区分。

Conclusion: HGPM通过从消息传递转向组合模式学习，成功建模了超图中被忽视的交互组合性信号，在标准基准和具有挑战性的实际应用中均展现出优越性能，特别是具备区分特征相同但组合行为不同的候选药物的能力。

Original Abstract: Hypergraphs model higher-order relations that drive real-world decisions, from drug prescriptions to recommendations. A central structural signal in such data, beyond what pairwise relations can express, is interaction compositionality: whether a higher-order relation is compositional, emergent, or inhibitory with respect to its observed or unobserved sets. In polypharmacy, the regime decides whether a drug should be dropped, kept, or excluded: a compositional drug triple can be safely simplified, an emergent triple requires all drugs jointly, and an inhibitory triple flags a drug that disrupts an existing interaction. However, existing hypergraph learning methods, which merely propagate messages over observed hyperedges, leave this compositional signal unmodeled, allowing dangerous drug combinations to slip through and be misclassified.
