Extensive evaluations across both offline and online benchmarks demonstrate SE-GA achieves state-of-the-art performance, reaching success rates of 89.0\% on ScreenSpot and 75.8\% on the challenging AndroidControl-High dataset. Furthermore, significant improvements on the AndroidWorld benchmark highlight the superior generalization to dynamic environments. Open source code: https://github.com/jinshilong-dev/SE-GA

中文摘要翻译: 自主图形用户界面（GUI）智能体在执行多步骤任务时，通常受限于有限的上下文窗口以及无法适应动态环境的静态策略。为解决这些局限，本文提出了自进化GUI智能体（SE-GA），一种将分层记忆结构与迭代自我改进机制相结合的新型框架。我们方法的核心是测试时记忆扩展（TTME），它通过动态检索情节记忆、语义记忆和经验记忆，在推理过程中提供关键上下文，从而支持长程规划。为确保持续学习，我们引入了记忆增强自我进化（MASE），这是一种训练流程，利用TTME收集的数据来稳定和增强智能体的基础策略。在离线和在线基准上的广泛评估表明，SE-GA达到了最先进的性能，在ScreenSpot上成功率为89.0%，在具有挑战性的AndroidControl-High数据集上成功率为75.8%。此外，在AndroidWorld基准上的显著改进突显了其在动态环境中的卓越泛化能力。开源代码：https://github.com/jinshilong-dev/SE-GA

</details>

### [150] [Learning Displacement-Aware WiFi Representations for Weakly Supervised Relative Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16357)
*Tzu-Ti Wei,Po-Cheng Chen,Yu-Chee Tseng,Jen-Jee Chen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出交叉路径（IP）框架，利用弱监督的惯性传感步态运动向量，通过跨模态学习对齐WiFi指纹轨迹与位移轨迹，在共享潜空间中实现相对定位，无需密集坐标标注。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有WiFi指纹室内定位方法大多依赖绝对定位与密集坐标标注，成本高昂。本文旨在研究一种本质不同的问题——相对定位，直接估计两条WiFi指纹轨迹之间的位移，从而降低标注开销。

Method: 提出Intersection Pathway (IP) 跨模态学习框架，将指纹轨迹（f-traces）与位移轨迹（d-traces）对齐到共享潜空间；通过强制潜空间具有加法结构，使潜空间中的加减法对应物理运动合成，从而直接推断相对位移。

Result: 在基于真实测量数据合成的数据集上，该方法学习了位移感知的WiFi表示，在不同位移范围内实现了准确的相对定位；此外，训练好的模型可扩展至基于稀疏锚点的少样本绝对定位。

Conclusion: 弱监督的跨模态学习能够有效实现WiFi指纹相对定位，潜空间加法结构的设计是核心创新，且模型具有良好的泛化能力，可迁移至少样本绝对定位场景。

Original Abstract: WiFi fingerprint-based indoor localization has been widely studied, but most existing approaches focus on absolute positioning and rely on dense coordinate annotations, which are costly to obtain at scale. In this paper, we study a fundamentally different problem: relative localization, where the goal is to directly estimate the displacement between two WiFi fingerprint traces without predicting their absolute positions. To reduce annotation overhead, we adopt weak supervision in the form of stepwise motion vectors obtained from inertial sensing. We propose Intersection Pathway (IP), a cross-modal learning framework that aligns fingerprint traces (f-traces) and displacement traces (d-traces) in a shared latent space. The key idea is to enforce an additive structure in the latent space, such that latent addition and subtraction correspond to physical motion composition, enabling direct relative-displacement inference. Experiments on a synthesized dataset derived from real measurements demonstrate that the proposed method learns displacement-aware WiFi representations and achieves accurate relative localization across varying displacement ranges. Furthermore, the learned model can be extended to few-shot absolute localization with sparse anchors.

中文摘要翻译: 基于WiFi指纹的室内定位已被广泛研究，但现有方法大多聚焦于绝对定位，并依赖密集的坐标标注，这在大规模部署时成本高昂。本文研究一个本质不同的问题：相对定位，其目标是直接估计两条WiFi指纹轨迹之间的位移，而无需预测它们的绝对位置。为了降低标注开销，我们采用从惯性传感中获得的步态运动向量作为弱监督信号。我们提出交叉路径（Intersection Pathway, IP），一种跨模态学习框架，将指纹轨迹（f-traces）与位移轨迹（d-traces）在共享潜空间中对齐。核心思想是在潜空间中强制施加加法结构，使得潜空间中的加法与减法对应于物理运动的合成，从而实现直接的相对位移推断。在基于真实测量数据合成的数据集上的实验表明，所提出的方法学习了位移感知的WiFi表示，并在不同位移范围内实现了准确的相对定位。此外，训练好的模型可以扩展到基于稀疏锚点的少样本绝对定位。

</details>


### [151] [Overcoming the Intrinsic Performance Limitations of MEMS IMU via Diffusion-Based Generative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16391)
*Jiarui Lv,Feng Zhu,Xiaohong Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于扩散生成学习的框架，利用低成本IMU数据生成高保真虚拟IMU数据，突破低成本IMU的性能限制，显著提升导航定位与姿态估计精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 低成本IMU受硬件限制精度不足，而生成式人工智能在高保真信号重建方面展现潜力，因此希望利用扩散模型从低成本IMU测量中合成高保真虚拟IMU数据，以突破硬件性能瓶颈。

Method: 构建基于U-Net架构的条件扩散模型，以高精度IMU测量作为真实先验，低成本IMU测量作为条件输入，生成虚拟高保真IMU数据，并将其用于后续导航定位任务。

Result: 生成的虚拟IMU数据在定位和姿态估计上显著优于原始低成本IMU测量；在机载测绘实验中，该方法生成了更薄且一致性更强的点云。

Conclusion: 所提框架突破了低成本IMU的性能极限，证明了基于扩散生成学习用于合成虚拟高精度IMU数据的潜力。

Original Abstract: Inertial measurement units (IMUs) are fundamental sensing components in multi-source integrated navigation systems, and their performance directly determines the accuracy and reliability of solutions. However, the precision of low-cost IMUs is inherently constrained by hardware limitations. Recently, generative artificial intelligence has demonstrated remarkable capability in modeling complex data distributions and reconstructing high-fidelity signals. Motivated by this, we propose a diffusion-based generative learning framework for synthesizing high-fidelity virtual IMU data from low-cost IMU measurements. Specifically, a conditional diffusion model based on a U-Net architecture is constructed, where high-grade IMU measurements are utilized as ground-truth priors and low-cost IMU measurements are employed as conditional inputs. The virtual IMU data generated by the model is used for subsequent navigation and localization tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that the generated virtual IMU data significantly outperform the original low-cost IMU measurements in both positioning and attitude estimation. Furthermore, we transfer the model to airborne mapping experiments, where the proposed method produces thinner and more consistent point clouds. Overall, the proposed framework breaks the performance limits of low-cost IMU and demonstrates the potential of diffusion-based generative learning for virtual high-grade IMU data.

中文摘要翻译: 惯性测量单元（IMU）是多源组合导航系统中的基础传感组件，其性能直接决定解算结果的精度和可靠性。然而，低成本IMU的精度受硬件条件固有制约。近期，生成式人工智能在建模复杂数据分布和重建高保真信号方面展现出卓越能力。受此启发，我们提出了一种基于扩散生成学习的框架，用于从低成本IMU测量中合成高保真虚拟IMU数据。具体而言，构建了一个基于U-Net架构的条件扩散模型，其中高精度IMU测量作为真实先验，低成本IMU测量作为条件输入。模型生成的虚拟IMU数据用于后续导航与定位任务。实验结果表明，生成的虚拟IMU数据在定位和姿态估计方面均显著优于原始低成本IMU测量。此外，我们将模型迁移至机载测绘实验，所提方法生成了更薄且一致性更强的点云。总体而言，所提框架突破了低成本IMU的性能极限，展示了基于扩散生成学习用于虚拟高精度IMU数据的潜力。

</details>


### [152] [Subject-Specific Analysis of Self-Initiated Attention Shifts from EEG with Controlled Internal and External Attention Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18251)
*Yuwen Zeng,Dengzhe Hou,Zhang Zhang,Sai Sun,Yongsong Huang,Chia-huei Tseng,Satoshi Shioiri*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文利用可解释机器学习（SHAP）分析多维度EEG特征，证明在受控实验范式下，任务约束的自我发起注意力转移与外部指令转移可通过准备期EEG活动进行可靠区分，其中高频段和额叶区域贡献显著，为个性化异步脑机接口提供了基础。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自我发起的注意力转移在自主行为中至关重要，但由于缺乏明确的时间标记难以研究。先前工作虽探索了其神经关联，但尚不清楚多维度EEG特征如何在可解释的计算框架内对其表征做出贡献。

Method: 采用机器学习方法进行两个互补分析：(1) 面向性能的频率特异性地形图模式评估；(2) 基于SHAP（SHapley Additive exPlanations）模型的特征归因分析，以结构化方式展示感兴趣区域的频谱特征如何影响模型决策。

Result: 结果显示可靠的被试内分类性能，表明准备期EEG活动包含被试特定的可区分信息。高频段和额叶区域对模型决策贡献强烈，但需谨慎解释高频EEG信号中非神经伪迹的潜在影响。

Conclusion: 本研究凸显了可解释机器学习在受控实验环境下分析被试特定EEG信号模式的价值，对个性化异步脑机接口系统具有潜在应用前景。

Original Abstract: Self-initiated attention shifts play a critical role in voluntary behavior but are difficult to study due to the absence of explicit temporal markers. While previous studies have examined their neural correlates, it remains unclear how multi-dimensional electroencephalography (EEG) features contribute to their characterization within an interpretable computational framework. In this study, we build on an experimental paradigm developed in our previous work, which enables controlled comparison between task-constrained self-initiated shifts and externally instructed shifts under identical visual stimulation. Within this setting, we investigate whether preparatory EEG activity can distinguish these two types of attention shifts. We adopt a machine learning-based approach and conduct two complementary analyses: (1) a performance-oriented assessment of frequency-specific topographic patterns, and (2) a model-based feature attribution analysis using SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP). These analyses provide a structured view of how spectral features across regions of interest contribute to model behavior. Our results demonstrate reliable within-subject classification performance, indicating that preparatory EEG activity contains subject-specific discriminative information within this paradigm. The analysis shows that higher-frequency bands and frontal regions contribute strongly to model decisions, although such contributions should be interpreted cautiously due to the potential influence of non-neural artifacts in high-frequency EEG signals. Overall, this work highlights the value of interpretable machine learning for analyzing subject-specific EEG signal patterns in a controlled experimental setting, with potential applications in personalized and asynchronous brain-machine interface systems.

中文摘要翻译: 自我发起的注意力转移在自主行为中扮演关键角色，但由于缺乏明确的时间标记而难以研究。尽管先前研究已探索了其神经关联，但多维度脑电图（EEG）特征如何在可解释的计算框架内贡献于其表征仍不清楚。在本研究中，我们基于前期工作开发的实验范式，该范式能够在相同视觉刺激条件下，对任务约束的自我发起转移与外部指令转移进行受控比较。在此设定下，我们探究准备期EEG活动能否区分这两种注意力转移类型。我们采用基于机器学习的方法，进行了两项互补分析：(1) 面向性能的频率特异性地形图模式评估；(2) 基于SHapley Additive exPlanations（SHAP）的模型特征归因分析。这些分析提供了感兴趣区域的频谱特征如何影响模型行为的结构化视图。我们的结果显示了可靠的被试内分类性能，表明在此范式下准备期EEG活动包含被试特定的可区分信息。分析显示高频段和额叶区域对模型决策贡献显著，但由于高频EEG信号中非神经伪迹的潜在影响，这些贡献应谨慎解释。总体而言，本研究凸显了可解释机器学习在受控实验环境中分析被试特定EEG信号模式的价值，对个性化异步脑机接口系统具有潜在应用前景。

</details>


### [153] [Q-Enhanced SH-SAW Ladder Filter in Thin-Film Lithium Tantalate Using Bartlett Apodization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16586)
*Taran Anusorn,Tzu-Hsuan Hsu,Yuchen Ma,Ruochen Lu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种采用Bartlett窗切趾技术的42°Y切薄膜铌酸锂SH-SAW谐振器设计，在4.35 GHz将品质因数从688提升至1,522，并实现4.3 GHz三阶梯形滤波器，插入损耗1.59 dB，3 dB分数带宽3.24%，带外抑制>14 dB，尺寸仅0.4 mm²，为低成本、小型化射频前端提供了有前景的声学解决方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 克服薄膜铝金属化及欧姆损耗的限制，提升SH-SAW谐振器的品质因数，实现低损耗、紧凑型GHz射频前端滤波器。

Method: 在42°Y切薄膜铌酸锂(LiTaO3)/SiO2/Si平台上，采用Bartlett窗切趾(apodization)技术对叉指换能器进行加权设计，以抑制带内杂散模式并提升品质因数。

Result: 1) 品质因数从688提升至1,522；2) 4.3 GHz三阶梯形滤波器插入损耗1.59 dB（对比传统滤波器的1.65 dB）；3) 3 dB分数带宽3.24%；4) 带外抑制>14 dB；5) 芯片面积仅0.4 mm²。

Conclusion: 切趾型薄膜LiTaO3设计在低损耗、小型化、低成本射频声学器件方面极具潜力，适用于下一代通信与传感应用。

Original Abstract: Shear-horizontal surface acoustic wave (SH-SAW) filters have shown strong potential for low-loss, compact, GHz-frequency RF front ends. In this work, we demonstrate a high-performance SH-SAW filter design at 4.35 GHz utilizing 42°Y-cut thin-film lithium tantalate (LiTaO3) on a SiO2/Si platform. Despite the limitations of thin aluminum metallization and its associated ohmic losses, we show that implementing a Bartlett window apodization technique, primarily intended for in-band spurious-mode suppression, yields a significantly improved quality factor (Q) of 1,522 from 688 in conventional interdigitated SH-SAW resonators. This enhancement enables a third-order ladder filter at 4.3 GHz with an insertion loss of 1.59 dB, compared with 1.65 dB for a conventional SH-SAW filter. In addition, our filter with apodized resonator designs achieves a 3 dB fractional bandwidth (FBW) of 3.24% and out-of-band rejection exceeding 14 dB, all within a compact footprint of 0.4 mm2. These results suggest that apodized thin-film LiTaO3 designs are highly promising for low-loss, miniaturized, cost-effective radio frequency acoustic solutions in next-generation communication and sensing applications.

中文摘要翻译: 剪切水平表面声波(SH-SAW)滤波器在低损耗、紧凑型GHz射频前端中表现出巨大潜力。本文在SiO2/Si平台上的42°Y切薄膜铌酸锂(LiTaO3)上，展示了一种4.35 GHz的高性能SH-SAW滤波器设计。尽管受限于薄膜铝金属化及其欧姆损耗，我们证明采用Bartlett窗切趾技术（主要用于带内杂散模式抑制）可将传统叉指SH-SAW谐振器的品质因数(Q)从688显著提升至1,522。这一提升使得4.3 GHz的三阶梯形滤波器实现了1.59 dB的插入损耗，而传统SH-SAW滤波器为1.65 dB。此外，采用切趾谐振器设计的滤波器实现了3.24%的3 dB分数带宽(FBW)和超过14 dB的带外抑制，且所有设计均在0.4 mm²的紧凑尺寸内完成。这些结果表明，切趾型薄膜LiTaO3设计对于下一代通信与传感应用中的低损耗、小型化、高性价比射频声学解决方案极具前景。

</details>


### [154] [Uplink Channel Estimation for Multi-User MISO Systems Assisted by a Fluid Reconfigurable Intelligent Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16609)
*Amarilton L. Magalhães,André L. F. de Almeida,George C. Alexandropoulos*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对流体可重构智能表面（FRIS）辅助系统中因位置不确定性导致的信道估计难题，提出了一种基于双时间尺度FRIS配置协议与张量建模的闭式信道估计框架，有效联合估计了单独信道与运动引起的相位系数。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: FRIS系统中反射元件的实际位置可能因校准误差或运动偏差而产生不确定性，这会影响信道状态信息的准确性。然而，现有研究尚未探索位置不确定性下的FRIS信道估计问题。

Method: 提出了一种基于双时间尺度FRIS配置协议的信道估计框架，利用正交导频序列和张量建模，推导出闭式解，联合估计各用户的单独信道与运动引起的相位系数。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提方法在未知位置偏差存在的情况下取得了显著性能。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了FRIS系统在位置不确定条件下信道估计的研究空白，验证了张量建模与闭式求解在该场景下的有效性。

Original Abstract: Fluid reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (FRISs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for wireless communications, wherein the reflecting elements can dynamically select their effective radiating positions from a dense preset grid, thereby introducing an additional degree of freedom. In contrast to conventional RIS architectures, FRISs can achieve spatial diversity with fewer physical elements. However, beyond the cascaded channel structure, FRIS-assisted systems are also affected by uncertainties arising from element-position mismatches caused by calibration inaccuracies or motion errors, which may degrade channel state information. To the best of our knowledge, channel estimation (CE) for FRIS-assisted systems under position uncertainty remains unexplored. To fill this gap, we propose a CE framework for a multi-user FRIS-assisted uplink system based on a two-time-scale FRIS configuration protocol that captures both reflection phase-shift and element-motion dynamics. By capitalizing on orthogonal pilot sequences and tensor modeling, we derive a closed-form solution that jointly estimates the individual channels and the motion-induced phase coefficients. Numerical results demonstrate notable performance in the presence of unknown position deviations.

中文摘要翻译: 流体可重构智能表面（FRIS）最近作为一种有前景的无线通信范式出现，其中反射元件可以从密集预设网格中动态选择其有效辐射位置，从而引入额外的自由度。与传统的RIS架构相比，FRIS可以用更少的物理元件实现空间分集。然而，除了级联信道结构外，FRIS辅助系统还受到由校准误差或运动误差导致的元件位置失配所引起的不确定性的影响，这可能降低信道状态信息。据我们所知，位置不确定性下的FRIS辅助系统信道估计（CE）尚未被探索。为填补这一空白，我们针对多用户FRIS辅助上行链路系统提出了一种基于双时间尺度FRIS配置协议的信道估计框架，该协议同时捕捉了反射相移和元件运动动态。通过利用正交导频序列和张量建模，我们推导出一个闭式解，联合估计了单独信道和运动引起的相位系数。数值结果表明，该方法在存在未知位置偏差的情况下性能显著。

</details>


### [155] [Constellation-Independent Range Estimation in Payload-Based OFDM-ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16831)
*Dongil Yang,Kaitao Meng,Christos Masouros,Kawon Han*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种感兴趣区域失配滤波器（ROI-MMF），用于解决非恒包络调制符号引起的OFDM-ISAC感知接收机数据依赖旁瓣问题，通过闭式解实现低复杂度旁瓣抑制，其测距MSE接近CRB，并经过实际平台验证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在OFDM-ISAC系统中，非恒包络调制（如QAM）符号会在多目标及杂波环境中产生数据依赖的高旁瓣，严重影响目标探测性能。传统的匹配滤波（MF）和互逆滤波（RF）无法有效抑制这些旁瓣，亟需一种既能抑制指定区域内旁瓣、又能保持主瓣响应且计算高效的感知接收机设计方法。

Method: 提出一种感兴趣区域失配滤波器（ROI-MMF），通过在时延域指定区域内设计失配滤波器来压制旁瓣，同时保持主瓣响应。利用Woodbury矩阵恒等式，推导出高效的闭式解实现，计算复杂度仅与ROI大小相关，而非子载波数。

Result: 理论推导了ROI-MMF的测距均方误差（MSE）表达式；仿真表明，在不同调制星座下，所提方法测距MSE接近克拉美-罗界（CRB），性能优于传统匹配滤波和互逆滤波；通过外场实际OFDM-ISAC测试平台验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的ROI-MMF能在非恒包络调制条件下有效抑制数据依赖旁瓣，同时保持目标测距精度接近理论最优界，且计算复杂度低，易于实际部署，为OFDM-ISAC系统提供了一种高性能感知接收机方案。

Original Abstract: Orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) is a key waveform for integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) due to its spectral efficiency and compatibility with modern wireless standards. In multi-target and clutter-rich environments, however, payload-based OFDM-ISAC can suffer from data-dependent sidelobes induced by non-constant-modulus modulation symbols. To overcome these limitations, this paper proposes a region-of-interest mismatched filter (ROI-MMF) that suppresses sidelobes within a prescribed delay region while preserving the mainlobe response. By leveraging the Woodbury identity, the proposed design admits an efficient closed-form implementation whose complexity scales with the ROI size rather than the number of subcarriers. We theoretically provide the ranging mean-square error (MSE) of the designed ROI-MMF, which shows the superior performance compared to conventional matched filtering (MF) and reciprocal filtering (RF) sensing receivers. Simulations across various constellations show that the proposed sensing receiver achieves a ranging MSE approaching the Cramér-Rao bound (CRB), which notably confirms that our design preserves the target ranging performance even under the non-constant-modulus constellation. Finally, the framework is experimentally validated with our over-the-air OFDM-ISAC testbed.

中文摘要翻译: 正交频分复用（OFDM）因其频谱效率高且与现代无线标准兼容，成为集成感知与通信（ISAC）的关键波形。然而，在多目标和杂波密集环境中，基于载荷的OFDM-ISAC会受到非恒包络调制符号所引发的数据依赖旁瓣的影响。为克服这些限制，本文提出了一种感兴趣区域失配滤波器（ROI-MMF），能够在指定时延区域内抑制旁瓣同时保持主瓣响应。通过利用Woodbury恒等式，所提设计实现了高效的闭式解，其计算复杂度随感兴趣区域（ROI）大小而非子载波数量增长。本文从理论上给出了所设计ROI-MMF的测距均方误差（MSE），表明其相比传统匹配滤波（MF）和互逆滤波（RF）感知接收机具有更优越的性能。多种调制星座下的仿真表明，所提感知接收机达到了接近克拉美-罗界（CRB）的测距MSE，这充分证实了即使在使用非恒包络星座时，我们的设计仍能保持目标测距性能。最后，该框架通过我们的外场OFDM-ISAC测试平台得到了实验验证。

</details>


### [156] [Performance Analysis of Movable Antenna Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16907)
*Gayani Siriwardana,Peter J. Smith,Himal A. Suraweera,Rajitha Senanayake*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文对连续可移动天线阵列进行了严格的数学分析，提出了SNR上尾CDF的渐近精确近似以及相关阵列元素下SNR的LCR闭合表达式，验证了MA阵列相对于单流体天线和固定阵列的性能优势。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究多集中于单流体天线或固定天线阵列，缺乏对连续可移动天线阵列在多天线场景下信号接收性能的严格数学分析，特别是在相关信道模型下的SNR统计特性。

Method: 采用全空间相干相关模型和连续阵列定位，对线性天线阵列进行渐近精确的CDF上尾近似分析，推导了非可分离二维相关条件下SNR的LCR闭合表达式。

Result: LCR表达式准确且CDF上界紧；MA阵列性能优于单流体天线和固定阵列系统；减小阵元间距可进一步提升性能增益。

Conclusion: 连续可移动天线阵列在相关和非相关信道条件下均具有显著性能优势，其数学分析框架为未来MA系统设计提供了理论依据。

Original Abstract: This paper provides a thorough mathematical analysis of continuous movable antenna (MA) arrays. Focusing on the multiple antenna case, we consider a linear antenna array with multiple fixed antenna elements that moves along a line. We assume a full, spatially coherent correlation model and continuous positioning of the array. We provide asymptotically exact approximations to the upper tail of the cumulative distribution function (cdf) of the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), considering both correlated and uncorrelated antenna elements in the array. We also obtain a novel closed-form expression for the level crossing rate (LCR) of the SNR under correlated array elements, where a non-separable two-dimensional correlation is present. The analysis is validated through simulations, confirming both the accuracy of the LCR expressions and the tightness of the cdf bounds in the upper tail. Numerical results show that the proposed MA array outperforms single fluid antenna and fixed array systems, with reduced inter-element spacing providing further performance gains.

中文摘要翻译: 本文对连续可移动天线阵列进行了深入的数学分析。聚焦于多天线场景，我们考虑一个具有多个固定天线单元的线性天线阵列沿一条直线移动。我们采用全空间相干相关模型和阵列的连续定位。我们提供了信噪比累积分布函数上尾的渐近精确近似，同时考虑了阵列中相关与非相关的天线单元。我们还在相关阵列单元下获得了一个新颖的信噪比水平跨越率闭合表达式，其中存在非可分离的二维相关性。通过仿真验证了分析结果，确认了水平跨越率表达式的准确性以及累积分布函数上界在上尾的紧致性。数值结果表明，所提出的可移动天线阵列优于单流体天线和固定阵列系统，且减小单元间距可带来进一步的性能提升。

</details>


### [157] [Analysis of Fluid Antenna Systems with Continuous Positioning and Spatial Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16920)
*Gayani Siriwardana,Peter J. Smith,Himal A. Suraweera,Rajitha Senanayake*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对连续定位的流天线系统，提出了基于电平穿越率（LCR）的分析框架，在空间相关模型下为SNR、SIR、SINR等性能指标的累积分布函数（cdf）提供了渐近精确的近似与紧界，推导了多天线接收机布局下的新LCR结果，并揭示了高阈值尾概率随天线轨迹长度L线性增长、约一个波长的移动可将中断概率降低三个数量级等关键洞见。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在连续定位的流天线系统中，由于天线位置连续变化引入的空间相关性，精确的性能分布分析在数学上难以处理。现有方法难以刻画这类系统中SNR、SIR、SINR等关键指标的统计特性，亟需一种有效的分析框架来克服这一理论瓶颈。

Method: 提出基于电平穿越率（LCR）的分析框架，推导出优化指标S* = sup_{0≤l≤L} S(l)的累积分布函数（cdf）的渐近精确近似与紧界。分别针对单流天线（瑞利衰落、莱斯衰落）、固定天线+流天线组合、二元移动天线阵列等多种场景推导LCR结果，并处理了阵列元素相关性与位置相关性相互耦合的实际问题。

Result: (1) 高阈值尾概率与天线轨迹长度L呈线性关系；(2) 推导了抵消同道干扰所需的L值；(3) 约一个波长的移动可将中断概率降低三个数量级；(4) 蒙特卡洛仿真验证了所提方法在多种场景下的准确性。

Conclusion: 本文建立的LCR框架有效解决了连续定位流天线系统性能分析的难题，为实际系统设计提供了定量指导，尤其是在利用天线移动来对抗干扰和降低中断概率方面具有重要工程意义。

Original Abstract: We analyze multi-user fluid antenna systems with continuous positioning over a track of length L under a spatial correlation model, where exact performance distributions become analytically intractable. We develop a level-crossing-rate (LCR) framework that yields asymptotically exact approximations and tight bounds for the cumulative distribution function (cdf) of the optimized metric S* = sup_{0 <= l <= L}, where S(l) denotes the performance metric at antenna position l. For a single fluid antenna, we characterize the cdfs of signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), signal-to interference ratio (SIR) and signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) under Rayleigh fading and extend the approach to Ricean desired channels. We further treat two multi-antenna receiver layouts with maximum-ratio combining: (i) a fluid antenna with a fixed antenna and (ii) a two-element moving array, deriving new LCR results for the practically important case where array-element correlation and positional correlation are inherently coupled. The analysis provides actionable insights: high-threshold tail probabilities scale linearly with L, we derive the required L to neutralize a co-channel interferer, and we show that about one wavelength of movement can reduce outage by three orders of magnitude. Monte Carlo results validate the accuracy across the considered scenarios and regimes.

中文摘要翻译: 我们分析了在空间相关模型下、在长度为L的轨道上连续定位的多用户流天线系统，其中精确的性能分布在解析上难以处理。我们开发了一个电平穿越率（LCR）框架，该框架为优化指标S* = sup_{0≤l≤L} S(l)的累积分布函数（cdf）提供了渐近精确的近似与紧界，其中S(l)表示天线位置l处的性能指标。针对单流天线，我们刻画了瑞利衰落条件下信噪比（SNR）、信干比（SIR）和信干噪比（SINR）的cdf，并将该方法扩展到莱斯期望信道。我们进一步处理了采用最大比合并的两种多天线接收机布局：（i）固定天线与流天线的组合，以及（ii）二元移动天线阵列，推导了阵列元素相关性与位置相关性内在耦合这一实际重要情况下的新LCR结果。该分析提供了可操作的洞见：高阈值尾概率与L呈线性关系，我们推导了抵消同道干扰所需的L，并表明约一个波长的移动可将中断概率降低三个数量级。蒙特卡洛结果验证了所提方法在所考虑场景和参数范围内的准确性。

</details>


### [158] [Design and Practical Validation of a Novel Modulation Scheme for RIS Detection and Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17009)
*Aymen Khaleel,Adam Umra,Aydin Sezgin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用RIS被动波束赋形增益进行空中调制ID的方案，结合被动波束扫描，有效降低RIS检测与识别中的漏检和虚警概率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 可重构智能表面的检测与识别（RISs-ID）是基站自适应地为用户设备分配合适RIS的关键过程，现有方案在可靠性和检测覆盖方面存在不足，需要降低漏检和虚警概率。

Method: 利用RIS的被动波束赋形增益实现RIS ID的空中调制，并结合被动波束扫描扩展角度空间的检测覆盖范围。

Result: 通过计算机仿真和原型实验验证了所提调制方案在降低漏检和虚警概率方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的调制方案能有效提升RIS-ID的可靠性，降低漏检和虚警概率。

Original Abstract: The reconfigurable intelligent surfaces detection and identification (RISs-ID) is a critical process that enables a base station (BS) to adaptively assign the appropriate RIS to a given user equipment (UE). This work proposes a novel modulation scheme to enhance the reliability of RIS-ID by reducing the miss detection and false-alarm probabilities. Specifically, we leverage the RIS's passive beamforming gain to enable over-the-air modulation of the RIS ID, combined with passive beam sweeping to extend detection coverage in angular space. The proposed modulation scheme is validated through computer simulations and prototype experiments, demonstrating its effectiveness in reducing miss-detection and false-alarm probabilities.

中文摘要翻译: 可重构智能表面的检测与识别（RISs-ID）是一个关键过程，它使基站（BS）能够自适应地为给定的用户设备（UE）分配适当的RIS。本文提出了一种新颖的调制方案，通过降低漏检概率和虚警概率来增强RIS-ID的可靠性。具体而言，我们利用RIS的被动波束赋形增益实现RIS ID的空中调制，并结合被动波束扫描来扩展角度空间中的检测覆盖范围。通过计算机仿真和原型实验验证了所提调制方案的有效性，结果表明其在降低漏检和虚警概率方面具有显著效果。

</details>


### [159] [Connectionless Bluetooth LE Channel Sounding via PAwR for Scalable and Energy-Efficient Ranging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17094)
*Leon Schex,Markus Cremer,Uwe Dettmar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种无连接的信道探测（CS）架构，结合LE CS测试命令与周期性广播响应（PAwR），通过应用层协调与确定性信道序列，消除碰撞、降低开销，实现高并发、低功耗的BLE测距。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准CS依赖于LE ACL连接，存在初始化开销大、并发伙伴受限、测距结果需通过连接传输等问题，限制了可扩展性和能效。

Method: ① 设计无连接CS架构，由中央协调器、网关、同步Tag/Anchor设备组成；② 通过PAwR分发点对点分配矩阵，设备根据索引推导角色、信道序列和响应时隙；③ 使用确定性信道序列避免并行CS过程中的同一步碰撞；④ 紧凑数据平面通过省略可从共享配置恢复的字段，将序列化测距数据载荷减少约69%。

Result: ① 确定性信道管理消除了密集部署下的碰撞异常值；② 1秒更新周期下，稳态有源充电减少40-48%，每次切换初始化开销降低约98%；③ 每周期伙伴切换下，24小时总充电量可降低88%；④ 经验时序模型预测每PAwR列车的容量上限为16,384个活跃设备。

Conclusion: 该无连接CS架构在保持高精度测距的同时，显著提升了可扩展性和能效，适合大规模、低功耗的BLE测距应用场景。

Original Abstract: Bluetooth Core Specification v6.0 introduces Channel Sounding (CS) as a standardized high-accuracy ranging primitive for Bluetooth Low Energy (BLE). However, standard CS usage remains tied to per-pair LE asynchronous connection logical transport (LE ACL) connections, which adds initiation overhead, limits concurrent partners, and transfers results over the connection itself. We present a connectionless CS architecture that combines the LE CS Test command with Periodic Advertising with Responses (PAwR). A Central Orchestrator, a Gateway, and synchronized Tag/Anchor devices coordinate measurement configurations and aggregate results at the application layer. Each device derives its role, channel sequence, and response slot assignment from its device index and a Peer-to-Peer Assignment Matrix distributed via PAwR. The deterministic channel sequence prevents same-step collisions across parallel CS procedures, while matrix updates reconfigure arbitrary device-to-device pairings within a PAwR subevent group. A compact data plane omits fields recoverable from the shared measurement configuration and reduces the serialized ranging-data payload by approximately 69%, enabling result reporting through PAwR response slots. A proof-of-concept evaluation on the Nordic nRF54L15 platform shows that deterministic channel management eliminates the collision-induced outliers observed under simulated dense-deployment channel overlaps. At a 1 s update cycle, the architecture reduces steady-state active charge by 40-48% relative to a fair connected baseline and cuts per-switch initiation overhead by approximately 98%. Under per-cycle partner switching, these effects combine to up to 88% lower total charge over a 24 h horizon. An empirical timing model projects a capacity upper bound of 16,384 active devices per PAwR train at four CS procedures per device per cycle, 37 channels, and a single antenna path.

中文摘要翻译: 蓝牙核心规范v6.0引入了信道探测（Channel Sounding, CS），作为蓝牙低功耗（BLE）的一种标准化高精度测距原语。然而，标准CS使用仍依赖于逐对的LE异步无连接逻辑传输（LE ACL）连接，这会增加初始化开销、限制并发伙伴数量，并且测距结果需通过连接本身传输。本文提出一种无连接的CS架构，将LE CS测试命令与周期性广播响应（PAwR）相结合。中央协调器、网关以及同步的Tag/Anchor设备在应用层协调测量配置并聚合结果。每个设备根据其设备索引和通过PAwR分发的点对点分配矩阵，推导其角色、信道序列和响应时隙分配。确定性信道序列能够防止并行CS过程中出现同一步碰撞，而矩阵更新则可在PAwR子事件组内重新配置任意设备间的配对。紧凑数据平面省略了可从共享测量配置中恢复的字段，将序列化的测距数据载荷减少了约69%，使得结果可通过PAwR响应时隙进行报告。基于Nordic nRF54L15平台的概念验证评估表明，确定性信道管理消除了在模拟密集部署信道重叠下观察到的碰撞导致的异常值。在1秒更新周期下，该架构相较于公平的有连接基线，将稳态有源充电降低了40-48%，并将每次切换的初始化开销降低了约98%。在每周期伙伴切换情况下，这些效果综合使得24小时内的总充电量最多降低了88%。一个经验时序模型预测，在每设备每周期执行4个CS过程、使用37个信道和单天线路径的条件下，每个PAwR列车的活跃设备容量上限为16,384个。

</details>


### [160] [A reversed solar illumination dependence of unintended emission from Starlink Direct-to-Cell satellites at 72-234 MHz with the EDA2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17150)
*Haofan Dong,Houtianfu Wang,Hanlin Cai,Ozgur B. Akan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文首次区分并分析了第二代星链直连手机（DTC）卫星与非DTC卫星在72-234 MHz频段的非故意电磁辐射（UEMR），发现DTC卫星辐射更强、存在极化异常，并识别出230.627 MHz附近的窄带过量辐射特征，推断其源自星上主动源而非太阳光电流效应。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 第二代星链直连手机（DTC）卫星搭载了用于直接蜂窝连接的有效载荷，但其在300 MHz以下频段的非故意电磁辐射（UEMR）尚未被单独表征。此前研究未区分DTC与普通星链卫星，因此需要专门分析DTC卫星的辐射特征。

Method: 利用EDA2望远镜在21个频率（72.685–234.375 MHz）上观测到的112,534次检测数据，通过McDowell星表将1,806颗星链卫星分为175颗DTC卫星和1,623颗仅Ku波段的v2-Mini对比卫星，进行流量密度、偏振特性和频率精细通道分析。

Result: （1）DTC卫星的修正流量密度是Ku-only对比卫星的1.45倍（Cliff's delta=+0.30, p=2.6e-11）；（2）230.469 MHz处XX极化检测率0.811 vs 基线0.481（p~1e-274），11/21频段存在显著极化异常；（3）DTC卫星在日食期间比光照时更亮（光照/日食流量比0.47），而Ku-only卫星相反（1.18），此反转在多个匹配条件下稳健；（4）230.469 MHz粗通道内的精细分析将过量辐射定位到~24 kHz宽的230.627 MHz附近单通道，该特征非均匀分布于v2-Mini星群。

Conclusion: DTC卫星的UEMR特征与星上主动辐射源一致，其有效占空比在较低平衡温度下更大，而非随太阳光电流单调变化；发现的窄带辐射特征（230.627 MHz）可用作后续机制区分测试，且该特征与已知时钟基频不重合、在EDA2分辨率下不可分辨、在星群中异质性表达。

Original Abstract: Second-generation Starlink Direct-to-Cell (DTC) satellites carry an additional payload for direct cellular phone connectivity whose unintended electromagnetic radiation (UEMR) at sub-300 MHz frequencies has not been individually characterised. We reanalyse 112,534 detections from 1,806 Starlink satellites observed with the Engineering Development Array version 2 (EDA2) at 21 frequencies between 72.685 and 234.375 MHz (Grigg et al. 2025), separating 175 DTC and 1,623 Ku-only v2-Mini comparison satellites via the McDowell General Catalogue (McDowell 2020). DTC satellites emit a range-corrected flux density 1.45x that of the Ku-only comparison (Cliff's delta = +0.30, p = 2.6e-11). At 230.469 MHz the XX detection fraction reaches 0.811 against a 0.481 baseline (p ~ 1e-274), and 11 of 21 frequency channels show Benjamini-Hochberg-significant polarisation anomalies. The DTC population is brighter in eclipse than in sunlight (illuminated/eclipsed flux density ratio 0.47) while the Ku-only comparison shows the opposite sense (1.18); the reversal persists across altitude, sub-satellite latitude, frequency, and launch-epoch matching. The reversal strongly disfavours UEMR mechanisms that scale monotonically with instantaneous solar photocurrent and favours an active on-board source whose effective duty cycle is larger at lower equilibrium temperature. Within the 230.469 MHz coarse channel, fine-channel inspection isolates the excess to a single ~24 kHz bin near 230.627 MHz, tail-driven and absent at five control channels. Three falsifiable mechanism-discrimination tests show this feature is not coincident with the LOFAR-resolved Bassa et al. (2024) clock fundamentals, is unresolved at the EDA2 24 kHz resolution, and is heterogeneously expressed across the v2-Mini fleet rather than driven by a few permanently bright units or by uniform thermal scaling.

中文摘要翻译: 第二代星链直连手机（DTC）卫星搭载了用于直接蜂窝连接的额外有效载荷，其在300 MHz以下频段的非故意电磁辐射（UEMR）此前尚未被单独表征。我们重新分析了使用第二代工程开发阵列（EDA2）在72.685至234.375 MHz之间21个频率上观测到的来自1,806颗星链卫星的112,534次检测数据（Grigg et al. 2025），通过McDowell通用星表（McDowell 2020）将175颗DTC卫星与1,623颗仅Ku波段的v2-Mini对比卫星区分开来。DTC卫星的距离修正流量密度是Ku-only对比卫星的1.45倍（Cliff's delta = +0.30，p = 2.6e-11）。在230.469 MHz处，XX极化检测率达到0.811，而基线为0.481（p ~ 1e-274），21个频率通道中有11个表现出Benjamini-Hochberg显著的极化异常。DTC星群在日食期间比光照期间更亮（光照/日食流量密度比为0.47），而Ku-only对比星群则表现出相反的规律（1.18）；该反转在经海拔高度、星下点纬度、频率和发射批次匹配后依然稳健。该反转强烈否定了随瞬时太阳光电流单调变化的UEMR机制，而支持一种有效占空比在较低平衡温度下更大的星上主动辐射源。在230.469 MHz粗通道内，精细通道检查将过量辐射定位到230.627 MHz附近单一的约24 kHz频段内，该特征呈拖尾驱动且五个对照通道均不存在。三个可证伪的机制区分测试表明：该特征与LOFAR分辨出的Bassa等人（2024）的时钟基频不重合，在EDA2的24 kHz分辨率下不可分辨，且在v2-Mini星群中呈异质性表达，而非由少数永久亮源或均匀热缩放驱动。

</details>


### [161] [FPGA-Based Experimental Analysis of Fixed-Point Precision Impact on SOP Estimation in Coherent Communications Receivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17521)
*Geraldo Gomes,Rafael Vieira,Hani Kbashi,Aleksandr Donodin,Shekhar Saxena,Stylianos Sygletos,Ian Phillips,Jaroslaw E. Prilepsky,Mikael Mazur,Sergei K. Turitsyn,Pedro Freire*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文实验评估了基于FPGA的定点精度MIMO均衡器中传感与通信之间的权衡关系。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究MIMO均衡器中定点精度对传感性能（噪声底限、角度误差）与通信性能（吞吐量/误码率）以及硬件复杂度之间的权衡关系。

Method: 采用FPGA实现固定点精度的MIMO均衡器，通过实验测量不同比特精度下的噪声底限、角度误差、通信性能及硬件复杂度。

Result: 在7比特精度时，噪声底限降低100倍，角度误差降低63%；但通信性能趋于饱和，而硬件复杂度持续上升。

Conclusion: 定点精度在7比特附近存在最优权衡点：传感性能显著提升，但继续提高精度会带来硬件复杂度增加而通信性能收益有限。

Original Abstract: We experimentally evaluated the sensing-communication trade-off from the fixed-point precision MIMO equalizer using FPGA. At 7-bit, noise floor drops 100x and angular error 63%, but the communication performance saturates while the hardware complexity rises.

中文摘要翻译: 我们通过FPGA实验评估了基于定点精度的MIMO均衡器中的传感-通信权衡。在7比特精度下，噪声底限降低了100倍，角度误差降低了63%，但通信性能趋于饱和，而硬件复杂度持续上升。

</details>


### [162] [A Framework of Near-Field Communication with Different Array Geometries: Analysis, Optimization, and General Channel Estimation Algorithms Based on Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17690)
*Kangda Zhi,Yi Song,Tianyu Yang,Tuo Wu,Tengjiao Wang,Songyan Xue,Fangzhou Wu,Giuseppe Caire*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对超大规模MIMO系统，研究了不同阵列几何结构下的近场通信框架，提出了基于去噪自编码器辅助的近似消息传递算法进行信道估计，并联合优化阵列几何与混合预编码以最大化用户和速率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有近场通信研究多集中于均匀平面阵列，对曲面阵列等不同几何结构下的近场空间非平稳信道特性缺乏系统性分析，同时缺乏不依赖特定码本且具有鲁棒性的信道估计方法。

Method: 1) 建立了均匀和模块化曲面阵列下的近场空间非平稳信道模型；2) 将信道估计建模为带学习正则项的压缩感知问题，提出去噪自编码器辅助的近似消息传递（AE-AMP）算法，并给出理论副本界；3) 基于估计信道，联合优化阵列几何与混合预编码以最大化下行用户和速率。

Result: AE-AMP算法相比传统和基于深度学习的基准方法，在估计近场空间非平稳信道时具有更好的鲁棒性和泛化性；使用模块化曲面阵列结合估计信道可有效提升数据速率。

Conclusion: 不同阵列曲率对近场区域扩展有显著影响，所提AE-AMP算法无需特定码本即可有效估计信道，联合优化阵列几何与预编码可提升系统性能。

Original Abstract: This work establishes a framework of near-field communication under different array geometries of extremely large-scale multiple-input multiple-output (XL-MIMO). We first formulate the near-field spatial non-stationary channel model which is characterized by the distance between the user and each antenna on uniform and modular curved arrays. By fixing the total number of antennas while varying the degree of curvature, we investigate a fair case where the horizontal arc length of the curved array is the same as the planar array. We explicitly unveil the non-trivial impact of array curvature on extending the near-field region for cell edges. Then, for arbitrary array geometries and arbitrary-field channels, we estimate the spatial-domain channel by tackling a compressed sensing problem with a learned regularizer. Without relying on specific codebooks, we propose a denoising autoencoder (AE)-aided approximated message passing (AMP) algorithm and provide the corresponding theoretical replica bound. Finally, based on the estimated channel, we propose an optimization algorithm to maximize the sum user rate for sub-connected XL-MIMO systems by jointly designing the array geometry and hybrid precoding in the downlink. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed AE-AMP algorithm can effectively estimate the spatial non-stationary near-field channels with robustness and generalities compared to several conventional and deep-learning-based benchmarks. The improvement of data rate by using modular curved arrays with the estimated channel is also validated.

中文摘要翻译: 本工作建立了超大规模多输入多输出（XL-MIMO）在不同阵列几何结构下的近场通信框架。我们首先构建了近场空间非平稳信道模型，该模型由用户与均匀及模块化曲面阵列上每个天线之间的距离表征。通过固定天线总数同时改变曲率程度，我们研究了一种公平情形，即曲面阵列的水平弧长与平面阵列相同。我们明确揭示了阵列曲率对扩大小区边缘近场区域的非平凡影响。然后，针对任意阵列几何和任意场信道，我们通过求解带学习正则项的压缩感知问题来估计空间域信道。不依赖特定码本，我们提出了一种去噪自编码器（AE）辅助的近似消息传递（AMP）算法，并提供了相应的理论副本界。最后，基于估计的信道，我们提出了一种优化算法，通过联合设计下行链路中的阵列几何和混合预编码，最大化子连接XL-MIMO系统的用户和速率。数值结果表明，与几种传统和基于深度学习的基准方法相比，所提出的AE-AMP算法能够有效估计空间非平稳近场信道，具有鲁棒性和泛化性。使用模块化曲面阵列结合估计信道带来的数据速率提升也得到了验证。

</details>


### [163] [Observation Modeling of Reference--Background Residuals in Single-Snapshot FDA-MIMO-GPR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17728)
*Yisu Yan,Jifeng Guo*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对单快拍FDA-MIMO-GPR成像中参考介质与真实背景介质不匹配的问题，建立了基于畸变Born近似的参考状态观测模型，定义了参考-背景介质残差，分析了其协方差结构及其对Tikhonov重建误差的影响，发现残差会引起显著的跨频率和跨通道相关伪影。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在基于畸变Born近似的GPR成像中，参考介质被用来表示部分已知的传播效应，但真实背景介质与所选参考介质之间往往存在差异，该差异会进入观测数据并传播到异常估计中。因此，需要系统地建模和分析这种残差对成像结果的影响。

Method: 建立了基于畸变Born近似的参考状态观测模型，定义了参考-背景介质残差；通过Cole-Cole色散映射、参考传播核和FDA频率-发射组织推导其响应；构建了观测域协方差，分析了非对角通道块结构；采用标准Tikhonov估计器展示残差响应如何传递到异常候选区域的重建误差和协方差。

Result: 数值结果表明，在参考状态不匹配的情况下，观测协方差呈现显著的跨频率和跨通道协方差结构；经过Tikhonov重建后，这些结构表现为低维、集中的伪异常误差；右侧相干性和通道间相关性主要源于多个收发通道联合观测同一残差场，而FDA空频编码决定了其在观测域和重建域中的组织方式。

Conclusion: 在单快拍FDA-MIMO-GPR的参考状态选择、背景抑制和通道协方差分析中，应明确地对参考-背景介质残差进行建模。

Original Abstract: Reference media are widely used in distorted-Born-approximation-based GPR imaging to represent partially known propagation effects. When the true host background differs from the chosen reference medium, the difference enters the observations and propagates into anomaly estimates. For single-snapshot FDA-MIMO-GPR, this paper establishes a reference-state observation model under the distorted Born approximation and defines that difference as the reference--background medium residual, namely, the effective residual between the reference medium and the physical background medium. Hereafter, this quantity is abbreviated as the reference--background residual. Its response is derived from the Cole--Cole dispersive mapping, the reference propagation kernels, and the FDA frequency--transmit organization. The paper then constructs its observation-domain covariance, analyzes the off-diagonal channel-block structure, and uses a standard Tikhonov estimator to show how the response transfers to reconstruction error and covariance over an anomaly candidate region. Numerical results show pronounced cross-frequency and cross-channel covariance under mismatched reference states. After Tikhonov reconstruction, these structures appear as low-dimensional, concentrated pseudo-anomaly errors. Right-hand-side coherence and inter-channel correlation arise mainly because multiple transmit--receive channels jointly observe the same residual field, while FDA space-frequency coding determines their organization in the observation and reconstruction domains. The reference--background residual should therefore be modeled explicitly in reference-state selection, background suppression, and channel-covariance analysis for single-snapshot FDA-MIMO-GPR.

中文摘要翻译: 在基于畸变Born近似的GPR成像中，参考介质被广泛用于表示部分已知的传播效应。当真实背景介质与所选参考介质不同时，该差异会进入观测数据并传播到异常估计中。针对单快拍FDA-MIMO-GPR，本文在畸变Born近似下建立了参考状态观测模型，并将该差异定义为参考-背景介质残差，即参考介质与物理背景介质之间的有效残差。此后，该量简称参考-背景残差。通过Cole-Cole色散映射、参考传播核和FDA频率-发射组织推导了其响应。然后，本文构建了其观测域协方差，分析了非对角通道块结构，并使用标准Tikhonov估计器展示了该响应如何传递到异常候选区域的重建误差和协方差。数值结果表明，在参考状态不匹配下，观测协方差呈现显著的跨频率和跨通道协方差结构。经过Tikhonov重建后，这些结构表现为低维、集中的伪异常误差。右侧相干性和通道间相关性主要源于多个收发通道联合观测同一残差场，而FDA空频编码决定了其在观测域和重建域中的组织方式。因此，在单快拍FDA-MIMO-GPR的参考状态选择、背景抑制和通道协方差分析中，应明确地对参考-背景介质残差进行建模。

</details>


### [164] [A Computationally Efficient Reciprocal Effective Roughness Model for Diffuse Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17988)
*Giacomo Melloni,Enrico M. Vitucci,Vittorio Degli Esposti,Samuel Berweger,Jack Chuang,Camillo Gentile,Nada Golmie*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种定向、互易的漫散射模型，在保持有效粗糙度（ER）模型物理一致性的前提下，将射线追踪的计算成本降低一个数量级，且在八种材料验证中精度无损甚至略有提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随着毫米波和亚太赫兹频段的应用，表面粗糙度与波长相当，漫散射可占接收功率的40%，而传统有效粗糙度（ER）模型在高指向性波瓣或参数迭代调优时计算成本过高，迫切需要一种既准确又高效的可扩展漫散射模型。

Method: 提出一种定向、互易的漫散射模型，保留有效粗糙度（ER）模型的物理结构，但通过改进模型设计实现计算复杂度的大幅降低。

Result: 在八种材料上验证，所提模型将计算成本降低一个数量级（约10倍），且精度没有损失，甚至略有提升。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了一种可扩展、物理意义明确的解决方案，适用于漫散射不可忽略的射线追踪场景。

Original Abstract: Ray-tracing (RT) has become central to site-specific electromagnetic propagation modeling in dynamic complex environments. Yet its computational burden grows sharply as high-fidelity digital twins of these environments scale to millions of facets whose material parameters must be continuously updated as the environment changes. The challenge is amplified at mmWave and sub-THz frequencies, where surface roughness becomes comparable to the wavelength and so diffuse scattering can account for up to 40% of the received power, making accurate yet tractable models essential. The popular Effective Roughness (ER) approach offers physical consistency but become increasingly costly when highly directive lobes are required or when parameters must be iteratively tuned. This communication introduces a directive, reciprocal diffuse scattering model that preserves the structure of the ER while enabling an order-of-magnitude reduction in computational cost. Validation across eight materials shows no loss in accuracy - and a slight improvement - demonstrating a scalable and physically meaningful solution for RT in scenarios where diffuse scattering is non-negligible.

中文摘要翻译: 射线追踪（RT）已成为动态复杂环境中特定地点的电磁传播建模的核心技术。然而，随着这些环境的高保真数字孪生体扩展到数百万个面片，且其材料参数必须随环境变化而持续更新，RT的计算负担急剧增加。在毫米波和亚太赫兹频段，表面粗糙度与波长相当，漫散射可占接收功率的40%，这一问题尤为突出，因此亟需既准确又易于处理的模型。流行的有效粗糙度（ER）方法具有物理一致性，但在需要高指向性波瓣或需要迭代调优参数时，其计算成本日益高昂。本文提出一种定向、互易的漫散射模型，该模型保留了ER的结构，同时实现了计算成本降低一个数量级。在八种材料上的验证表明，该模型精度无损甚至略有提升，为漫散射不可忽略的射线追踪场景提供了一种可扩展且具有物理意义的解决方案。

</details>


### [165] [Fast 5G Signal Acquisition by Using Non-Uniform Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18189)
*Alejandro Gonzalez Garrido,Carla Amatetti*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于确定性非均匀采样（多陪集架构）的快速信号捕获框架，将捕获问题建模为延迟-多普勒空间中的参数推断，而非传统的波形重建。在5G NR同步场景下，该方法相比均匀采样可将平均捕获时间降低2.8x至34.2x，同时量化了采样压缩带来的估计精度损失。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统采样理论基于波形重建视角，要求奈奎斯特率采样，导致接收机在同步阶段面临高采样率和高计算复杂度。本文观察到信号捕获本质上是延迟-多普勒空间中的参数推断问题，因此无需重建完整波形，只需保留检测与估计所需的统计量，从而大幅降低采样率和计算开销。

Method: (1) 将压缩域捕获建模为广义似然比检验(GLRT)问题；(2) 利用多陪集采样直接对保留样本进行降维相关器结构处理；(3) 提出离线穷举设计方法，通过联合优化峰值隔离度和延迟搜索区间内的均匀能量覆盖两个指标来选择陪集模式。

Result: 在5G NR同步场景下，使用PSS/SSS信号在最恶劣多普勒条件下评估：相比均匀采样，平均捕获时间降低2.8倍至34.2倍；同时给出了不同压缩比下延迟和多普勒均方根误差，量化了采样压缩带来的估计性能损失。

Conclusion: 实验结果展示了清晰的复杂度-性能权衡，证实了多陪集采样在面向快速同步的接收机中的潜力，为低功耗、低复杂度接收机设计提供了有效路径。

Original Abstract: This paper proposes a framework for fast signal acquisition based on deterministic non-uniform sampling, with emphasis on multi-coset architectures and receivers driven by known synchronization sequences, pilots, or preambles. Unlike conventional sampling theory, which is formulated from a waveform-reconstruction perspective, the proposed approach is derived from the observation that acquisition is fundamentally a parametric inference problem in delay-Doppler space. Accordingly, the objective is not to reconstruct the full Nyquist-rate signal, but to preserve the statistics required for detection and estimation. The paper formulates compressed-domain acquisition through a generalized likelihood ratio test and shows how multi-coset sampling leads to reduced correlator structures operating directly on the retained samples. An offline exhaustive design procedure is introduced to select the coset pattern for a given sampling ratio by minimizing a cost that jointly enforces peak isolation in the acquisition surface and uniform retained-energy coverage over the delay search interval. The framework is evaluated on 5G NR synchronization using the PSS/SSS signals under a worst-case Doppler scenario. Results show that substantial reductions in mean acquisition time can be achieved relative to uniform sampling, with measured gains ranging from 2.8x to 34.2x, depending on the selected compression ratio. The corresponding delay and Doppler root-mean-square errors quantify the estimation penalty introduced by aggressive sample reduction. These results demonstrate a clear complexity-performance trade-off and confirm the potential of multi-coset sampling for fast synchronization-oriented receivers.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种基于确定性非均匀采样的快速信号捕获框架，重点研究多陪集架构以及由已知同步序列、导频或前导码驱动的接收机。与从波形重建角度出发的传统采样理论不同，本文提出的方法源于一个基本观察：捕获本质上是在延迟-多普勒空间中的参数推断问题。因此，目标并非重建完整的奈奎斯特率信号，而是保留检测与估计所需的统计量。本文通过广义似然比检验对压缩域捕获进行建模，并展示了多陪集采样如何实现直接对保留样本进行操作的降维相关器结构。提出了一种离线穷举设计方法，通过最小化联合优化捕获曲面中峰值隔离度和延迟搜索区间内均匀保留能量覆盖的代价函数，为给定采样率选择陪集模式。该框架在5G NR同步场景下使用主同步信号/辅同步信号在最恶劣多普勒条件下进行评估。结果表明，相比均匀采样，平均捕获时间可实现显著降低，根据所选压缩比的不同，实测增益范围为2.8倍至34.2倍。相应的延迟和多普勒均方根误差量化了激进样本缩减带来的估计性能损失。这些结果展示了清晰的复杂度-性能权衡，并证实了多陪集采样在面向快速同步的接收机中的潜力。

</details>


### [166] [Mixture-of-Experts Diffusion Models for Adaptive Massive MIMO Channel Estimation via Variational Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18325)
*Zhuorui Jiang,Jun Fang,Boyu Ning,Hongbin Li,Ying-Chang Liang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合混合专家（MoE）扩散模型与变分贝叶斯推断的信道估计方法，通过多个先验专家模型自动适配不同信道环境，解决了单一生成先验在多变信道分布下适应性不足的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的基于轻量扩散模型的生成式信道估计方法仅依赖单一数据驱动先验，在实际场景中难以适应多变的信道分布，尤其是在不同传播环境的信道样本不均衡时性能下降明显。

Method: 提出MoE扩散模型框架，使用多个预训练扩散模型（每个针对特定类型传播信道训练）。构建概率图模型，将信道建模为以一定概率从候选生成先验中采样的潜变量，通过变分贝叶斯推断联合估计信道和专家指示变量，实现信道估计的自动模型适配。

Result: 在3GPP CDL信道上的仿真结果表明，所提方法相比使用单一先验（在所有信道类型聚合数据上训练）的标准扩散模型方法，在信道估计性能上有明显提升，尤其在来自不同传播环境的信道样本不均衡时优势更为显著。

Conclusion: 所提出的MoE扩散模型结合变分贝叶斯推断的方法能够有效提升信道估计在多变信道环境下的适应性和性能，为massive MIMO系统中的信道估计提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Channel estimation is essential to massive multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems. While recent generative model-based approaches using lightweight diffusion models (DMs) have achieved superior performance, they typically rely on a single data-driven prior, which limits their adaptability to varying channel distributions in real-world scenarios. To address this deficiency, we propose a mixture-of-experts (MoE) diffusion model (DM) framework combined with variational Bayesian inference. Specifically, our approach employs multiple pre-trained DMs, with each trained on a specific type of propagation channels. We then propose a probabilistic graphical model in which the channel is modeled as a latent variable drawn from one of these candidate generative priors with a certain probability. By integrating variational Bayesian inference with DM-based data priors, the underlying channel along with the expert indicator variable are jointly inferred, thus enabling automatic model adaptation for channel estimation. The effectiveness of our approach is evaluated on 3GPP CDL channels. Simulation results demonstrate that our proposed approach achieves a clear performance improvement over the standard DM-based method that employs a single prior trained on aggregated data from all channel types, particularly when the channel samples from different propagation environments are imbalanced.

中文摘要翻译: 信道估计对于大规模多输入多输出（massive MIMO）系统至关重要。尽管近期基于轻量扩散模型（DM）的生成式方法取得了优越性能，但它们通常依赖单一数据驱动先验，这限制了其在真实场景中对多变信道分布的适应能力。为解决这一缺陷，我们提出了一种结合变分贝叶斯推断的混合专家（MoE）扩散模型框架。具体而言，我们的方法使用多个预训练扩散模型，每个模型针对特定类型的传播信道进行训练。然后我们提出一个概率图模型，其中信道被建模为以一定概率从某个候选生成先验中采样的潜变量。通过将变分贝叶斯推断与基于DM的数据先验相结合，我们联合推断底层信道及专家指示变量，从而实现对信道估计的自动模型适配。我们在3GPP CDL信道上评估了所提方法的有效性。仿真结果表明，与使用在所有信道类型聚合数据上训练的单一先验的标准DM方法相比，我们的方法在信道估计性能上取得了明显提升，尤其是在来自不同传播环境的信道样本不均衡时优势更为显著。

</details>


### [167] [Multi-dimensional hierarchical dictionary search for large MIMO-OFDM systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18363)
*Nay Klaimi,Philippe Mary,Luc Le Magoarou*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于系统结构特征的低复杂度贪心稀疏恢复算法原子选择策略，在保证恢复性能的同时显著降低计算复杂度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大规模MIMO等无线通信系统维度快速增长，导致传统稀疏恢复算法的计算复杂度急剧上升，亟需低复杂度的实现方案。

Method: 利用无线通信系统固有的结构特征，在贪心稀疏恢复算法的"原子选择步骤"中提出低复杂度的实现策略，并给出理论证明。

Result: 使用真实信道数据的测试表明，所提方法相比经典稀疏恢复方法带来了显著的计算增益。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于结构特征的低复杂度原子选择策略能够有效降低贪心稀疏恢复算法的计算复杂度，适用于大规模无线通信系统。

Original Abstract: Sparse recovery algorithms are of utmost importance for estimation processes in wireless communications. However, communication systems such as massive multiple input multiple output (MIMO) systems are rapidly growing in dimension, which consequently increases the computational complexity of these algorithms. This work proposes a low-complexity strategy for the efficient implementation of the ''atom selection step'' in these greedy sparse recovery algorithms, based on the structural features of these systems. A theoretical justification is presented along with tests using realistic channel data, to demonstrate the computational gain induced by the proposed approach and compare it to the classical sparse recovery approach.

中文摘要翻译: 稀疏恢复算法在无线通信的估计过程中至关重要。然而，大规模多输入多输出（MIMO）等通信系统的维度正在快速增长，这相应地增加了这些算法的计算复杂度。本文基于此类系统的结构特征，提出了一种低复杂度的策略，用于高效实现贪心稀疏恢复算法中的"原子选择步骤"。本文给出了理论证明，并利用真实的信道数据进行了测试，以展示所提方法带来的计算增益，并与经典的稀疏恢复方法进行了对比。

</details>


### [168] [Baseband-Efficient WMMSE Precoding: From a Signal Weighting Cost Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18368)
*Shuai Gao,Fan Xu,Mian Li,Xinzhi Ning,Lei Qiu,Ye Yang,Qingjiang Shi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模MU-MIMO下行链路中预编码计算复杂度和信号加权开销的双重瓶颈，提出了一种稀疏预编码框架，通过设计公共支撑行稀疏和用户特定行稀疏两种架构，在保持近最优和速率的同时显著降低预编码计算复杂度和信号加权成本。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统预编码研究主要关注降低预编码矩阵设计的计算复杂度，却忽视了另一个关键瓶颈——对高速数据流反复应用预编码器所产生的巨大信号加权开销。本文旨在同时解决这两个挑战。

Method: 1) 提出两种稀疏预编码架构：公共支撑行稀疏架构和用户特定行稀疏架构；2) 严格证明最优预编码器存在于由信道矩阵决定的特定低维子空间中；3) 在WMMSE框架下开发交替优化算法，联合优化稀疏波束选择和低维预编码系数；4) 采用基于惩罚的MM方法处理组合波束选择问题，得到低复杂度的闭式解。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方案在实现近最优和速率性能的同时，显著降低了预编码计算复杂度和整体信号加权开销。

Conclusion: 本文提出的稀疏预编码框架能够有效同时解决大规模MU-MIMO下行链路中的预编码计算复杂度和信号加权开销双重瓶颈，为全数字架构下的高效预编码设计提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: For downlink transmission in massive multi-user multiple-input multiple-output (MU-MIMO) systems, conventional precoding research heavily focuses on reducing the computational complexity of precoding matrix design, while largely overlooking another critical bottleneck: the substantial signal weighting cost incurred by repeatedly applying the precoder to high-speed data streams. To address both challenges simultaneously, this paper proposes a novel sparse precoding framework tailored for fully-digital architectures. Within this framework, from the sum-rate maximization perspective, we design two sparse precoding architectures: a common-support row-sparse architecture and a user-specific row-sparse architecture, so as to reduce the number of multiplication operations required in baseband signal weighting without sacrificing system capacity. For the formulated mixed-integer non-linear programming (MINLP) problem, we rigorously prove, for the first time, that the optimal precoder under both sparse architectures strictly resides in a specific low-dimensional subspace determined by the channel matrices, thereby reducing the dimensionality of the optimization variables. Based on this insight, an alternating optimization algorithm is developed within the weighted minimum mean square error (WMMSE) framework to jointly optimize sparse beam selection and low-dimensional precoding coefficients. The combinatorial beam selection problem is handled using an efficient penalty-based majorize-minimization (MM) method, yielding a low-complexity closed-form solution. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed scheme achieves near-optimal sum-rate performance while substantially reducing both the precoding computation complexity and the overall signal weighting cost.

中文摘要翻译: 针对大规模多用户多输入多输出（MU-MIMO）系统的下行链路传输，传统预编码研究主要集中在降低预编码矩阵设计的计算复杂度上，而 largely 忽视了另一个关键瓶颈：对高速数据流反复应用预编码器所产生的巨大信号加权开销。为同时应对这两大挑战，本文提出了一种适用于全数字架构的新型稀疏预编码框架。在该框架下，从最大化系统和速率的角度出发，我们设计了两种稀疏预编码架构：公共支撑行稀疏架构和用户特定行稀疏架构，旨在减少基带信号加权所需的乘法运算次数，同时不牺牲系统容量。针对所表述的混合整数非线性规划（MINLP）问题，我们首次严格证明，在两种稀疏架构下，最优预编码器严格存在于由信道矩阵决定的特定低维子空间中，从而降低了优化变量的维度。基于这一发现，我们在加权最小均方误差（WMMSE）框架内开发了一种交替优化算法，以联合优化稀疏波束选择和低维预编码系数。组合波束选择问题采用一种高效的基于惩罚的majorize-minimization（MM）方法处理，得到了低复杂度的闭式解。仿真结果表明，所提方案在实现近最优和速率性能的同时，显著降低了预编码计算复杂度和整体信号加权开销。

</details>


### [169] [A Fast Robust Adaptive filter using Improved Data-Reuse Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18417)
*Yi Peng,Haiquan Zhao,Jinhui Hu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合改进数据重用和在线截断策略的鲁棒广义自适应滤波器（RTGA-IDROC），在EIV模型下同时处理输入和输出噪声，实现快速收敛、低复杂度和强鲁棒性，并在系统辨识和声学回声消除中验证了优越性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 复杂场景下的自适应滤波器需要在不同噪声条件下同时实现快速收敛、低复杂度和鲁棒性能，现有算法难以兼顾这些要求。

Method: 1) 结合总最小二乘（TLS）策略和鲁棒广义自适应（RGA）函数，提出RTGA变体，有效处理EIV模型下的输入噪声；2) 引入改进数据重用（IDR）方法加速早期迭代收敛而不牺牲稳态性能；3) 采用在线截断（OC）策略降低IDR带来的计算复杂度，并将OC阈值从复数域修改为适用于实数域算法；4) 进行局部稳定性分析并推导理论稳态均方差偏差（MSD）。

Result: 在系统辨识和声学回声消除（AEC）场景中的仿真实验验证了所提算法的优越性能。

Conclusion: 提出的RTGA-IDROC算法有效兼顾了快速收敛、低复杂度和强鲁棒性，特别适用于存在输入噪声的复杂环境。

Original Abstract: Adaptive filter in complex scenarios demands algorithms that integrate fast convergence, low complexity, and robust performance under diverse noise conditions. To address this challenge, we propose a online censoring robust total generalized adaptive filter using improved data-reused method (RTGA-IDROC) algorithm. The proposed RTGA variant possesses the advantages of both the total least squares (TLS) strategy and the robust generalized adaptive (RGA) function. This algorithm not only effectively handles input noise under the errors-in-variables (EIV) model but also achieves excellent performance across diverse noise environments. Furthermore, to meet the high demand for convergence speed in practical applications, an improved data reuse (IDR) method is introduced, enabling faster convergence in the early stages of iteration without compromising steady-state performance. The increased computational complexity brought by the IDR method is mitigated using the online censoring (OC) strategy. We also modify the OC threshold for real-valued algorithms, as the original threshold was defined for the complex domain. Beyond these algorithmic enhancements, a local stability analysis for the proposed algorithm is provided, and the theoretical steady-state mean-square deviation (MSD) is derived. Finally, simulation experiments in system identification and acoustic echo cancellation (AEC) scenarios validate the superior performance of the proposed algorithm.

中文摘要翻译: 复杂场景下的自适应滤波器要求算法能够在多样化的噪声条件下实现快速收敛、低复杂度和鲁棒性能。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了一种基于改进数据重用方法的在线截断鲁棒广义总自适应滤波器（RTGA-IDROC）算法。该RTGA变体兼具总最小二乘（TLS）策略和鲁棒广义自适应（RGA）函数的优势，不仅能在误差变量（EIV）模型下有效处理输入噪声，还能在多种噪声环境中实现优异性能。此外，为满足实际应用中对收敛速度的高要求，引入了改进数据重用（IDR）方法，在不牺牲稳态性能的前提下实现迭代早期阶段的更快收敛。IDR方法带来的计算复杂度增加通过在线截断（OC）策略得以缓解。我们还针对实数域算法修改了OC阈值，因为原始阈值是针对复数域定义的。除上述算法改进外，本文还提供了所提算法的局部稳定性分析，并推导了理论稳态均方差偏差（MSD）。最后，在系统辨识和声学回声消除（AEC）场景中的仿真实验验证了所提算法的优越性能。

</details>


### [170] [Movable Antenna-Enabled Integrated Sensing and Communication in Low-Altitude UAV Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18486)
*Bin Li,Pengcheng Rao,Xuedong Zhang,Xinyi Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了配备可移动天线阵列的多无人机辅助集成感知与通信系统，通过HDBSCAN聚类和软演员-评论家算法联合优化无人机轨迹、用户关联、天线位置和波束赋形，在保证感知性能的同时最大化总数据速率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无人机辅助ISAC系统多采用固定天线阵列，且对地面用户动态漫游和无人机三维部署的联合优化研究不足，难以应对用户移动导致的动态未知状态转移问题。

Method: 将原问题分解为两步求解：1) 利用HDBSCAN算法处理地空关联问题，周期性更新聚类并重新关联，用聚类热点引导无人机飞行方向；2) 开发软演员-评论家算法联合优化无人机轨迹、天线位置和波束赋形。

Result: 实验结果表明，配备可移动天线阵列的无人机在ISAC系统中优于传统固定天线阵列，所提优化策略能在保证感知性能的同时有效提升通信速率。

Conclusion: 可移动天线阵列结合两步优化分解策略能够有效提升多无人机辅助ISAC系统的通信性能，且HDBSCAN与软演员-评论家算法的组合可应对动态未知状态转移的挑战。

Original Abstract: This paper investigates a multiple unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-assisted integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) system equipped with movable antenna (MA) arrays. To align with practical scenarios, we simulate the dynamic roaming of ground users and the three-dimensional deployment of UAVs in the airspace. We aim to maximize the total data rate by jointly optimizing key operational variables, including UAV trajectories, user association, antenna positions, and beamforming. This formulated problem is subject to constraints on transmission power and the sensing signal-to-noise ratio. To address the challenge of dynamically unknown state transitions due to user mobility, the original problem is decomposed into two steps and solved using different algorithms. First, we utilize the hierarchical density-based spatial clustering of applications with noise (HDBSCAN) algorithm to address the ground-to-air association problem, periodically updating clusters and re-associating during training. The clustering hotspots are used to suggest flight directions for the UAVs. Second, we develop the soft actor-critic algorithm to solve the joint optimization problem of UAV trajectories, antenna positions, and beamforming. Experimental results demonstrate that UAVs equipped with MA arrays outperform those with traditional fixed antenna arrays in ISAC systems, and the proposed optimization strategy effectively enhances communication rates while ensuring sensing performance.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了一种配备可移动天线阵列的多无人机辅助集成感知与通信系统。为贴合实际场景，我们模拟了地面用户的动态漫游以及无人机在空域中的三维部署。目标是通过联合优化关键操作变量（包括无人机轨迹、用户关联、天线位置和波束赋形）来最大化总数据速率。该问题受到发射功率和感知信噪比的约束。为应对用户移动性导致的动态未知状态转移挑战，原始问题被分解为两步并使用不同算法求解。首先，我们利用层次密度噪声空间聚类算法处理地空关联问题，在训练过程中周期性更新聚类并重新关联。聚类热点被用于为无人机提供飞行方向建议。其次，我们开发了软演员-评论家算法来联合优化无人机轨迹、天线位置和波束赋形。实验结果表明，在ISAC系统中，配备可移动天线阵列的无人机优于传统固定天线阵列，且所提出的优化策略在保证感知性能的同时有效提升了通信速率。

</details>


### [171] [Sparse Channel Estimation for Pixel Antennas: Addressing the Pilot Rank Deficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18516)
*Yiting Chen,Yumeng Zhang,Hongyu Li*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于像素天线系统的信道估计方法MMP-GAMP，利用稀疏传播路径和GAMP算法，配合MMP初始化，克服导频序列秩亏问题，以较低导频开销实现高精度信道状态信息恢复。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 像素天线系统产生的可重构导频序列存在固有秩亏问题，导致难以有效获取所有辐射模式下的完整信道状态信息（CSI）。

Method: 利用像素天线模式不变的稀疏传播路径，将全信道估计建模为角度域的稀疏恢复问题，采用广义近似消息传递（GAMP）求解，并引入多径匹配追踪（MMP）算法进行鲁棒初始化以缓解导频序列的秩亏问题，形成MMP-GAMP方案。

Result: MMP-GAMP方案相比其他基线算法实现了更高的估计精度，同时所需导频开销更低。

Conclusion: MMP-GAMP有效解决了像素天线系统中导频序列秩亏导致的CSI获取难题，在低导频开销下实现了高精度信道估计。

Original Abstract: Composed of multiple interconnected pixels controlled by on/off RF switches, the pixel antenna can generate reconfigurable radiation patterns that can be further exploited to construct diverse pilot sequences for effective channel estimation. However, such pilot sequences inherently have rank deficiency, making it difficult to effectively and efficiently acquire the full channel state information (CSI) across all available radiation patterns. To tackle this difficulty, we consider a sparse environment with a limited number of propagation paths for a pixel antenna system, where a user equipped with a pixel antenna transmits only a limited number of pilots to recover the CSI under all radiation patterns. The proposed algorithm exploits the limited number of propagation paths that are invariant with the pixel antenna patterns, and then formulates the full channel estimation as a sparse recovery problem in the angular domain solved by Generalized Approximate Message Passing (GAMP). Moreover, to mitigate the rank deficiency of pilot sequences, we additionally incorporate a Multipath Matching Pursuit (MMP) algorithm for robust initialization. The overall proposed scheme, termed MMP-GAMP, achieves higher estimation accuracy than other algorithm baselines, while requiring lower pilot overhead.

中文摘要翻译: 像素天线由多个通过射频开关控制通断的互连像素单元组成，能够生成可重构的辐射方向图，进而用于构建多样化导频序列以实现有效的信道估计。然而，此类导频序列存在固有的秩亏问题，使得难以有效且高效地获取所有可用辐射模式下的完整信道状态信息（CSI）。为解决这一难题，本文考虑像素天线系统中存在有限传播路径的稀疏环境，其中配备像素天线的用户仅发送少量导频即可恢复所有辐射模式下的CSI。所提算法利用了与像素天线模式无关的有限传播路径，将全信道估计问题建模为角度域上的稀疏恢复问题，并通过广义近似消息传递（GAMP）求解。此外，为缓解导频序列的秩亏问题，我们额外引入多径匹配追踪（MMP）算法进行鲁棒初始化。所提出的整体方案称为MMP-GAMP，在导频开销更低的情况下，实现了比其他基线算法更高的估计精度。

</details>
